# Whole-Project Code & Theory Review (2026-11-23)

*Scope:* Two-pass whole-repository code and theory audit of `rheon` on `main` at code commit `c4e7c7d2` (schema 72, post-`v2.0.0`), with primary focus on the ~200 commits merged since `v2.0.0` (schema 67), plus complete coverage of pre-existing crates, Python modules, tests, CI workflows, scripts, and documentation. Items already tracked in [PLAN.md](docs/PLAN.md) or recorded as intentional decisions in `docs/records/` and [ENHANCEMENTS.md](docs/ENHANCEMENTS.md) are explicitly eliminated (Section 7).

*Two-pass methodology & environment:*
- **Pass 1 (Neon):** Twelve-domain read of `crates/rheon-core`, `crates/rheon-polars`, `crates/rheon-py`, `crates/rheon-cli`, `python/rheon` (excluding private `_gbdt` per task 470), `tests/`, `scripts/`, `.github/workflows/`, and `docs/`, producing 170 initial findings.
- **Pass 2 (Independent Second-Opinion & Adversarial Audit):** Eight-domain independent first-principles review of 100% of the repository **plus** line-by-line adversarial verification of all 170 Pass-1 findings and all 38 Section-6 eliminations against the Rust/Python source, `docs/PLAN.md`, `docs/records/`, IEEE-754 float64 scripts, and live executions on `rheon 2.0.0` / `polars 2.1.0` / `numpy 2.5.5` (no `cargo`/`rustc` was installed on the review host). Pass 2 removed **2** invalid Pass-1 entries (`CLI-1` false positive; `TEST-8` open task 550 duplicate), recovered **1** High-severity bug (`CLOCK-1`) that Pass 1 mistakenly dismissed in Section 6, corrected **broken fix proposals, false sub-claims, or severities on 68 Pass-1 items** (Section 1, applied inline in Sections 3–5), and uncovered **46 net-new confirmed findings** (`1` Critical, `22` High, `21` Medium, `2` Low) that Pass 1 missed.

## Executive Summary

Since `v2.0.0`, `rheon` has added four substantial new model families (`garch`, `glm` + `family.rs`, `expectile`, `hawkes`), five new `drift_kind` detectors (`cusum`, `adwin`, `ddm`, `eddm`, `kswin`), square-root Carlson Kalman filtering, HMM Cholesky factor form (`d >= 13`) and Kulhavý–Zarrop stabilized forgetting, clock-unit BOCPD hazards (task 547), Lasso split-sweep coordinate descent (`SPLIT_MIN_K = 40`, task 479), a custom 13–64 Cholesky kernel, `rp.stream.merge` (task 541), a unified checksummed state envelope and atomic pipeline checkpoints (task 542), clock-span forms with `HeldCap` state budgets (tasks 537 and 538), and universal zero-weight clock exclusion (task 539).

The core linear-algebra kernels (including AVX2 vs scalar bit-identity), exact Hawkes integration-by-parts Gram recursion, Hansen skewed-t/Student-t scores, Carlson square-root algebra, split-sweep coordinate descent, BNHLS/CKP realized covariance, `ewridge/unread.rs` Higham bound, envelope header cursor, N-way heap merge ordering, and KSWIN/Gnedenko–Korolyuk combinatorics are mathematically strong (Section 6). Across both passes, the deduplicated, verified inventory totals **214 confirmed net-new findings** (**12 Critical**, **81 High**, **86 Medium**, **35 Low**):

| Severity | Post-v2.0.0 Code & Theory | Pre-Existing Code & Theory | Tests, Oracles, CI & Docs | Total |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| **Critical** | 8 | 3 | 1 | **12** |
| **High** | 35 | 35 | 11 | **81** |
| **Medium** | 35 | 42 | 9 | **86** |
| **Low** | 22 | 13 | 0 | **35** |
| **Total** | **100** | **93** | **21** | **214** |

*(Provenance breakdown across the 214 verified items: **100** confirmed unchanged from Pass 1, **68** confirmed from Pass 1 with Pass-2 fix/claim/severity revisions, and **46** net-new items discovered in Pass 2.)*

### Highest-Risk Issues to Fix First

1. **Post-v2.0.0 hangs, silent stale/leaked predictions & wrong outputs (`Critical` & top `High`):**
   - `DRIFT-4` *(Pass 2)*: Infinite 100% CPU loop in `Kswin::ks_h` on a single `NaN` (`Kswin::sound()` accepts `NaN` in `ModelBank.load`, `Kswin::update` lacks `is_finite()`, and neither inner `== v` loop advances when `v = NaN`).
   - `EXP-1` & `EXP-5`: `Expectile` `Fit::refit` (`tau != 0.5`) returns stale `old_beta` on pass-1 failure (emitting expired-window predictions), and `Fit::loss`/`Fit::wls` overflow to `+inf` inside `INPUT_BOUND` (disabling line search and falsely declaring 1-pass convergence).
   - `GLM-1`, `GLM-5`, `GLM-6`, & `GLM-METRICS`: `ETA_CLIP` objective/gradient mismatch causes ascent and `!fell` returns `converged = true`; `Glm::learn` swaps `work.g` into `gram[j]` before checking `work.s` (freezing `beta`); out-of-support formula targets silently skip learning while inflating `n_eff`; and `emit_metrics` + `rp.eval` completely omit `ModelKind::Glm`.
   - `STRM-1`: Under `embargo`/`label_clock`, `w = 0` rows skip `check_clock` yet drain live `pending` and train live models (leaking future labels into earlier `w = 1` rows, mutating live state on trailing `w = 0` rows, suppressing `restart_after_step_back`, and diverging across numeric vs temporal clocks).
   - `CLOCK-1` *(Pass 2, recovered from Pass-1 Section 6)*: Monotonic `Float64` clock jump overflowing `f64` sets `Exact::removed = +inf` permanently, turning `Stamp::since` to `NaN` and `Stamp::cmp_span` to `Ordering::Equal` forever across all spans, clock windows, and `embargo`.
   - `GARCH-1..4` & `GARCH-12`: Trapezoid ascent acceptance, bound-optimum decrement doublings, non-monotone EGARCH `guard::cut`, `self.h` `NaN` poisoning, and `Gjr`/`Figarch` projection slack emitting finite **negative conditional variances** (`s_t < 0`).
   - `HAWKES-7` *(Pass 2)*: `EventWindow` anchors `w.since` at `form_old_block()` instead of the newer block's first event, collapsing `g0 -> 0.0` (`pred = NaN`) after quiet stretches in both `window_events` and `window_clock`.
   - `DIAG-11` *(Pass 2)*: Asymmetric `expectile` (`tau != 0.5`) and `glm` allow mean/Gaussian `Checks` (`calibration_wald ~ 578`, `cusum ~ -8.5`, `breusch_pagan`, `jarque_bera`) that false-alarm on ~100% of exact oracle data.
   - `REG-1`, `REG-2`, `SPEC-1`, `STATE-1`, `MERGE-1`: `>= 1075`-HL gap panics `frexp(0)` / breaks `RefitBuf` reload; ageing rows bypass `HeldCap` up to 512 GiB; missing `'\0'` validation panics Arrow C export; `kind_of` ignores `Seal::Absent` in `save_checkpoint`; `rp.stream.merge(...).head(n)` over-reads past the slice.
2. **Pre-existing silent numerical corruption (all live-reproduced on `rheon 2.0.0`):**
   - `AUDIT-1`: Unchecked `*e -= cut` in `Counts::merge` panics in debug or wraps to `u64::MAX` (`top_count = i64::MAX`, `equal_by_chance = 5e35`) in release.
   - `WIN-1`, `WIN-2`, `WIN-8`, `WIN-9`, `WIN-10`: `mass_between*` 100% cancellation at `>= 54` HL; backward & forward `ewm_quantile` null-anchor wipeout at `> 1074` HL; null-row subnormal drift driving `ewm_mean` outside `[min(x), max(x)]` (`33.0` on `[42, 50]`) before collapsing to `None`; `Float64` `elapsed` overflow saving unloadable `"damaged"` window states; and `Acc::held` emitting literal `±inf` into DataFrames.
   - `REG-18` & `REG-19` *(Pass 2)*: `quantile(standardize=True)` zeroes active binary/regime slopes (`0.0` vs `+20.0`), and `Constraint` KKT cancellation explodes `coef_sum` (`-3.63e121`).
   - `COV-3`, `CLUST-3`, `MARG-3`, `MATH-7`, `MATH-8`, `LASSO-3`, `PY-1` *(Pass 2 / expanded)*: Hard-window `EwCov`/`EwClass` whole-history `precision_scale` leak + `ew_class("diag")` `0/0` death; `Micro::unit() -> 0.0` admitting `1.4e6`-distant points; `Marginal` `n_kish = +inf` on noise; Rust `solve_subsets` `0 * NaN`; `lars_lasso_weighted` dropping priority features; `Lasso` warm-start `+inf` triggering `LASSO-1` live; and `eval.window_metrics` wrapping `+316.9 yr` `Duration("ms")` to `-267.7 yr`.
3. **CI / mutation / oracle blind spots (`CI-1`..`CI-4`, `TEST-2`, `TEST-10`):**
   - Eight heavy oracle grids (~9,900 `arch` + 60 `glum` fits) marked `extended` instead of `oracle_grid` run inside Intel SDE (`cpu-paths.yml`), all 9 `ci.yml` legs, `release.yml`, and `polars-canary.yml` while bypassing `oracles.toml` (`CI-1`); `mutants_report.py` ignores `hung` (`Timeout`) mutants so infinite-loop mutants pass CI green (`CI-3`); `vm_checks.py` benchmarks SSE2 instead of AVX2+FMA (`CI-4`); `garch` is 100% null in `release_probe.py`, `state_portability.rs`, and `zero_weight.rs` (`CI-2`, `TEST-2`); and `model_contract.rs` omits bounded-extreme recovery tests for 6 models and 4 post-2.0 modes (`TEST-10`).

---

## 1. Second-Opinion Audit of Pass 1 (Neon) — Verdicts, Removals & Fix Corrections

Every one of Pass 1's 170 findings and 38 Section-6 eliminations was independently audited against `rheon` (`c4e7c7d2`). **Do not implement Pass 1's original fixes without the corrections below** (all corrections have already been applied inline in Sections 3–5):

### 1.1 Invalid / Duplicate Pass-1 Items Removed (2) & False Elimination Recovered (1)
- **Removed — `CLI-1` (False Positive):** Pass 1 claimed `runner::check_writable` passes when `output`/`save_state`/`closed_groups` is an existing directory, proposing `fs::metadata(&dest).is_ok_and(|m| m.is_dir())`. In fact, `check_writable` (`runner.rs:87-92`) already calls `check_parent` (`runner.rs:58-64`), which **begins with `if path.is_dir()`** (landed in task 160 YB7 — identical in Rust `std`). The *actual* unguarded directory-like bug — a **non-existent** path ending in `/` (`-o /tmp/results/`), which bypasses `path.is_dir()` and fails `fs::rename` (`ENOTDIR`) after the full run — is added as **`CLI-5`**.
- **Removed from Findings — `TEST-8` (Direct Duplicate of Open Task 550):** [PLAN.md](docs/PLAN.md) task 550 (`line 2049`, open `- [ ]`) explicitly tracks auditing and adding non-degenerate goldens/fixtures for every non-GARCH model (*"the audit table for every other model is still to do"*). Pass 1 cited task 550 in Section 6 while still counting `TEST-8` in Section 1/4; its concrete `golden.rs` notes are folded into Section 7 under task 550.
- **Recovered from Pass-1 Section 6 — `CLOCK-1` (High, Post-v2.0.0):** Pass 1's Section 6 dismissed `Stamp::cmp_span` returning `Ordering::Equal` on `NaN`/overflow (`clock.rs:316`) as a benign defensive choice, missing `clock.rs:712` (`self.removed += raw - d`), which permanently poisons `self.removed = +inf` across a single capped `-1e308 -> +1e308` float step and breaks `Stamp::since`, all `*_span` cadences, clock windows, and `embargo` on every normal row thereafter.

### 1.2 Broken Fix Proposals & False Sub-Claims Caught in Pass-1 Items

| ID | Pass-1 Claim or Fix Flaw Caught in Pass 2 | Verified Replacement (Applied Inline Below) |
|---|---|---|
| `GARCH-3` | Claimed $\mu(\theta, z)$ is 1-homogeneous in $(\alpha, \gamma, \beta)$ so $O(1)$ `t = exp(BOUND - mean_log_mu(to))` works. **Algebraically false:** quadratic terms $-\beta\alpha(\|z\|-c) - \beta\gamma z$ leave `mean_log_mu(t * to) = +0.0417 > -1e-6` (**still explosive**) across 64 grid points in `egarch_set`. | Bisect $t \in [0, 1]$ along `[to[0], t*to[1], t*to[2], t*to[3]]` toward `[to[0], 0, 0, 0]` ($L(0) = -708.4 \ll -10^{-6}$). |
| `GARCH-9` | Proposed `let unseen = UNSEEN * e.f.abs()`, which collapses to `0.0` whenever continuous Gaussian/t log-likelihood crosses `e.f == 0.0`. | Use homogeneous `UNSEEN * (w_sum + e.f.abs())`. |
| `EXP-3` | Updating `key_ahead` (`1154`) alone misses `window_key(..., counting = false)` (`1141-1150`), which returns `counted` (stamped) or `0 + d_clock` (unstamped), leaving `predict == step(..., w=0)` broken. | Return `Stamp::from(counted as f64 + d_clock)` in **both** `key_ahead` and `!counting` in `window_key`. |
| `DRIFT-2` | Recomputing `(total, var)` only when `self.var - inc < 0.0` misses subtractive cancellation rounding **up** (`+1.209e24 > 0` on an all-zero window), still blinding ADWIN for `10,000+` rows. | Recompute from buckets whenever `inc >= 0.5 * self.var` (either sign) and clamp `.max(0.0)`. |
| `SPAN-1` | Second fix option (`ns_of_seconds(span)` inside `cmp_span`) cannot recover nanoseconds already lost in `Span::value` (`f64`) past 97 days. | Carry `span_ns: Option<i128>` from `Span`. |
| `HMM-3` | Changing `Shape::of` in-place **violates Hard Rule 5**: `Shape` is unserialized in `Saved`, so `Hmm::restore` would multiply `G_ii` by dimensional `Saved.scale`, squaring variances `[1e-20, 1e20] -> [1e-40, 1e40]`. | Pair `Shape::of` change with a `SCHEMA_VERSION` loader migration (or `#[serde(default)] shaped: bool` in `Saved`). |
| `KAL-1` | Unreachable in Polars, documented in `gap_moments.rs:232`, mutates `EwDiagWire` (Rule 5), **and directly contradicts Pass 1's own `REG-15` fix** (`floor.min(self.w_sum) == 0.0` when `w_sum == 0.0`). | Downgraded High → Low (`rheon-core` boundary note). |
| `BOCPD-1` | Under `Robust`, when *all* runs yield `lp == -inf` (`mode == -inf`), `lp - mode = NaN` and `(f64::NAN).clamp(0, 1) == NaN != 0.0`, bypassing `weights[i] == 0.0`. | Return `false` from `log_predictive` when `!mode.is_finite()` before forming `weights`. |
| `REG-1` | Early `if count == 0 { return; }` in `HeldMedian::run` leaves `self.d` stale from the previous call instead of resetting `self.d = self.run_base`. | Set `self.d = self.run_base; return;` on `count == 0`. |
| `REG-3` | Weight **scale-up** (`1e-6 -> 1.0`) explosion claim is **false** (`EwDiag` stores mean variances and `w_eff == w_new` when `w >= floor`, verified smooth live); parenthetical fix `.min(sc.n_eff())` fails under slow/`inf` half-life and row 0. | Keep only the real scale-down freeze (`1.0 -> 1e-6`) and fix via decaying `Q_t / W_t`. |
| `REG-5` | `s1 + age * (w * aged)` still overflows `w * aged` at `w = 1e100, d = 1e220` and underflows at `w = 1e-305, d = 1e-5`. | Store `fit_spread` as mean clock variance $V' = a_w V + a_w b_w d_{\text{aged}}^2$. |
| `REG-8` | `ti = (r_i * r_i).max(MIN_POSITIVE)` overflows `t_hi` on ordinary mixed-scale inputs (`c=[0.2, 10], sc=[1e-155, 1]`), dropping breakpoints and returning `[0, 0]` (`sum = 0 != 1`). | Use unified caller-units + signed-infinite-breakpoint + pivot-residual KKT solver (`REG-19`). |
| `REG-13` | Proposed adding `qj` to `Cross` (schema change), missing that `Acc.tm: TargetMoments` **already** tracks per-target `q` both live and in `AccSnap`. | Read `view.tm.n_kish(view.wj)` with zero schema change. |
| `CWIN-1` | Unguarded 2-step rescale `(self.0 * UP) * (lam * UP)` **breaks existing unit test `window.rs:2025-2033`** (`take(2^-1030) -> 0.0`) and still wipes `< 2^-1536`. | Guard 2nd rescale with `if lam >= f64::MIN_POSITIVE` (or 3 rescales + intentional test update). |
| `CWIN-2` | Missed 5 identical `(vaa * vbb).sqrt()` sites in `corrchange.rs`; fixing `window_moments.rs` alone leaves `crit = 0.0` vs `stat > 0` (**100% false alarms** at `1e80`). | Fix all 6 sites (`window_moments.rs:254` + `corrchange.rs:975, 1181, 1293, 1338, 1646`). |
| `MARG-1` | Single-$W_P$ fix fails when `y` has `None`s because `(cxx, cyx)` use $P_x$ while `(cyy, cxy)` use $P_y$ (live repro: `lag_corr_xx = -0.919` vs `lag_corr_yy = -251.85`). | Restrict all 4 lag stats to $P_y$ or track separate current-side moments for $P_x$ and $P_y$. |
| `STRM-1` | Cloning all models and replaying `pending` on *every* `w = 0` row is $O(M \cdot H)$ quadratic on `rp.stream.embargo` bursts, and missed `check_clock` + `restart_after_step_back` (`clock.rs:1504`). | Enforce `check_clock` + step-back resets on `w = 0` and advance a lazy transient scoring shadow in $O(M + H)$. |
| `MERGE-1` | Setting `want = None` when `picks.len() >= budget` in `merge.rs` causes **0-row `DataFrame` round-trips on 100% of aligned chunks** because stream `i` was already popped off `self.heap`. | Keep `want = Some(i)` in `merge.rs`; break on `seen >= n_rows` in `stream.py:1134` before refilling `want`. |
| `STATE-1` | Missed `legacy_kind` (`envelope.rs:194-204`), which ignores `schema_version` and accepts 28-byte truncated legacy headers. | Require `open(bytes).is_some() && Seal::Holds` in `save_checkpoint`. |
| `BANK-4` / `BANK-5` / `CLI-3` | `ArrowChunk` erases `Null` into `ArrowCol::F64`; `BANK-5` missed `*_refit_span` reserving `max_state_bytes`; `CLI-3` `!weight_sum.is_null()` fails on `w = 0` (`weight_sum == 0.0`). | Record `Null` cols on `ArrowChunk`; name `max_state_bytes` on span refits; replace `LastReasons` with `bank.last_row()`. |
| `WIN-1` / `WIN-2` / `WIN-4` / `WIN-7` | `WIN-1`: test oracle `mass_bf` (`windows.rs:4098`) shares the bug; `WIN-2`: **backward** `ewm_quantile` also anchors on nulls, and forward `add_newest`-style rescaling overflows `front` to `+inf`; `WIN-4`: missed `windows.rs:485`; `WIN-7`: `total == 0` validly has finite `anchor`. | Update `mass_bf`; anchor at non-null rows and rebuild forward `front` on `pop`; normalize `Mean`/`Var` mass by `h/LN_2`; check `!(total > 0 && anchor.is_nan())`. |
| `WIN-3` | **Part 2 (`finish()` at EOF) is a False Positive:** pinned by `task 160 PC5` (`windows.rs:4323, 5832`) and `WINDOWS.md:143`; changing `finish()` breaks `PC5` and fabricates tail look-ahead targets. | Downgraded High → Low; keep only Part 1 (`closed="right"` regular-grid doc note). |
| `PYO3-2` | Executing `XGETBV` (`0x0f 0x01 0xd0`) when `OSXSAVE == 0` **itself raises `#UD` (`SIGILL`)** on x86-64! Also `vm-checks.yml` actually uses `-C target-cpu=x86-64-v3`. | Branch past `XGETBV` unless `CPUID.1:ECX[27] == 1`; parse `target-cpu=x86-64-v2/v3/v4` in `build.rs`. |
| `PY-4` / `MATH-3` | `PY-4`: tuple claim is false (`@_checked` calls `_lists()`), and `window_size is None` prints `"keeps None rows"` on windowless specs; `MATH-3`: `ModelBank.gram()`/`from_row()` store `0.0` (not `NaN`), and `fit_intercept=False` (`gram.py:749-752`) was missed. | Both downgraded to Low with corrected conditions and `gram.py:749-752` added (Rust `solve_subsets` is `MATH-7`). |

### 1.3 Pass-1 Severity Reclassifications (11 Items)
- **Upgraded (1):** `REG-14` (`Medium -> High`: live `solve_subsets` fabricates `n = 102,010`, 100× SE understatement, and `NaN` fits).
- **Downgraded (10):** `KAL-1` (`High -> Low`), `WIN-3` (`High -> Low`), `MATH-3` (`High -> Low`), `HMM-2` (`High -> Medium`), `DRIFT-1` (`High -> Medium`, task 508 contract), `DRIFT-3` (`High -> Medium`, task 508 contract), `HMM-4` (`Medium -> Low`), `WIN-6` (`Medium -> Low`), `PY-4` (`Medium -> Low`), `PY-10` (`Medium -> Low`, `@unstable`).

## 3. Confirmed Findings — Post-v2.0.0 Merged Code & Theory (`v2.0.0..c4e7c7d2`)

### Critical (Post-v2.0.0)

#### `EXP-1` — `Expectile` `Fit::refit` (`tau != 0.5`) returns stale `old_beta` on pass-1 failure, emitting expired-window predictions
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/expectile.rs:783-834` (`Fit::refit`), `1183-1217` (`Expectile::refit_window`), `1301-1337` (`Expectile::run_refit`).
- **Mechanism & counterexamples:**
  When `tau != 0.5` and `warm = Some(old_beta)` (always true once target `j` has fitted once), `Fit::refit` sets `best = (f_old, old_beta)` before pass 1 solves anything on the current rows. If `self.wls(...)` fails on **pass 1** (`counted < enough` or singular Gram), line 829 returns `Some(Refitted { beta: best.1 /* old_beta */, passes: 1, converged: false })` instead of `None`.
  1. **Empty clock window (`window_size`):** 10 rows arrive in `[0, 4]` (`fitted[j] = true`), then a gap arrives at `t = 14` with `window_clock = 5.0`. `trim(key)` empties the window (`fit.n() == 0`). For `tau == 0.5`, line 799 returns `None` (`fitted[j] = false`, `pred = NaN`). For `tau != 0.5`, pass 1 fails (`counted = 0 < enough`), line 829 returns `Some(old_beta)` → `fitted[j]` stays `true`, `solve_failures += 1`, and under default `min_weight = 0.0` both `step` and `predict` emit **stale predictions from expired rows**.
  2. **Underdetermined / missing-target window:** With `window_rows = 10, min_weight = 1.0, ridge = 0.0, p = 3`, after 8 rows with `y[j] = None` leave 2 rows (`weight = 2.0 >= 1.0`, `< 3`), `tau != 0.5` keeps `fitted[j] = true` and emits stale dropped-row predictions, while `tau == 0.5` correctly sets `fitted[j] = false`.
  3. **First-rows `run_refit` (`expectile.rs:1301-1337`):** If pass 1 fails while `warm = Some(...)` (e.g., high-level collinear feature where one-pass solved via `centred_solve` but `Fit::wls` failed; see `EXP-2`), `fit.refit` returns `Some` with `converged: false` instead of `None`, leaving `r.failed = false` and rebuilding `G, c, S` around the un-refit warm start.
- **Fix:** Short-circuit `if self.n() == 0 { return None; }` at the top of `Fit::refit`, and when `!ok` on `pass == 1` return `None` instead of `Some(Refitted { beta: best.1, ... })`.

#### `GLM-1` — `Glm` `refit_fit` objective–gradient mismatch at `ETA_CLIP`, spurious ascent in trapezoid line search, and `!fell` reporting `converged = true`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/glm.rs:706-736, 806-857`; `crates/rheon-core/src/family.rs:272-329`.
- **Mechanism & counterexamples (Python-verified against exact transcription of `refit_fit`):**
  1. **Gradient–objective mismatch at `|eta| > ETA_CLIP (30.0)`:** `objective` calls `fam.mean(eta)`, clamping `eta` to `[-30, 30]` (zero derivative beyond `±30`), whereas `refit_fit` (line 809) and `score_along` (line 732) call `fam.working(eta, y)`, which returns the unclipped score `w * (z - eta) != 0`. Once step halving enters the trapezoid branch `t * slope * (1 - 0.5 * t) <= unseen`, line 844 reads the unclipped `score_along` and **accepts steps that strictly increase `objective`**:
     - `Poisson(log)`, `ridge = 1.0`, 20 rows with valid `y = 1e14` (`start_eta = 32.236 > 30`): `passes = 100, converged = False`, `b0: 32.236 -> 32.333`, `f_end - f_start = +3.000` (**strict ascent on every pass**).
     - `y = 1e25`: `b0` explodes to `1,048,575.15`, then on pass 13 hits `!fell` and returns **`converged = True`** with `|grad| = 2.000e+26`.
  2. **Exhausted line search (`!fell`) falsely returns `converged = true` (`glm.rs:852-854`):** On `Gamma(inverse)`, `ridge = 1.0`, valid positive `X = [[1.0], [0.0]], y = [1e15, 1.0], w = [1.0, 1e16]`, pass 1 gives `d = [0.0452, -4.525e14]`. Even at `t = 2^-39`, `min(eta) = -822.2 < 0`, so `objective(trial) = +inf` for all 40 halvings → `!fell` → returns intercept-only `[0.9091, 0.0]` with **`converged = True`** and `solve_failures = 0`.
  3. **Trapezoid rule accepts ascent on non-quadratic links:** With cubic error $-\frac{t^3}{12}\phi'''(\xi)$, on `Gamma(log)` (`y = 1, eta0 = -0.5, t = 1.54`), `psi(t) - psi(0) = +0.2447` (**rose**), yet `slope + at_t = +0.0022 >= 0` (**accepted**). On `Tweedie(log)` with `p > 2`, $\partial^2 (\frac{1}{2} d) / \partial \eta^2 = [(p-1)y - (p-2)\mu]\mu^{1-p} < 0$ whenever $\mu > \frac{p-1}{p-2} y$ (non-convex objective).
- **Fix:** (1) Evaluate `objective` on unclipped `eta` (softplus for Binomial, safe log-space for Poisson) or zero the row's score/weight when `|eta| > ETA_CLIP` (and clamp `start_eta` to `±ETA_CLIP`); (2) on `!fell`, return `converged = true` only if `full <= 1e-10 * (1 + size)` or `t * slope <= unseen`, and cap initial `t` on `Inverse`/`Power` links so `eta > 0`; (3) gate the trapezoid rule on small `|t * d_eta| <= 1e-2` and `at_t <= slope`.

#### `GLM-METRICS` — `emit_metrics` and `rp.eval` completely omit `ModelKind::Glm`, producing wrong `hit_rate` and wrong metric columns for all 4 GLM families
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/stream.rs:3891-3910`; `python/rheon/polars/eval.py:250-256, 699-707` (cross-confirmed in core stream and Python package reviews).
- **Mechanism:**
  - In `stream.rs:3891-3905`, `binary_loss` matches only `ModelKind::Sgd` (`"logistic"`) and `ModelKind::Ftrl`, and `poisson` matches only `ModelKind::Sgd` (`"poisson"`). `ModelKind::Glm` falls through to `HitTest::Sign`.
  - In `eval.py:250-256, 699-707`, `_logistic()` ignores `type == "glm"` and `_no_hit()` matches only `sgd(loss="poisson")`.
  - Consequently: (a) `glm(family="binomial")` gets `HitTest::Sign` (`pred > 0 && y > 0`), scoring every `y = 0` row as a miss since `pred in (0, 1)`, and `rp.eval.metrics(out, spec)` returns regression metrics (`r2`, `hit_rate`) instead of `log_loss`, `brier_skill`, and `accuracy`; (b) `glm(family="poisson" | "gamma" | "tweedie")` emits a degenerate `hit_rate = 1.0` instead of `None` (`HitTest::Undefined`), repeating the exact defect task 195 fixed for `sgd(loss="poisson")`.
- **Fix:** Match `ModelKind::Glm { family, .. }` in `stream.rs:3891-3905` (`"binomial"` → `HitTest::Threshold`; `"poisson" | "gamma" | "tweedie"` → `HitTest::Undefined`) and update `_logistic` / `_no_hit` in `eval.py` identically. Add `emit_metrics=True` and `rp.eval` tests for all four GLM families.
- **Pass-2 fix note:** In both Rust (`ModelKind::Glm { family, .. }`) and raw Python spec dicts, `family` is optional and defaults to `"poisson"` when omitted (`family.as_deref().unwrap_or("poisson")` / `model.get("family") or "poisson"`); unwrap `None` to `"poisson"` so default `glm()` maps to `HitTest::Undefined` rather than falling through to `HitTest::Sign`.

#### `STRM-1` — Zero-weight (`w = 0`) rows under `embargo` / `label_clock` bypass future-target anti-leakage (Hard Rule 2), mutate live state (Hard Rule 9), and diverge across numeric vs temporal clocks
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/stream/label_delay.rs:415-439, 805-820`; `crates/rheon-polars/src/stream.rs:2428-2434`; `crates/rheon-polars/src/stream/weightless.rs:38-91`; `crates/rheon-core/src/clock.rs:979-1069, 1496-1514`.
- **Distinction from intentional design:** [ZERO-WEIGHT-AUDIT.md](docs/records/ZERO-WEIGHT-AUDIT.md) deliberately scores a monotonic mid-stream `w = 0` row from labels already mature at its clock. However, executing that release directly on live `self.persisted.pending` while skipping `check_clock` creates **four concrete defects** (three from Pass 1, one additional step-back leak caught in Pass 2):
  1. **Future-target leakage (Hard Rule 2 violation):** Under `embargo = 10.0` (or `label_clock = "lt"`), Row 0 `(t = 0, y = 100, w = 1)` enters `pending`. Row 1 `(t = 100, y = 0, w = 0)` skips `check_clock` (`stream.rs:2432`), computes `place = Stamp::Raw(100, 0)`, pops Row 0 from live `pending`, and permanently trains the live model on `y = 100` (and pops `held` in `check_labels`, bypassing `max_held_rows`). Row 2 `(t = 5, y = 50, w = 1)` compares `5.0 >= 0.0` (Row 1 never moved `clock_state`), passes `check_clock`, and is scored at `t = 5` by a model that already learned Row 0's future target.
  2. **Hard Rule 9 & Hard Rule 3 violation on strictly monotonic clocks:** Appending `(t = 15, w = 0)` after `(t = 0, y = 10, w = 1)` under `embargo = 10` drains `pending` and trains live state, permanently changing `save()`, `predict()`, `coef()`, `gram()`, `summary()`, and (under `drift_action = "reset"`) firing `drift` and resetting live models on the `w = 0` row.
  3. **Numeric vs temporal parity break on `session_gap = "reset"` + `embargo`:** `weightless.rs:58-65` calls `clock_state.clone().advance_stamped(..., true)` (`scoring = false`, `clock.rs:985`), resetting `Exact::default()` so temporal `elapsed_stamp` becomes `Stamp::Ns(0)` (releasing nothing) while numeric `elapsed_stamp` becomes `Stamp::Raw(c, 0.0)` (releasing pre-reset rows that a session reset discards anyway).
  4. **`restart_after_step_back` suppressed on `w = 0` rows (Pass-2 catch):** `weightless_plan` calls `advance_scoring` (`scoring = true`), which suppresses `adv.reset` at `clock.rs:1504` (`let reset = !scoring && ...`), so a `w = 0` row after a day-boundary step-back gets `Weightless::AsIs` instead of `Weightless::Fresh`, leaking Day 1's fit into Day 2's `w = 0` prediction.
- **Verified fix (Pass-2 corrected to avoid $O(M \cdot H)$ replay):** (1) Enforce clock monotonicity (`check_steps` / `check_clock`) across **all** rows in stream order (including `w = 0`) while keeping *decay* (`d_clock`, `prev_clock` for `w > 0`) anchored at the last `w > 0` row, and honor `restart_after_step_back` in `weightless_plan`; (2) never mutate `self.persisted.pending`, `held` in `check_labels`, or live `Instance`s on `w = 0` rows — instead, maintain a lazy transient scoring shadow cloned **once** after a `w > 0` row and advanced incrementally across consecutive `w = 0` rows in $O(M + H)$ total work (avoiding $O(M \cdot H)$ quadratic cloning on `rp.stream.embargo` bursts), discarded on the next `w > 0` row or chunk end; (3) on `Weightless::Fresh`, release nothing from pre-reset `pending` on either clock kind.

#### `REG-1` — Long clock gap (`>= 1075` HL, `lam == 0.0`) while a refit buffer is open panics in debug (`frexp(0)`), corrupts the median in release, and permanently breaks `save_state` → `load_state`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/held_median.rs:65-77, 397-399`; `crates/rheon-core/src/huber_refit.rs:330-336, 559, 614, 619-623, 677, 690, 696-697`.
- **Mechanism & live reproductions (`rheon 2.0.0`):**
  `Decay::Halflife(h).factor(d_clock)` underflows to exact `0.0` whenever `d_clock >= 1075 * h` (e.g., a 1-day gap `86,400 s` at `half_life = 60 s` is `1440` HL). While any refit buffer (`huber`, `quantile`, `sgd(loss="huber")`, count or span) is open:
  1. **`HeldMedian::run(0.0, count)` on ageing rows (`y[j] = None` in Polars; also `w = 0` in direct `rheon-core`):** Calls `Split::pow(0.0, count)` → `frexp(0.0)`. Debug panics at `debug_assert!(x > 0.0 && x.is_finite())`; release (`rheon 2.0.0`, live-reproduced) returns `(0.5, -1086)` = `2^-1087` instead of `0.0`, leaving pre-gap weights intact so `huber_scale` stays stuck at `148.26` across a `10,000`-HL gap.
  2. **Permanent save/load failure (every single-target Polars stream, live-reproduced):** `learn_scored` and `age` store `lam = 0.0` / `lam_s = 0.0` into `self.rows` and `self.ages`, while `RefitBuf::fits` (`huber_refit.rs:677`) and `RefitBufFile::from` (`huber_refit.rs:330`) require `f > 0.0 && f <= 1.0`, failing `load_bytes` with `"it is damaged"`.
- **Verified fix (Pass-2 corrected):** In `HeldMedian::run`, if `count == 0`, set `self.d = self.run_base; return;` (do **not** bare-`return` as Pass 1 suggested, which leaves `self.d` stale from a prior `run`!); if `!(lam > 0.0)`, call `self.decay(0.0); return;`. In `RefitBuf::fits` and `RefitBufFile::from`, allow `f >= 0.0 && f <= 1.0`.

#### `REG-2` — Unbounded memory bypass of `max_state_bytes` (`HeldCap`) on ageing rows in `huber_refit_span` / `quantile_refit_span` (+ 32-bit overflow)
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/sgd.rs:2832, 2855-2859`; `crates/rheon-core/src/robust.rs:1716-1729`; `crates/rheon-core/src/huber_refit.rs:600-629, 694`.
- **Mechanism:** Under `huber_refit_span` / `quantile_refit_span`, `refit_cap()` returns `OPEN = u32::MAX` (`4,294,967,295`). Neither `Sgd::step_into` (which gates `HeldCap` on `t.is_some()`) nor `Robust::age_refit` calls `HeldCap::admit` or `note` on ageing rows (`y[j] = None` with `w > 0` in Polars; also `w = 0` in direct `rheon-core`). Inside `RefitBuf::age`, the abandonment threshold is `REFIT_RUNS_PER_ROW as usize * OPEN as usize = 17,179,869,180` runs × 32 B = **512.00 GiB (`549.76 GB`)** on 64-bit (and overflows `usize` on 32-bit). On an irregular clock where successive `lam` factors differ by `>= 1` ULP, sparse targets append 32 B to `ages` on every null-target row without firing `take_state_warning()` or `state_over_cap()`.
- **Verified fix (Pass-2 corrected):** Precompute whether `age` will append a non-coalescing run (+32 B) *before* mutating any online model state on the row (see `SPAN-3`), include it in `HeldCap::admit` / `note` when `refit_clock.is_some()`, use `saturating_mul` in `age`/`fits`, and do not silently set `abandoned = true` behind `HeldCap` when `cap == OPEN`.

#### `SPEC-1` — Missing NUL-byte (`'\0'`) validation on `features`, `feature_sets`, `deco.blocks`, and `ew_class.classes` panics Arrow C export after learning
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/spec.rs:3039, 3373-3385`; `spec/models.rs:677-699`; `spec/checks.rs:291-344`; `stream/model_cfgs.rs:782-790` (cross-confirmed in bank and spec reviews; **all 4 cases reproduced live**).
- **Mechanism & live reproductions:** Review P11 added `'\0'` checks only to `self.name` and `t.name`, leaving four user-supplied strings interpolated directly into Arrow struct field names unchecked:
  1. `ewridge(features=["x\0a"], targets=["y"])` (and `ew_cov`, `emit_feature_health=True`) → `PanicException` (`NulError(1, ...)`) at `polars-arrow/src/ffi/schema.rs:119`.
  2. `ewridge(..., feature_sets={"s\0a": ["x"], "s2": ["x"]})` (and `huber`) → `PanicException` on `"pred_y__s\0a"` even on a 100% clean frame, **after mutating the bank**.
  3. `deco(..., blocks={"b\0a": ["x1", "x2"]})` → `PanicException` on `"u_b\0a"`.
  4. `ew_class(..., classes=["a\0b", "c"])` → `PanicException` on `"p_a\0b"`.
  Column-name fields (`clock`, `group`, `session`, `weight`, `label_clock`, `exog_tvtp`, `hazard_col`) also omit `'\0'` checks.
- **Fix:** In `Spec::check()` alongside `duplicate_field(self)`, reject any output field in `output_index(self)` (plus `self.name` and all column-name fields) containing `'\0'`.

#### `DRIFT-4` — Infinite 100% CPU loop in `Kswin` (`ks_h`) on a single `NaN` via unchecked `Kswin::sound()` (`ModelBank.load`) or `Kswin::update`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/drift/kswin.rs:145-159` (`ks_h`), `191-197` (`Kswin::sound`), `227-246` (`Kswin::update`); `drift/detector.rs:87-94`.
- **Mechanism & Python verification:**
  1. `Kswin::sound()` checks only structural sizes and `alpha`, **omitting `self.window.iter().all(|v| v.is_finite())`**, so a damaged/crafted state file with `NaN` in `Kswin.window` loads cleanly into `ModelBank`. Direct `Kswin::update(x)` (`227`) also lacks `!x.is_finite()`, pushing `NaN` into `self.window`.
  2. Once `window.len() == window_size`, `sc.old` and `sc.new` are sorted via `f64::total_cmp` (`-NaN` at index `0`, `+NaN` at index `r - 1`) and passed to `ks_h` (`148-157`):
     `let v = if a[i] <= b[j] { a[i] } else { b[j] }; while i < r && a[i] == v { i += 1; } while j < r && b[j] == v { j += 1; }`
  3. Whenever `b[j]` is `NaN` (immediately at `j = 0` for `-NaN`, or at `j = r - 1` whenever `max(old) > max_finite(new)` — >50% probability on stationary data — for `+NaN`), `a[i] <= b[j]` is `false`, setting `v = NaN`. Both `a[i] == NaN` and `b[j] == NaN` are `false`, so neither `i` nor `j` advances and **`ks_h` hangs at 100% CPU forever**.
- **Fix:** Add `&& self.window.iter().all(|v| v.is_finite())` to `Kswin::sound()`, guard `if !x.is_finite() { return false; }` in `Kswin::update`, and use `total_cmp` inside `ks_h`.

---

### High (Post-v2.0.0)

#### `GARCH-1` — Trapezoid line-search branch triggers on large overshooting steps and accepts likelihood decreases
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/garch/optim.rs:228-242`.
- **Derivation & counterexamples (Python-verified):** The `else` branch checks `slope - 0.5 * quad(&m, &delta) > unseen` is false — which happens not only for tiny steps, but whenever a **large overshooting step** has `0.5 * quad(&m, &delta) > slope` (negative quadratic predicted gain!). In that `else` branch `ec.f` is never compared to `e.f`; only `slope + dot(&ec.g, &delta) >= 0.0` is tested.
  - 1D Gaussian variance (`n = 500, v = 1, s: 0.2 -> 100.0`): `f` drops by **`-306.15` nats**, `pred_gain = -3.06e7 <= unseen`, `trapezoid = +4.99e5 >= 0` → **accepted**.
  - Actual GARCH-t log-likelihood (100 rows `t(3)` + 1 outlier, exact `optim.rs` loop from `fit.rs` grid): at `it = 8`, full Newton step `s = 1.0`, `f` drops from `-167.9533` to `-190.1391` (**`-22.19` nats**) and is **accepted**.
- **Fix:** Require `slope > 0.0` in both branches, and enter the trapezoid branch only when `slope <= 2.0 * unseen && 0.5 * quad(&m, &delta) <= 2.0 * unseen`.

#### `GARCH-2` — Unconstrained Newton decrement flags exact constrained optima on shape bounds (`nu = 300`, `2.05`, `lam = ±0.99`) as unsound, causing spurious start doublings
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/garch/optim.rs:211, 286-296`; `crates/rheon-core/src/garch/fit.rs:173, 433, 619`.
- **Derivation & counterexample (Python-verified):** `fit.rs:619` intentionally checks `on_bound` only on volatility parameters `theta[..k]` (so Gaussian tails at `nu = 300` are valid optima), yet `optim.rs:292` computes the **unconstrained** Newton decrement $\frac{1}{2} g^\top M^{-1} g$ over all `k + m` coordinates including blocked shape gradient `g_nu > 0` (and `curvature()` probes `300 + h > NU_HI`, falling back to BHHH). As $\nu \to \infty$, $g_\nu \approx -(n/(4\nu^2))\hat\kappa$ and $H_{\nu\nu} \approx 1.5 n / \nu^4$, so $\nu^4$ cancels completely: $\frac{1}{2} g_\nu^2 / H_{\nu\nu} \approx n\hat\kappa^2 / 48 = O(1) \gg 10^{-6}$. Across 12 simulated 2,000-row Gaussian GARCH(1,1) series fitted with `dist="t"`, **8/12 (67%)** hit exact constrained optima at `nu = 300.0` (`‖g_vol‖ ~ 1e-12`) yet had unconstrained decrements from `3.08e-4` to `4.00` nats, triggering spurious start doublings `2,000 -> 4,000 -> 8,000` (withholding 6,000 extra rows).
- **Fix:** Compute `decrement` and the early-stop step test at `optim.rs:211` on the **free coordinates** not blocked by `project` (evaluating `curvature` on those free coordinates or with one-sided differences near bounds).

#### `GARCH-3` — EGARCH `guard::cut` fallback assumes monotonicity in `beta`, jumping `beta -> 0` and making the filter 9× more explosive
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/garch/guard.rs:81-116`.
- **Derivation & counterexamples (Python-verified):** $L(\theta) = \mathbb{E}[\ln |\mu(\theta, z)|]$ with $\mu(\theta, z) = |(1 - \beta)(\alpha(|z| - c) + \gamma z) + \beta|$ is **not** monotonic in $\beta$. When `!admits(from)` and `!admits(to)`:
  - `|z| ≈ 5`, `from = [0, 0.8, 0, 0.90]`, `to = [0, 0.8, 0, 0.92]`: `L(from) = +0.0943`, `L(to) = +0.0759` (improving!), yet `with_beta(0.0)` has `L = +0.6928`, so `cut()` **jumps `beta` to `0.0`**, making the Lyapunov exponent **9× more explosive**.
  - `|z| ≈ 3.375`, `from = [0, 0.8, 0, 0.30]`, `to = [0, 0.8, 0, 0.34]`: `beta = 0.9` is contracting (`L = -0.6982`), yet `cut()` jumps to `beta = 0.0` (`L = +0.3358`).
- **Verified fix (Pass-2 mathematical correction):** *(Pass 1's proposed $O(1)$ formula `t = exp(BOUND - mean_log_mu(to))` assumed 1-homogeneity in $(\alpha, \gamma, \beta)$, which is **false** due to the quadratic cross-terms $-\beta\alpha(|z|-c) - \beta\gamma z$: on `to = [0, 0.55, -0.55, -0.99]`, Pass 1's $t = 0.9075$ leaves `mean_log_mu = +0.0417 > -1e-6`, still explosive!)* Instead, bisect $t \in [0, 1]$ along the ray `[to[0], t * to[1], t * to[2], t * to[3]]` toward `[to[0], 0.0, 0.0, 0.0]` (at $t = 0$, $\mu \equiv 0 \implies L = \ln(\text{MIN\_POSITIVE}) = -708.4 \ll -10^{-6}$, unconditionally admissible for any positive bin weight).

#### `GARCH-4` — Usable extreme inputs (`|e| <= 1e100`) permanently poison `self.h` with `NaN`, freezing online learning forever
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/garch/dist.rs:95-121`; `crates/rheon-core/src/garch/series.rs:237-261`.
- **Counterexamples (Python-verified):**
  1. Small-scale series (`e ~ 1e-80, s ~ 1e-160`): `dl[0] = 1/s ~ 1e160` → `0.5 * dl[0]^2 = +inf` → `self.h[0] = +inf`; on the next row `(+inf) + a * (finite - inf) = NaN`, permanently poisoning `self.h`.
  2. Student-t / skewed-t with `|z| >= 1.34e154` (e.g. `e ~ 1e-60` series hit by valid `e = 1e96`, `z = 1e156`): `q = z * z = +inf` → `w = inf/inf - 1 = NaN`, `s_nu = NaN`, `s_lam = NaN`, folded straight into `self.h`.
  Once `self.h` holds `NaN`, `chol_solve` returns `None` forever, freezing `theta`.
- **Fix:** Avoid `z * z` / `m * m` overflow in `dist::scores` and `dist::log_density` when `|z| > 1e150` (`inv = n2 / m / m`), and verify all `df[i]*df[j]` / `sc[i]*sc[j]` terms are finite in `series.rs::learn` before mutating `self.h`.

#### `EXP-2` — `Fit::wls` lacks `centred_solve` fallback, and `centred_solve` suffers `E[X^2] - E[X]^2` cancellation and rejects mixed-scale collinear features
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/expectile.rs:632-706, 746-779, 1264-1279`.
- **Counterexamples (Python-verified):**
  1. `Fit::wls` completely omits task 538 part 3's `centred_solve` fallback: on `refit_clock_tests::flat` or a 1-feature constant `x = -1500.0` (`ridge = 1e-6`), `Fit::wls` returns `false`, so **windowed `expectile` emits `NaN` forever** and `run_refit` fails pass 1.
  2. In `centred_solve` (`g[i+1, t+1] - m[i] * m[t]`), uncentered subtraction loses precision on offset features (`lam = 0.99, ridge = 1e-6, tau = 0.5`): at `x = 1e6 + 1e-3 * N(0,1)`, `min(diag + pen) = -8.525e-4`, failing **124/200 rows**; at `x = 1e8 + 0.1 * N(0,1)`, failing **26/200 rows**.
  3. If feature 0 has variance `1e7` and feature 1 is constant (`0.0`), `a` has diagonals `[1e7 + 2e-6, 2e-6]`, failing `2e-6 < 1e-12 * 1e7` even with `ridge = 1e-6 > 0`.
- **Fix:** Center `z_i - m` directly from held rows in `Fit::wls`; use Welford centered covariance (or clamp negative diagonal rounding noise) in one-pass `Expectile`, and use diagonal preconditioning (or `s > 0.0` when `ridge > 0`).

#### `GLM-2` — `Glm` decay-mode objective mismatch: `refit_fit` applies undecayed `cfg.ridge` to decayed weights, and documented `F_t(beta)` is algebraically inaccurate
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/glm.rs:13-35, 706-716, 803-819`; `python/rheon/_spec_glm.py:240-243`; `docs/MODELS.md:485`.
- **Derivation & counterexample (Python-verified):**
  1. `refit_fit` minimizes $\sum v_i d(y_i, \mu_i)/2 + \frac{R}{2}\|\beta\|^2$ with **undecayed** `R = cfg.ridge` (including the intercept!) against decayed buffer weights `v_i = w_i * lam^{age_i}`. At `n = 200, half_life = 5.0, ridge = 1.0, true_beta = [1.5, 0.8]`, `Binomial(logit)` drops from online `[0.913, 1.204]` (decayed MLE `[0.576, 1.683]`) to **`refit_fit` `[0.448, 0.764]`** (>54% slope cut at row 200); `Gamma(log)` jumps ~13% in predicted mean.
  2. Telescoping $(G_s + R I)(\beta_s - \beta_{s-1}) = w_s W_s (z_s - \tilde x_s^\top \beta_{s-1})\tilde x_s$ gives $G_t\beta_t + R(\beta_t - (1-\lambda)\sum_{s<t}\lambda^{t-1-s}\beta_s) = \sum \lambda^{t-s} w_s W_s z_s \tilde x_s$ — a moving proximal objective, **not** `glm.rs:22`'s `F_t(beta)` (2-step linear check `y=[10,0], lam=0.5, R=1`: recursion `3.0`, doc formula `2.5` or `2.857`, `refit_fit` `2.0`).
- **Fix:** Scale `refit_fit`'s ridge by the tracked decay from the buffer's first row (with a small floor to prevent Binomial separation) or document full undecayed `R` explicitly, and correct `glm.rs:22`'s proximal-objective identity.
- **Pass-2 contract & steady-state note:** Undecayed `cfg.ridge` in `refit_fit` is currently pinned by `glm/tests.rs:230-261` (`the_refit_reads_the_decayed_weights`), whereas online `glm.rs:636` omits `-R * beta` from the RHS score and *does* converge to unpenalized `GLM(freq_weights = lam^age)` in steady state (`0.0029` vs `0.327` penalized gap at `lam = 0.95, R = 10`). Never decay `R` toward `0` in `glm.rs:636` (which would make `G_t + R_t I` singular across gaps); either document the undecayed refit penalty explicitly or change `refit_fit` intentionally alongside `glm/tests.rs:230`.

#### `EXP-3` — `Expectile` `predict` vs `step` parity and `embargo` broken on row-clock windows (`key_ahead` & `window_key`)
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/expectile.rs:1138-1158, 1386-1388, 1566-1574`.
- **Counterexamples (expanded in Pass 2):**
  1. Once `self.clock.on_rows == true`, `key_ahead(d_clock)` (`1154`) hardcodes `Stamp::from((self.clock.counted + 1) as f64)`, ignoring `d_clock`: `predict(x, 0.0)` advances by `1.0` (trimming row 1 where `step(..., 0.0, 0.0)` keeps it), and under `embargo = 5` on a row clock (`stream.rs:4062`), `predict_into(..., 5.0)` advances by `1.0` instead of `5.0`.
  2. **Pass-2 catch in `window_key(..., counting = false)` (`1141-1150`):** Updating `key_ahead` alone still leaves `predict(x, d) == step(x, y, d, 0.0)` broken! On `w = 0` rows (`counting = false`), stamped `Some((_, true))` returns `counted` (**missing `+ d_clock`**), while unstamped `None` (`stream/weightless.rs:73`) returns `self.clock.own + d_clock` where `self.clock.own` is still **`0.0`**!
- **Verified fix:** Return `Stamp::from(self.clock.counted as f64 + d_clock)` in **both** `key_ahead` and the `!counting` path of `window_key` whenever `self.clock.on_rows` (or `matches!(stamp, Some((_, true)))`) holds.

#### `EXP-4` — `Expectile` `n_eff` / `min_weight` / `withheld_reason` desync under clock window
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/expectile.rs:1342-1371, 1420-1431`; `crates/rheon-polars/src/stream.rs:4035, 4252-4260`.
- **Counterexample:** With `window_clock = 5.0, min_weight = 3.0` and 5 rows at `t = 0..4`, row 5 arrives at `t = 8`: pre-trim `n_eff = 5.0` and `target_n_eff_into` reports `[5.0]`, then `trim(8.0)` leaves 1 row (`weight = 1.0 < 3.0`). `read_into` gates `pred` on post-trim `1.0 < 3.0` (`pred = NaN`) while returning pre-trim `out.n_eff = 5.0`, so `rheon-polars` emits `pred = null`, `weight_sum = 5.0`, and **`withheld_reason = null`** (contrast `hawkes.rs:1040, 1109`).
- **Fix:** Capture per-target weights before `trim(key)` (or align both `n_eff` and `min_weight` consistently with `stream.rs`).

#### `GLM-3` — `refit_budget` / `refit_give_up()` permanently kills `Gamma(inverse)` and `Tweedie(power)` groups and drops `step_cap = 1.0` on all GLM families
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/glm.rs:159-161, 301-315, 581-587, 644-649, 920-935`; `python/rheon/_spec_glm.py:118-126`.
- **Two coupled defects:**
  1. **Canonical `inverse` / `power` links (`NaN` forever):** `GlmCfg::validate` forbids `link="inverse"` and `link="power"` without a refit because `beta = 0` gives `eta = 0`, where `fam.mean(0.0)` and `fam.working(0.0, y)` return `None`. When a grouped stream exceeds `refit_budget`, `bank.rs` calls `Glm::refit_give_up()` (`r.done = true, r.over_budget = true`) while `beta` is still all zeros, so `learn` never runs and the group emits `NaN` forever.
  2. **Uncapped steps on all families when `step_cap` was defaulted (Pass-2 catch):** When `step_cap` is unset alongside a refit (`refit_rows > 0` or `refit_span`), `model_cfgs.rs:1302` sets `cfg.step_cap = f64::INFINITY`, relying on `waiting = refit.get(j).is_some_and(|r| !r.done)` (`glm.rs:644`) to apply `WAITING_CAP = 1.0`. Because `refit_give_up()` sets `r.done = true`, **`waiting` becomes `false` and `cap` becomes `+inf` from row 0** — losing the default `1.0` one-pass step cap promised by `_spec_glm.py:118`!
- **Fix:** (1) When `r.over_budget`, `fit_intercept`, `!link.defined_at_zero()`, and `beta[j]` is all zeros, seed `beta[j][0] = fam.start_eta(t)` on the first valid target row; (2) change `glm.rs:644` to `let waiting = refit.get(j).is_some_and(|r| !r.done || r.over_budget);` so over-budget one-pass groups retain `WAITING_CAP = 1.0` when `step_cap` was defaulted to `inf`.

#### `DRIFT-2` — ADWIN `delete_oldest()` cancellation leaves negative (`NaN` `eps`) or huge positive ghost `self.var`, blinding ADWIN after outliers leave
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/drift/adwin.rs:287, 306`.
- **Counterexamples (Python-verified across both signs):**
  1. **Negative `self.var` (`eps = NaN`):** Unlike River's `adwin_c.pyx`, `delete_oldest()` subtracts `self.var -= inc` without clamping. Even on default `AdwinCfg::default()` (`max_width = 1_000_000`), evicting `[1e20, 3.3e19]` leaves `self.var = -5.44e26 < 0`, turning `eps` to `NaN` so `dmean.abs() > eps` is `false` forever.
  2. **Positive ghost `self.var` (Pass-2 catch in Pass 1's fix):** Subtractive cancellation is equally likely to round **up**: on `max_width = 25, clock = 32, restart = true` after `[1e20, 3.3e19]` + 29 zeros, `self.var - inc` evaluates to **`+1.2089e24 > 0.0`** on an **all-zero window**, leaving `eps ~ 1e11` and blinding ADWIN for `10,000+` rows (missed if recomputation only runs when `self.var - inc < 0.0`!).
- **Verified fix:** Whenever `inc >= 0.5 * self.var` in `delete_oldest()` (regardless of sign), recompute `(self.total, self.var)` from the remaining $\le M \lceil \log_2 W \rceil \le 90$ buckets via Chan's parallel merge in $O(\log W)$, and clamp `.max(0.0)` in both `delete_oldest()` and `cut()`.

#### `HAWKES-1` — Unchecked integer overflow in `Kswin::state_bytes`, `2 * stat_size`, and `Hawkes::window_bytes` bypasses `max_state_bytes` and triggers OOM / panic
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/drift/kswin.rs:179-181, 193`; `crates/rheon-polars/src/spec/drift.rs:182, 194-195, 396`; `crates/rheon-core/src/hawkes.rs:581-612`; `crates/rheon-polars/src/spec.rs:3327`.
- **Counterexamples (64-bit release build; debug panic):**
  1. `kswin` with `window_size = 1 << 61`: `8 * window_size + 48` wraps mod $2^{64}$ to **`48` bytes**, passing `check_drift_bytes` and aborting with OOM in `VecDeque::with_capacity(1 << 61)`.
  2. `kswin` with `stat_size = 1 << 63, window_size = 100`: `2 * stat_size` wraps to `0`, bypassing validation (and `Kswin::sound()`), then panics on `window_size - stat_size` inside `draw()`.
  3. `hawkes` (`n_targets = 1, kernel_decays = [1.0], window_events = 1 << 59`): `8 * (rows * 4 + 3)` wraps to `24` bytes (`state_bytes = 72 <= 262,144`), bypassing `max_state_bytes`.
  4. On 32-bit targets, `w as usize` in `spec/drift.rs:182, 194-195` truncates `u64`.
- **Fix:** Use `saturating_mul`/`saturating_add` in all `state_bytes`/`window_bytes` helpers and `checked_mul(2)` for `2 * stat_size`; validate `u64` / `usize::try_from` before casting.

#### `DRIFT-5` — `Adwin::sound()` allows an empty top level (infinite loop on `update()`), and `DriftDetector::restores_as` leaves 4 detectors unchecked
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/drift/adwin.rs:197-213, 228-230, 275-280`; `crates/rheon-core/src/drift/detector.rs:87-94`; `crates/rheon-core/src/drift/eddm.rs:86`.
- **Counterexamples:**
  1. Damaged `Adwin` with `levels = [[[1.0, 0.0]], []], width = 1.0, max_width = 1` passes `Adwin::sound()`. Next `update()` makes `width = 2.0 > 1` and loops `while self.width > self.max_width { self.delete_oldest(); }`, where `delete_oldest()` sees empty `levels[1]` and returns early without popping it → **infinite loop**. `Adwin::sound()` also omits `min_window_length >= 1`, `width <= max_width`, `total.is_finite()`, and `var >= 0 && var.is_finite()`.
  2. `DriftDetector::restores_as` returns `true` unconditionally for `Eddm`, `Ddm`, `PageHinkley`, and `Cusum`: a damaged `Eddm` with `last_error > n` panics in debug / wraps to `1.8e19` in release at `eddm.rs:86`; invalid `ddm.p`/`ddm.n`/`error_above`/thresholds load unchecked.
- **Fix:** Tighten `Adwin::sound()` (including non-empty trailing level when `levels.len() > 1`), defensively pop empty trailing levels in `delete_oldest()`, and add `.sound()` checks for all six detectors in `DriftDetector::restores_as`.

#### `HAWKES-2` — `drop_oldest()` does not zero `r`, `b`, `q`, or `w.since` when a clock window empties, leaking negative `Q` and ULP drift
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/hawkes.rs:843-853, 942-944`.
- **Counterexample (Python-verified):** When `window_clock` empties via `drop_oldest()` (without triggering `form_old_block()`), `w.w_in` is reset to `0.0` but `r, b, q, w.since` are not. Two rows with marks `1e8` and `1.0` (`1e16 + 1.0 == 1e16` in `f64`) dropped across a gap leave the empty window with **`Q = -1.0`** and `r = 2.844e-17`. On a subsequent burst of 10 normal `m = 1.0` events (`dt = 0.15, window_clock = 2.0, beta = 2.0`), `Q = 9.0` instead of `10.0`, producing **8.54% error in `alpha`** (and with 10 normal events after `1e8`, `Q = -10.0` causes non-PD `G`). Separately, `w.since += d_clock` runs unconditionally even while empty, degrading `w.time` offset precision after long quiet stretches.
- **Fix:** When `w.time.is_empty()` in `drop_oldest()`, zero `r, b, q, w.new_sum, w.since, w.w_in`; in `advance()`, only increment `w.since` while `!w.time.is_empty()`.

#### `HAWKES-3` — `Hawkes` omits the 64 MiB `STATE_BYTES_CEILING`, skips the default-cap check in `HawkesCfg::validate()`, and leaves `window_clock` uncapped in `Hawkes::restore()`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/spec.rs:3320-3353`; `crates/rheon-core/src/hawkes.rs:317-337, 1209-1239` (cross-confirmed in Hawkes and spec reviews).
- **Details:** (1) Unlike every other bounded-state model (`clock_span::STATE_BYTES_CEILING = 64 MiB`), `Hawkes` checks only `cap == 0`, accepting `max_state_bytes = u64::MAX`; (2) `HawkesCfg::validate()` checks `bytes > cap` only inside `if let Some(cap) = self.max_state_bytes`, skipping validation when `max_state_bytes: None` (default 256 KB); (3) `Hawkes::restore()` checks `window_events` length but never checks `clock_state_bytes <= cfg.cap()` under `window_clock`.
- **Fix:** Use `clock_span::check_max_state_bytes("hawkes", self.max_state_bytes)`, check `bytes > self.cap()` unconditionally, enforce `STATE_BYTES_CEILING` in `spec.rs`, and validate `clock_state_bytes <= m.cfg.cap()` in `Hawkes::restore()`.

#### `HMM-1` — `learn_stabilized` poisons `Stabilized::scale` with `0.0 * inf = NaN` (or `+inf -> NaN`), permanently breaking `Hmm::restore`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/hmm.rs:2056-2068`; `crates/rheon-core/src/hmm/stabilized.rs:331-339, 356-358, 463-475, 504-508`.
- **Counterexample (Python-verified):** Under given `means`/`covs` (`Some(shape)`), if one state has valid variance `gi = 1e-150` and a row arrives with valid `dv = 1e80` (`<= INPUT_BOUND`): `dv * dv / gi = +inf`. If `p == 0.0`, `p * (+inf) = NaN`, immediately poisoning `self.scale[i] = NaN`; if `p > 0.0`, `self.scale[i] = +inf` and the next row computes `+inf + b * (ei - inf) = NaN`. Because `Stabilized::rebuild` requires finite non-negative `scale` and `held`, saving and restoring the state **permanently fails**. (`targets()` / `settle_all()` can similarly overflow `prior_rows * p * lift` into `st.held`.)
- **Fix:** Skip `p == 0.0` states in `learn_stabilized`, compute `(dv / gi.sqrt()).powi(2)`, guard `observe` updates on `ei.is_finite() && next.is_finite()`, and clamp/guard `prior_rows * p * lift`.
- **Pass-2 sister sites added:** Apply the same guards in `seed_stabilized_with` (`hmm.rs:1728-1748`, where migrating a pre-schema-70 state with decayed `c.n_eff() == 0.0` and `c.var(i) / g_ii == +inf` computes `0.0 * inf = NaN`) and rewrite `(w * v + p0 * p) / t` in share form `(w / t) * v + (p0 / t) * p` in `take_up_factors` (`hmm.rs:830-838`) and `Stabilized::shrink` (`stabilized.rs:367-390`) so large `prior_rows` (`p0`) cannot overflow `p0 * p`.

#### `KAL-2` — Unscaled Givens rotation in `kalman::forms::factor_add` underflows to `NaN` and overflows to silent zeroing bypassing `lost_definiteness`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/kalman/forms.rs:124-142`.
- **Counterexamples (Python-verified):** Unlike `SpdFactor::updated` (`solve.rs:1654`), `factor_add` computes `h = (ljj * ljj + xj * xj).sqrt()` without scaling:
  - Underflow (`|ljj|, |xj| < 1.5e-154`): `h = 0.0` → `cs = NaN, sn = +inf`, poisoning `L` with `NaN`.
  - Overflow (`|ljj|` or `|xj| > 1.34e154`): `h = +inf` → `cs = 0.0, sn = 0.0`, overwriting row `j` of `L` with **`0.0`**. Because `L` remains finite with non-negative diagonal, **`lost_definiteness` does NOT trigger** and posterior variance silently collapses to zero!
- **Fix:** Scale by `big = ljj.abs().max(xj.abs())`: `(a, b) = (ljj / big, xj / big); s = (a * a + b * b).sqrt(); (cs, sn) = (a / s, b / s)`.

#### `LASSO-1` — `f64::max` drops `NaN` deltas in coordinate descent, causing diverged `±inf`/`NaN` fits to falsely declare convergence
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/lasso.rs:1236-1244, 1355`; `crates/rheon-core/src/lasso/split.rs:111`; `crates/rheon-core/src/gramfit/cd.rs:68-70, 254`.
- **Counterexample (Python-verified):** Rust's `f64::max` (`maxNum`) returns `0.0` for `0.0_f64.max(NaN)`. When CD overflows to `±inf` (even on a **valid PSD correlation matrix** with `d = 1e308`), the next sweep computes `|inf - inf| = NaN` → `max_delta` remains `0.0` → `settled(0.0, limit)` returns **`true` (converged!)**, reporting `solve_failures = 0` and warm-starting `±inf` across the rest of the path. In `lasso.rs:1236`, `soft_over(NaN, ...)` also silently returns `0.0`.
- **Fix:** Propagate `NaN` in `max_delta` (`if diff > max_delta || diff.is_nan() { max_delta = diff; }`, or abort on `!newb.is_finite()`) in `lasso.rs`, `lasso/split.rs`, and `gramfit/cd.rs`, and return `f64::NAN` for `rho.is_nan()` in `soft_over`.

#### `REG-3` — Frozen `Warmup::row_weight()` (`ŵ`) freezes the `Sgd` / `Pa` feature scaler on weight scale-down
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/warmup.rs:110-192`; `crates/rheon-core/src/sgd.rs:1969-1979`; `crates/rheon-core/src/pa.rs:505-625`.
- **Live reproduction & Pass-2 correction:** `Warmup` freezes `ŵ = Σw²/Σw` permanently once $W^2/Q \ge 22$. When weights **scale down** (`25` rows at `w = 1.0, y = 0` followed by `275` rows at `w = 1e-6, y = 3x + eps, half_life = 20`, reproduced live on `rheon 2.0.0`), `EwDiag::update` forces `w_eff = max(w_new, 1.0) = 1.0 >> 1e-6`, freezing `sgd(standardize=True)` at slope **`2.07e-5`** instead of `3.0`!
  - *(Pass-2 refutations: (1) Pass 1's weight **scale-up** `1e-6 -> 1.0` explosion claim is **false** — `EwDiag` stores mean variances and `w_eff = w_new.max(floor.max(w)) == w_new` whenever `w >= floor`, verified completely smooth live on `rheon 2.0.0`; (2) Pass 1's parenthetical fix `floor.max(weight).min(sc.n_eff())` is **broken**, keeping `floor >= 0.65`–`1.0` under slow/`inf` half-life and zeroing the floor on row 0.)*
- **Verified fix:** Maintain decaying `(w_sum, q_sum)` on the model's `lam` clock so $\hat{w}_t = Q_t / W_t$ adapts smoothly to weight-regime shifts while preserving constant-weight invariance.

#### `REG-4` — Post-v2.0.0 C7 guard in `Holt::step_into` bypassed by `d_clock = 1e-308`, permanently poisoning `trend = +inf -> level = NaN`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/holt.rs:402, 418-448`.
- **Counterexample (Python-verified):** On `y = [0, 10, 20], w = 1, hl = 10` with `d_clock = 1e-308`, `ri = 1.0 / s = 1e308` is finite (passing C7's `ri.is_finite()`), so `slope = 5.0 * 1e308 = +inf` → `trend = +inf`. On row 2 (`d_clock = 0` or `1`), `level` and `trend` become permanently `NaN`. Independently, `(held_b * trend + weight * slope) * rb` overflows intermediate `weight * slope` (`s = 1e-200, dy = 1e50, w = 1e100`) even when `(weight * rb) * slope` is finite (same at line 402).
- **Fix:** Compute weighted means in share form `(held_b * rb) * trend + (weight * rb) * slope` (and same for `level`), and guard `next_trend.is_finite()` before committing `self.trend[j]`.

#### `REG-5` — `Holt::line_fit_gains` `s0 * w * aged * aged * r0` underflows for `w <= 1.5e-154` (killing trend) and overflows on large `w, d_clock`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/holt.rs:233-244`.
- **Live reproduction (`rheon 2.0.0`):** On `t = [0, 1, 2, 3], y = [0, 10, 20, 30]`, `w = 1.0` emits `[None, 0.0, 20.0, 30.0]`, while `w = 1e-200` underflows `s0 * w = 0.0` (`trend == 0.0`), emitting `[None, 0.0, 11.72, 23.37]` (`41.4%` error); at `w = 1e100, d_clock = 1e60`, `s0 * w * aged^2 = +inf` pins `rs = 1/inf = 0.0` forever.
- **Verified fix (Pass-2 corrected):** *(Pass 1's `s1 + age * (w * aged)` still overflows `w * aged` on float clocks `d = 1e220` and underflows at `w = 1e-305, d = 1e-5`.)* Store `fit_spread` as the weighted **mean** clock variance $V_t = S_t / W_t$, updated via $V' = a_w V + a_w b_w d_{\text{aged}}^2$ with $a_w = s_0 / (s_0 + w), b_w = w / (s_0 + w) \in [0, 1]$ and `gain = b_w * d_aged / V'` (requires schema loader conversion on `Holt.fit_spread`, exact across all $w \in [\text{MIN\_POSITIVE}, 10^{100}]$).

#### `STATE-1` — `envelope::kind_of` ignores `Seal::Absent`, allowing truncated and trailer-corrupted states into `save_checkpoint` and `load_checkpoint`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/envelope.rs:209-222`; `crates/rheon-polars/src/checkpoint.rs:155-192, 237-260, 306-317`.
- **Mechanism:** `checksum::check(bytes)` returns `Seal::Absent` (not `Seal::Broken`) whenever the last 19 bytes do not start with `TAIL = b"\xa8checksum\xc4\x08"`. Because `kind_of` only checks `== Seal::Broken`, any enveloped state truncated after its 33-byte header, any 1-bit flip inside `bytes[n-19..n-8]`, or any trailing garbage byte passes `kind_of` as `Ok(kind)`. `save_checkpoint(path, stages)` then **atomically overwrites the previous good checkpoint on disk** (violating `stream.py:1866`), and `load_checkpoint` returns the corrupted stage bytes unchecked.
- **Fix:** Capture `seal = checksum::check(bytes)` in `kind_of`; reject `Seal::Broken`, require `seal == Seal::Holds` for `Some(head)` (enveloped), and require `Seal::Holds` for sealed legacy versions (bank schema 71, windows v11).
- **Pass-2 fix addition:** `legacy_kind` (`envelope.rs:194-204`) never inspects `schema_version`/`version` and accepts 28-byte truncated legacy headers (`"magic": "polars-online-bank"`). Because `save_checkpoint` only receives freshly enveloped stages from the current library, `save_checkpoint` should require `envelope::open(bytes).is_some() && checksum::check(bytes) == Seal::Holds` unconditionally.

#### `MERGE-1` — `rp.stream.merge` over-reads and pushes an extra chunk past `.head(n)`
- **Location:** `python/rheon/polars/stream.py:1126-1145`; `crates/rheon-polars/src/merge.rs:701, 723, 795-818`.
- **Counterexample:** `a = [1.0, 2.0, NaN]` (or step-back `[1.0, 2.0, 1.5]`), `b = [10.0, 20.0]`, `rp.stream.merge(a.lazy(), b.lazy(), clock="t", chunk_size=2).head(2).collect()`. When row 1 exhausts `a`'s first chunk while reaching `picks.len() == budget == 2`, `merge.rs:802` sets `want = Some(0)`. `stream.py:1134-1144` yields the 2 rows without checking `seen >= n_rows`, pulls chunk 2 of `a`, and calls `m.push(0, chunk)` — **failing `.head(2)` on dirty data past the slice** (and reading up to 100,000 unneeded rows).
- **Verified fix (Pass-2 regression prevention):** Break immediately in `stream.py:1134` (`if n_rows is not None and seen >= n_rows: break`) right after yielding `out` and *before* refilling `want`. *(Do **not** set `want = None` when `picks.len() >= budget` in `merge.rs:799-806` as Pass 1 proposed: stream `i` was already popped off `self.heap` at line 723, so returning `want = None` forces the next `m.pull()` in normal streaming to hit `self.waiting()` at line 701 and return an empty 0-row `DataFrame` on 100% of aligned chunks!)*

#### `STRM-2` — `Weightless::Fresh` on a `w = 0` row at a session boundary leaks the old session's diagnostics, `settled_frac`, and `learned_clock`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/stream.rs:3968-3979, 4004-4064, 4513-4785, 3087-3090, 3219-3222`.
- **Counterexample:** `ewridge` with `session = "s", session_gap = "reset", min_settled_frac = 0.5, emit_sigma = True, emit_metrics = True, emit_clocks = True, half_life = 10`. After 100 rows in session `"A"`, row 100 `(s = "B", w = 0)` replaces only `inst.model`, leaving `inst.decay_time`, `sigma`, `r2`, `conformal`, `checks`, and `last_learned` from session `"A"`. `fit_predict` emits `settled_frac = 0.999` (bypassing `min_settled_frac`!), `sigma`, `r2`, and `learned_clock` all leaked from session `"A"`, contradicting `predict()` and `weightless.rs` (*"scored as `predict` scores a row"*). (`predict_chunk:3221` also leaks `learned_clock` on fresh-session rows.)
- **Fix:** Route `Weightless::Fresh` rows through a fresh `Stream::new(spec)` (or suppress/zero `settled_frac`, `score_d`, and all residual diagnostics), and write `None` to `out.learned_clock[ri]` for fresh-session rows in both `process_chunk` and `predict_chunk`.
- **Pass-2 note:** `Stream::predict_chunk` (`stream.rs:3087-3230` and `conformal_rows.rs`) has the identical old-session leak on `fresh[ri] == true` rows, so fix both `process_chunk` (`Weightless::Fresh`) and `predict_chunk` (`fresh[ri]`).

#### `STRM-3` — `w = 0` rows mutate `last_row`, `summary()["weight_sum_settled"]`, `inst.notified.unstable`, closing session `DataSummary`, and premature `refit_budget` reservations
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/stream.rs:1674-1688, 2514-2516, 2848-2850, 4349-4358`; `summary.rs:448-450`; `bank.rs:1257-1283, 3455-3474, 3953-3986`.
- **Verified defects (reconciled with [ZERO-WEIGHT-AUDIT.md](docs/records/ZERO-WEIGHT-AUDIT.md) in Pass 2):**
  1. `remember_last` overwrites `bank.last_row()` ([PLAN.md](docs/PLAN.md) §5: *"the last row it learned from"*) with `w = 0` rows.
  2. `Stream::readiness` divides `d.weight_sum` by `rows_seen = rows_processed - rows_skipped` (which includes `rows_zero_weight`), halving `row_weight` and corrupting `summary()["weight_sum_settled"]` when `w = 0` rows are interleaved.
  3. `inst.notified.unstable` (`stream.rs:4353`) fires and mutates state on `w = 0` rows (missing `!plan.weightless()`).
  4. Under `group_close = "session"`, a leading `(s="S2", w=0)` row before `(s="S2", w=1)` stays in `seg = 0` (`"S1"`), contaminating `"S1"`'s `DataSummary` and `clock_range` before `"S1"` closes.
  5. `w = 0` rows pollute `DataSummary.clock_min`/`clock_max` and prematurely reserve `refit_budget` before any `w > 0` row arrives *(note: per `ZERO-WEIGHT-AUDIT.md`, `rows_zero_weight` summary rows and `group_close = "monotone"` `high_water` advancement on `w = 0` are intentional and should be kept)*.
- **Fix:** Restrict `remember_last` to `w > 0` rows; exclude `rows_zero_weight` from `rows_seen` in `Stream::readiness`; guard `inst.notified.unstable` with `!plan.weightless()`; exclude `Weightless::Fresh` / `w = 0` rows from `clock_min`/`clock_max` and closing-session summaries; and defer `reserve_refits` until a group's first `w > 0` row.

#### `SPEC-4` — `KMeans` (`update_every`, `split_merge_every`) and `CorrChange` (`permute_every`) accept `inf`, freezing updates and crashing `resolved_defaults()` / corrupting JSON
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/cluster/kmeans.rs:177-182, 269-281`; `crates/rheon-core/src/corrchange.rs:295-296, 560-568`; `crates/rheon-polars/src/defaults.rs:62-81`.
- **Mechanism:** Unlike `pca_every`, `prune_every`, `snapshot_every`, and `coef_every`, these three fields check only `d.is_nan() || d < 0.0`, accepting `f64::INFINITY`. Passing `inf` sets row cadence to `u32::MAX`/`usize::MAX` (silently disabling updates forever), serializes as JSON `null` (no `humanfloat`), crashes `resolved_defaults(&spec)` via `faithful_json()`, and drops the setting in JSON state export.
- **Fix:** Require `d.is_finite() && d >= 0.0` in `KMeansCfg::validate`, `CorrChangeCfg::validate`, and `spec/models.rs`.

#### `SPEC-5` — Task 538 mutual exclusion bypassed: `*_refit_rows = 0` + `*_refit_span = <span > 0>` accepted across all five refitting models
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/stream/model_cfgs.rs:128-140, 943-958, 1043-1053, 1179-1199, 1277-1299`; `rheon-core/src/{glm,expectile,robust,sgd}.rs`.
- **Mechanism:** Because `model_cfgs.rs` collapses `Option<u32>` into `u32` (mapping `None` to `0` when `*_refit_span` is set), core `validate()` only refuses `refit_clock.is_some() && refit_rows > 0`. Explicitly setting `*_refit_rows = 0` (refit off) alongside `*_refit_span = 10.0` is silently accepted on `glm`, `expectile`, `huber`, `quantile`, and `sgd(loss="huber")`, running the span refit and silently ignoring `*_refit_rows = 0`.
- **Fix:** Check `*_refit_rows.is_some() && *_refit_span.is_some()` in `Spec::validate_model` before unwrapping `Option<u32>`, matching `garch`, `kmeans`, `hmm`, and `bocpd`.

#### `SPEC-6` — `Spec::validate()` falsely refuses a feature column named `"intercept"` on six models that have no intercept (regression in commit `8cab69af`)
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/spec.rs:3390-3409`.
- **Mechanism:** `Spec.fit_intercept` defaults to `true`, and `has_intercept_term` was written as a partial blocklist that omitted `Audit`, `Bocpd`, `CorrChange`, `Deco`, `Rcov`, and `Marginal`. Calling `rp.spec.audit("a", columns=["intercept", "x"])` (or any of those six models with a column named `"intercept"`) is falsely refused with advice to set `fit_intercept=False`.
- **Fix:** Convert `has_intercept_term` to an explicit allowlist of the 11 models that actually fit an `"intercept"` term (`EwRidge | Rls | Lasso | Kalman | Huber | Quantile | Ftrl | Sgd | Pa | Glm | Expectile`).

#### `PY-3` — `drift_options` typed as `Mapping` bypasses `_matches` type validation and `_finite` checks in `_spec.py`
- **Location:** `python/rheon/_spec.py:177-181, 245-247, 360-367, 1122, 1221-1222`.
- **Counterexamples:** `typing.get_origin(Mapping[...])` is `collections.abc.Mapping`, not `dict`, so `_matches` falls through to `return True`: `drift_options=123` crashes with raw `TypeError: 'int' object is not iterable`; `drift_options="ab"` crashes with raw `ValueError: dictionary update sequence element #0 has length 1`; `MappingProxyType({"delta": float("inf")})` bypasses `_finite()`.
- **Fix:** Handle `origin in (dict, Mapping)` in `_matches`, `_describe`, `_finite`, and `_lists`.
- **Pass-2 fix additions:** Also handle `Mapping` in `_spec.py:88` (`_json`), `_spec.py:732` (`_finite` container message), and `_spec_words.py:61` (`_bad_word`); see `PY-13` for per-key `drift_options` type validation.

#### `CLOCK-1` — Monotonic `Float64` clock gap overflow (`raw = +inf`) permanently poisons `Exact::removed = +inf`, breaking `Stamp::since`, `Stamp::cmp_span`, clock windows, spans, and `embargo`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/clock.rs:316-358, 708-732` *(mistakenly dismissed as benign in Pass 1's Section 6)*.
- **Mechanism:** `Float64` `clock` columns admit any finite `f64` (`±1.79e308`). On a valid monotonic jump `-1e308 -> +1e308` with `gap_cap = 10.0`, `raw = +inf` and `d = 10.0`. Right above the comment warning that `inf - inf` is `NaN`, line 712 executes unguarded **`self.removed += raw - d`**, setting `self.removed = +inf` permanently (and finite `session_gap` across a session change sets `self.elapsed_removed = +inf`). On **every subsequent normal row** (`1e308, 1e308 + 1, ...`):
  1. `Stamp::since` (`clock.rs:354`, tasks 501/539) evaluates `(a - b) - ((+inf) - (+inf)) = NaN`, poisoning `Expectile`/`Hawkes` across `w = 0` rows.
  2. `Stamp::cmp_span` (`clock.rs:333, 340`) maps `NaN` to **`Ordering::Equal` forever** — immediately firing `start_span`/`refit_span`/`warm_span` at 0 elapsed time, evicting every row on the next stamp in `closed="right"` clock windows, preventing eviction forever in `closed="both"` clock windows (until `max_state_bytes` errors), and releasing `embargo` rows immediately (Hard Rule 2 violation).
- **Fix:** Represent `Float64` `Stamp`s via accumulated finite capped clock `place` (or rebase epoch when `!raw.is_finite()`) so `removed` / `elapsed_removed` never become `±inf`, and never map `NaN` to `Ordering::Equal` in `cmp_span`.

#### `GARCH-12` — `Garch` (`Gjr` & `Figarch`) emits finite negative conditional variance `s_t < 0` on valid `<= 1e100` shocks and poisons `Gjr(dist="t")` `self.h` with `NaN`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/garch.rs:624-637, 689-700`; `garch/series.rs:247, 364, 395`; `garch/linalg.rs:149-209`.
- **Mechanism & Python-verified counterexamples:** `project_metric` (`moved < 1e-14`) and `holds(..., 1e-12)` / `1e-15` slack leave slightly negative shock weights — e.g., real Dykstra output on well-conditioned `Gjr` (`cond(H) ~ 200`) gives `alpha + gamma = -4.108e-15 < 0`, and `Figarch` within `1e-12` slack gives `lam_1 = -9.0e-13` / `lam_2 = -1.8e-13`. On a valid shock `e = -1e8` (`Gjr`) or `1e9` (`Figarch`), `Filter::predict` returns **`s_t = -41.08`** / **`-899,946.8`**. Because `Garch::report` (`garch.rs:624`) checks only `s.is_finite()` (missing `s > 0.0`), it emits **finite negative `variance_<t>`** (and `std_resid = NaN`), and under `Gjr(dist="t")` feeds `s < 0` into `Series::learn`, permanently poisoning `self.h` with `NaN`.
- **Fix:** Clamp projected parameters onto exact feasibility (`y[2] = y[2].max(-y[1])` for `Gjr`; `d in [0, 1 - 1e-4]`, `phi in [0, 0.5*(1 - d)]`, `beta in [0, phi + d]` for `Figarch`), floor `Filter::predict` at `f64::MIN_POSITIVE`, and require `s > 0.0 && s.is_finite()` in `Garch::report` and before `Series::learn`.

#### `HAWKES-7` — `Hawkes` `EventWindow` anchors `w.since` at `form_old_block()` instead of the newer block's first event, collapsing gaps and `g0 -> 0.0` (`pred = NaN`)
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/hawkes.rs:357-359, 773-805` (`window_learn`), `859-920` (`form_old_block`).
- **Mechanism & counterexample (Python-verified):** `hawkes.rs:357-359` states times are held relative to the newer block's start $t_m$, yet `w.since` resets to `0.0` **only** at the end of `form_old_block()` (`w.old > 0`, 0 newer events). After a quiet stretch $G$ (`1e9..1e16`), newer events push `G + dt_i`; the next `form_old_block()` subtracts `w.time[r] - w.time[r-1]` and `now - w.time[0]` at magnitude $G$. On `window_events = 2, beta = 2.0`, 3 unit events + quiet gap `d = 1e16` + two events `0.25` apart collapses **`g0 = 0.0`** (true `0.50`), failing Cholesky and emitting `NaN` intensities/coefs in **both** `window_events` and non-emptying `window_clock`.
- **Verified fix:** In `window_learn` (`hawkes.rs:779`), when `w.rows() == w.old && w.since > 0.0`, add `w.since` to `w.time[..w.old]` and reset `w.since = 0.0` (verified to `< 3.5e-15` across 500 random steps).

#### `GLM-5` — `Glm::learn` commits `work.g` into `self.gram[j]` before checking `work.s` / `solve_ridged`, permanently freezing `beta[j]` on valid `<= 1e100` rows
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/glm.rs:621-639, 931-934`.
- **Counterexample (Python-verified):** On `Gamma(log)` (`eta = -30.0`, `wk.w = 1.0`), a valid row `x = [1e100], y = 1e100, weight = 1e98` (`<= INPUT_BOUND`) produces finite `work.g` (`1e298`), which line 633 **permanently swaps into `self.gram[j]`** *before* line 635 forms `work.s = [1.07e211, +inf]` and line 636 fails `solve_ridged` without moving `beta[j]`. Under `half_life = inf` (or long half-lives), `gram[j][2] = 1e298` freezes `beta[j][1]` (`step ~ 1e-298 == 0.0`) on all subsequent normal rows.
- **Fix:** Form and check finiteness of `work.s` and solve against `&work.g` first; swap `work.g` into `self.gram[j]` only after `solve_ridged` and finite `beta` update succeed.

#### `EXP-5` — `Expectile` `Fit::loss` / `Fit::wls` overflow to `+inf` inside `INPUT_BOUND` (disabling line search and falsely declaring 1-pass convergence) + subnormal `weight * a == 0.0` skip
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/expectile.rs:729-779, 815-878, 1221-1228, 1501`.
- **Counterexamples (Python-verified):** (1) Unscaled `(v * a * z_r) * z_s` in `Fit::wls` overflows `self.g` to `+inf` at `x = 1e100, w = 1e100`, and `Fit::loss` overflows `r * r = +inf` even at `w = 1.0` when warm `beta = -1e55` meets `x = 1e100`: `f_old = +inf` disables step halving (`839`), and `rss = +inf` sets `scale = +inf` (`857`), making `moved <= +inf` (`876`) **falsely return `converged: true` on pass 1**! (2) Subnormal `weight = 5e-324 > 0` increments `w_sum`/`w_target` while `weight * a == 0.0` skips `learn` and `wls`.
- **Fix:** Normalize `v_i /= v_max` inside `Fit`, rescale residuals by `r_max` when `r_max > 1e150`, require `scale.is_finite()` for convergence, and rescale subnormal weights in `Expectile::learn`.

#### `DIAG-11` — Asymmetric `expectile` (`tau != 0.5`) and `glm` permit mean/homoskedastic/Gaussian `Checks` (`emit_calibration`, `emit_breaks`, `emit_specification`, `emit_tails`) whose null hypotheses fail by construction
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/spec/diagnostics.rs:188-196`; `spec.rs:3665-3740`; `checks.rs:124-215`.
- **Derivation:** Unlike `Quantile` (routed via `Spec::quantile_level()` to quantile coverage/CUSUM with `specification`/`tails` suppressed), post-`v2.0.0` `Expectile` and `Glm` return `quantile_level() == None` and accept all four residual diagnostic switches:
  1. **`expectile(0.9)`:** Feeds raw $y - \hat e_{0.9}(x)$ into mean Mincer–Zarnowitz `calibration_wald` ($H_0: \mathbb{E}[Y \mid \hat y] = \hat y$) and uncentered `cusum` ($H_0: \mathbb{E}[r] = 0$). On exact stationary Gaussian $Y = 2X + \mathcal{N}(0, 1)$ (`half_life = 50`), $\mathbb{E}[r_t] = -1.0006 \neq 0$, driving `calibration_wald` to **`~578 >> 5.991`** and `cusum` to **`~-8.5`** (~100% false alarms).
  2. **`glm` (`poisson`/`gamma`/`tweedie`/`binomial`):** Feeds heteroskedastic, non-Gaussian raw residuals $y - \hat\mu$ into `breusch_pagan` and `jarque_bera`, rejecting ~100% of well-specified GLMs.
- **Fix:** Refuse inapplicable mean/Gaussian `Checks` on `Expectile(tau != 0.5)` and `Glm` in `Spec::validate_each` (matching `emit_se_coef`/`emit_robust_se`), or evaluate `Expectile` calibration/breaks on generalized score residuals $|\tau - \mathbb{1}\{r < 0\}| r$ and `Glm` breaks on Pearson residuals $(y - \hat\mu)/\sqrt{V(\hat\mu)}$.

#### `GLM-6` — `ModelKind::Glm` omitted from formula-target validation, silently skipping out-of-support resolved targets while inflating `n_eff`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/spec.rs:3488-3533`; `bank.rs:491-537`; `bank/refusals.rs:353-373`; `resolvers.rs:236-290`.
- **Mechanism:** `spec.rs:3505-3523` refuses formula targets on `Ftrl(logistic)` and `Sgd(logistic | poisson)`, but omits post-`v2.0.0` `ModelKind::Glm`, and `outside_support` (`bank.rs:532`) runs only on plain column targets (`!t.is_formula()`). When a resolved formula target on `glm` produces values outside the family's support (`y < 0` on Poisson/Tweedie, `y <= 0` on Gamma, `y` outside `[0, 1]` on Binomial), no refusal is raised — `Glm::step_into` (`glm.rs:920-935`) silently skips learning while still adding `weight` to `self.w_sum` (`weight_sum` / `n_eff`).
- **Fix:** Align `ModelKind::Glm` (`binomial`/`poisson`) with `Sgd`/`Ftrl` in `spec.rs:3505-3523`, and call `outside_support(spec, &resolved)` inside `resolve_targets` for any `Glm` formula target before mutating stream state.

---

### Medium (Post-v2.0.0)

#### `DRIFT-1` — ADWIN hardcodes `R = 1` (per task 508 River parity), contradicting `"scale-free"` docs and blinding ADWIN on return-scale residuals (`sigma <= 1e-3`)
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/drift/detector.rs:14-16, 69`; `crates/rheon-core/src/drift/adwin.rs:303-308`.
- **Derivation, simulation & task 508 context (Pass-2 refined):** Bifet & Gavaldà's Bernstein threshold $\sqrt{2 (V/n) d/m} + \frac{2}{3} R d/m$ assumes bounded range $R = 1$. Feeding raw `|r|` is mandated as the default by [PLAN.md](docs/PLAN.md) task 508 (`line 1996`, bit-equal to `river.drift.ADWIN`), **but** is not scale-free: at return scales ($\sigma \le 10^{-3}$), $\frac{2}{3} d/m \approx 6/n_1$ dwarfs $|\bar x_0 - \bar x_1|$ (`0` detections in 5,000 rows after a 3× volatility jump; at `sigma = 1e-2`, 29× slower than `sigma = 1.0`), while at `sigma = 1e1..1e4` the linear tail term vanishes.
- **Fix (preserving task 508 River default):** Remove the inaccurate `"scale-free / has no unit"` claim in `detector.rs:14-16`, `spec.py`, and `DIAGNOSTICS.md`, document ADWIN's $O(1)$ residual-scale assumption, and optionally expose a `range` / `scale` key in `drift_options` (defaulting to `1.0`).

#### `DRIFT-3` — Clock-integrated tabular CUSUM and Page-Hinkley multiply noise by `d_t` (`Var = d_t^2`), collapsing ARL on irregular clocks
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/drift/cusum.rs:7-21, 60-69`; `crates/rheon-core/src/drift.rs:17, 107`; `python/rheon/spec.py:738-745`.
- **Derivation, simulation & task 508 context (Pass-2 refined):** [PLAN.md](docs/PLAN.md) task 508 (`line 1996`) explicitly specified clock-integrated CUSUM (`drift_threshold` as a `Span`, matching `PageHinkley`). However, multiplying standardized $z_t \sim N(0, 1)$ by $d_t$ gives $\mathrm{Var}(d_t(z_t - k)) = d_t^2$ (neither discrete SPRT $\mathrm{Var} = 1$ nor Brownian $\mathrm{Var} \propto d_t$): at unit mean step $\mathbb{E}[d_t] = 1$, $d_t \sim \mathrm{Exp}(1)$ collapses $\mathrm{ARL}$ (`h = 5, k = 0.5`) from `465` to **`81.8`** (5.7×), and bursty arrivals (90% `0.1`, 10% `9.1`) collapse $\mathrm{ARL}$ to **`30.8`** (15×).
- **Fix:** Document the $d_t^2$ variance scaling on irregular clocks in `spec.py` and `DIAGNOSTICS.md`, and consider an opt-in row-counted or $\sqrt{d_t}$ diffusion scaling option in `drift_options`.
