#### `HMM-2` — Dimensional mismatch in `Stabilized::fallback` shrinks given `G_s` by `1e6×` or bricks `diag_stabilized` after a total-forgetting gap + exact-mean hit
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/hmm.rs:770-776, 1779-1785, 2264-2270`; `crates/rheon-core/src/hmm/stabilized.rs:205, 427-436`.
- **Mechanism (Python-verified, downgraded High → Medium in Pass 2):** Under `Some(shape)`, `psi` is a **dimensionless** multiplier on $G_s$ (initialized to `1.0`), yet `self.fallback` is always dimensional `cfg.precision_prior` (default `1e-6`). When a `>= 1075`-HL gap (`lam == 0.0`) is followed immediately by a row landing bit-exactly on the mean (`ei == 0.0`), `top == 0.0` resets `psi` to `precision_prior`, shrinking $G_s$ by `1e6×` (and if `precision_prior * G_ii` underflows to `0.0`, `ln(0) = -inf` bricks `diag_stabilized`).
- **Fix (wire-compatible since `fallback` is unserialized):** Set `fallback = if shape.is_some() { 1.0 } else { precision_prior }` in `Stabilized::new` and `Stabilized::rebuild`.

#### `GARCH-5` — EGARCH filter upper-clamp `s / hi` overflow, zeroed `dl` vs analytic slope, dimensional mismatch, and zero backcast / EWMA underflow
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/garch/filter.rs:114-120, 192-198, 245`.
- **Details (Python-verified):** (1) When `|e| ~ 1e-6` (`hi = 1e-6`) and `lt = 700` (`s = exp(700) ~ 1e304`), `s / hi` overflows to `+inf` even though `lt.min(LN_S_MAX) - ln(hi) = 713.8` is finite; (2) `hi + ln(s / hi)` adds variance-unit `hi` to dimensionless `ln(s / hi)` (breaking return-scale equivariance: at `hi = 1e-4`, `s_clamped = 1.2104`), and line 195 zeroes `dl` even though true $\partial \ln s_{\text{clamped}}/\partial \theta = (\partial lt / \partial \theta) / s_{\text{clamped}} = 0.8261 \neq 0$, causing analytic gradient `e.g` to disagree with `e.f` in `optim.rs`; (3) 75 initial zeros make `bc = 0.0` (`lag[1] = -inf`, start fails across all 3 doublings), and ~12,050 consecutive zeros online underflow `lag[2] = 0.94 * lag[2]` to `0.0`, producing `0.0 + ln(s / 0.0) = +inf -> NaN`.
- **Fix:** Replace `ln(s / hi)` with `lt.min(LN_S_MAX) - ln(hi)`; compute true sensitivity `dl[i] /= s_clamped` (or use scale-equivariant `hi * (1 + (lt - ln(hi)))` with `dl[i] *= hi / s_clamped`); floor `bc` at buffer mean square `v` when `bc == 0 && v > 0`, and guard `lag[2] > 0.0`.

#### `GARCH-6` — `Garch::restore` allows corrupted `nu = -1e17` (infinite loop in `ln_gamma`) and bypasses `max_state_bytes` under `start_span`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/garch.rs:772-793`; `garch/series.rs:85-107`; `garch/dist.rs:45-73`.
- **Details:** A damaged state with `started = true, dist = T, theta[k] = -1e17` passes `restore`; on the next `step()`, `ln_gamma` runs `while x < 20.0 { x += 1.0; }` forever (`-1e17 + 1.0 == -1e17`). Corrupted `nu = 2.0`, FIGARCH `beta = 1.0`, `w_sum < 0`, or oversized `start_span` buffer (`bytes > cap`) also pass `restore`.
- **Fix:** Validate `m.bytes() <= m.cfg.cap()`, finite non-negative `w_sum` and buffer weights, and when `started == true`, finite `theta` within `[NU_LO, NU_HI]`, `|lam| <= LAM_LIM`, and `beta < 1.0`; guard `if !(x > 0.0 && x.is_finite()) { return f64::NAN; }` in `ln_gamma` and `digamma`.

#### `GARCH-7` — `series.w_sum = 0.0` on start re-buffering breaks `target_n_eff` monotonicity and `min_weight` gating
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/garch/series.rs:161-170`; `crates/rheon-core/src/garch.rs:624, 762-766`.
- **Counterexample:** Single-target `Garch(start_rows=500, min_weight=600)` where rows `0..499` are i.i.d. (`!identified, doubles=false`) resets `series.w_sum` from `500` to `0.0` while `self.w_sum` stays `500`. After rows `500..999` start cleanly, rows `1000..1099` have `started = true`, `weight_sum = 1000..1099 >= 600` (zero nulls, no decay), yet `report()` withholds predictions as `below_min_weight` because `series.w_sum = 500 < 600`.
- **Fix:** Split `series.w_sum` into `learned_w` (cumulative weight ever learned, never reset, used for `min_weight` / `target_n_eff_into`) and `est_w` (RPEM denominator `W_t`, reset on re-buffer).

#### `GARCH-8` — FIGARCH `start_span` overcharges byte cap by 16 B/row (failing row 0 at exact cap) and bypasses `FIGARCH_MIN_START_ROWS = 2000`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/garch.rs:704-712`; `crates/rheon-core/src/garch/series.rs:410-419`.
- **Counterexamples:** (1) `Figarch(truncation=1000, start_span=100.0, max_state_bytes=8232)` passes `validate()` (`state_bytes(0) = 216 + max(0, 8000) + 16 = 8232`), and row 0's true size is still `8232`, yet `garch.rs:705` blindly adds `+ 16` (`with = 8248 > 8232`) and fails row 0; (2) `start_due` checks `MIN_START_ROWS` (50) instead of `FIGARCH_MIN_START_ROWS` (2,000), allowing 50-row FIGARCH starts under `start_span`.
- **Fix:** Compute `with = others + Self::series_bytes(&self.cfg, buf.len() + 1) + 16` at `garch.rs:705`, and use `FIGARCH_MIN_START_ROWS` for `GarchVol::Figarch` in `series.rs:415`.

#### `GARCH-9` — `unseen = UNSEEN * (1.0 + e.f.abs())` breaks weight-scale invariance for tiny weights (`w << 1/n`)
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/garch/optim.rs:215`.
- **Verification:** On 500-row GARCH(1,1), scaling weights by `1e-10` shrinks `|f0|` from `853.2` to `8.53e-8` while `1.0 + |f0|` stays `1.0`, routing `< 1.0` nat steps into the trapezoid branch (`GARCH-1`) and breaking `garch.rs:127-128`.
- **Verified fix (Pass-2 corrected):** Pass `w_sum` and set `let unseen = UNSEEN * (w_sum + e.f.abs());` *(do not use `UNSEEN * e.f.abs()`, which collapses to `0.0` whenever continuous Gaussian/t log-likelihood crosses `e.f == 0.0`)*.

#### `GLM-4` — Numerical precision & overflow issues in `family.rs` and `glm.rs`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/family.rs:283, 304-349`; `crates/rheon-core/src/glm.rs:640-657, 678-694, 733, 811, 933`.
- **Verified counterexamples (Python):**
  1. `wk.w * (wk.z - wk.eta)` adds then subtracts `eta`, losing >1% accuracy on `Poisson(log)` at `eta = 30, y = round(mu) + 1` (`rel_err = 1.04e-2`).
  2. Binomial `1.0 - mu` at `eta = +30` loses ~43 mantissa bits (`rel_err = 1.02e-3`, `unit_deviance` error `2.04e-3`).
  3. Tweedie `unit_deviance` returns `NaN` (`inf + (-inf)`) for valid `p = 30, eta = -30, y = 1`.
  4. Gamma `unit_deviance` underflows `y / mu` to `0.0` (`+inf` deviance) for `eta = 1e-150, y = 1e-200` (exact `805.90`).
  5. Unchecked `moved = NaN` bypasses `step_cap` (`NaN > cap` is `false`); `beta[j] += scale * d` overflows to `+inf` on valid `ridge = 1e-300, step_cap = inf, Gamma(log), x = 1e-205, w = 1e100, y = 1e100`; `run_refit` writes `gram[j]` without finiteness check; `Family::takes(f64::INFINITY)` returns `true` for Poisson/Gamma/Tweedie.
- **Fix:** Add direct closed-form `score` to `Working`; compute `1 - mu = 1/(1 + exp(eta))` for `eta > 0`; use `ln(y) - ln(mu)` on underflow/overflow; factor `mu^(1-p)` in Tweedie deviance; guard `moved`, `beta`, and rebuilt `gram` for finiteness; require `y.is_finite()` in `Family::takes`.
- **Pass-2 additions:** Also `Tweedie(p = 2.5, link = "power")` at valid `eta = 1e200` gives `mu = 4.64e-134 > 0`, finite `wk.w = 4.64e66`, and `mu^p = 0.0 -> wk.z = -inf`! *(Note: `Family::takes(f64::INFINITY)` is masked in `ModelBank` / `Glm::step_into` by `usable(f)`, affecting direct `rheon_core::Family` callers.)*

#### `SPAN-1` — `Stamp::cmp_span` loses nanosecond exactness on temporal spans past `2^23` seconds (~97 days)
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/clock_span.rs:59-61`; `crates/rheon-core/src/window.rs:262-267`; `crates/rheon-core/src/clock.rs:318-341`.
- **Counterexample (Python-verified):** `span_reached` and `WindowClosed::has_left` pass `None` to `cmp_span_ns`, converting `a - b` to `f64` seconds via `seconds_of_ns`. At `span = 365d` (`1 ULP = 3.725 ns`), `365d - 1ns` and `365d + 1ns` both round to `Equal`, contradicting `_spec_glm.py:100` and `_spec_expectile.py:85, 107`.
- **Verified fix (Pass-2 corrected):** Carry `span_ns: Option<i128>` from `Span` into `clock_span` and `WindowClosed` *(comparing against `ns_of_seconds(span)` inside `cmp_span` cannot recover sub-ULP nanoseconds already lost when `Span::value` converted to `f64` seconds)*.

#### `SPAN-2` — `HeldCap` post-refit / `refit_give_up()` cap trip, pre-rejection state mutation, and `2 * bytes` overflow risk
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/glm.rs:563-567, 894-918, 972-976`; `expectile.rs:243-250, 1022-1029, 1408-1477`; `clock_span.rs:137-142`.
- **Details:** (1) `validate()` omits `32 * m` bytes (`m * size_of::<Option<Stamp>>()`) for `refit_clock`, while runtime `admit`/`note` include it and run even when `refit.iter().all(|r| r.done)` (including after `refit_give_up()`), tripping spurious `state_over_cap` errors/warnings after buffers are freed; (2) in `glm.rs`, `self.own += d_clock` mutates before `admit` rejects and `w == 0` decays state even after `self.over.is_some()`; in `expectile.rs`, `self.clock` and `self.trim(key)` + `refit_window()` mutate before `over`/`admit` returns; (3) `2 * bytes > cap` in `clock_span.rs:138` overflows `u64` if `bytes > u64::MAX / 2` (whereas `bytes > cap / 2` is mathematically identical for all `u64` with zero overflow).
- **Fix:** Gate `refit_span` cap checks on `self.over.is_some() || self.refit.iter().any(|r| !r.done)`; check `self.over.is_some()` before mutating `own`/`clock`/trimming; align `32 * m` accounting between `validate()` and runtime; replace `2 * bytes > cap` with `bytes > cap / 2`.

#### `DRIFT-6` — Page-Hinkley permanent `(-inf) - (-inf) = NaN` death and silent drop of `+inf`; CUSUM state wipe on `d = 0` with `±inf`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/drift.rs:100-116`; `drift/detector.rs:60-68`; `drift/cusum.rs:60-69`.
- **Counterexamples (Python-verified):** (1) Finite `r = 1e100, scale = 1e-205` (`e = 1e305`) followed by `e = 0, d = 86400` drives `cum = -inf, min_cum = -inf`, making `statistic() = NaN` forever (`0` flags on 1,000 rows of massive drift); (2) `|r| / scale = +inf` hits `!e.is_finite()` in `PageHinkley::update` and is silently dropped (`false`) instead of flagging; (3) simultaneous timestamp `d = 0.0` with `r / scale = ±inf` computes `0.0 * inf = NaN`, and `(self.up + NaN).max(0.0)` returns `0.0`, silently wiping CUSUM's accumulated state!
- **Fix:** Rebase `cum = 0.0; min_cum = 0.0` whenever `cum <= min_cum` in `PageHinkley::update` (wire-compatible and eliminates long-run cancellation too); clamp overflowed finite `r / scale` in `DriftDetector::update`; return `false` early on `!z.is_finite() || d <= 0.0` in `Cusum::update`.

#### `DRIFT-7` — DDM accepts `0 < drift_threshold <= 1.0`, guaranteeing false alarms every `warm_start + 1` rows; doc comment misstates baseline
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/spec/drift.rs:424-431`; `crates/rheon-core/src/drift/ddm.rs:30, 82-90`; `spec/drift_tests.rs:135-137`.
- **Verification (Python):** DDM flags when `p + s > p_min + drift_threshold * s_min` (relative to `p_min`, NOT `p_min + s_min` as `ddm.rs:30` states). With `drift_threshold = 0.5, warm_start = 30`, stationary Bernoulli(0.2) fires on **every 31st row** (10 flags in 310 rows); `1.0` fires on every 1-ULP wiggle.
- **Fix:** Require `drift_threshold > 1.0` in `spec/drift.rs:424` and correct `ddm.rs:30`'s doc comment.

#### `HMM-3` — Discontinuous `10^9×` violation of given-covariance first-row guarantee in `Shape::of`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/hmm/stabilized.rs:98-100, 147-169, 427-436, 483-492`; `hmm.rs:2264-2270`.
- **Counterexample:** Identical anisotropic diagonal `covs = diag(1e-20, 1e20)` across states returns `Shape::of == None`, causing `floor_psi` to clamp `psi[0]` up to `2^-100 * 1e20 = 7.89e-11` (**`7.89e9×` inflation** on row 0); perturbing state 1 by 1 ULP activates `Some(shape)` and uses exact `1e-20`.
- **Verified fix (Pass-2 Hard Rule 5 state-compatibility warning):** `Saved` (`stabilized.rs:483-492`) does **not** serialize whether `Shape` was `Some` or `None` — `Hmm::restore` (`hmm.rs:2269`) recomputes `m.cfg.prior_shape()`, and `Saved.scale` stores dimensional variances under `None` vs dimensionless ratios under `Some(Shape)`. Changing `Shape::of` in-place without a schema migration would **silently square `G_ii` (`[1e-40, 1e40]`) when restoring schema 70–72 states**! Pair the `Shape::of` change (returning `None` only for identical scalar diagonals) with a `SCHEMA_VERSION` bump + loader conversion (or an explicit `#[serde(default)] shaped: bool` flag in `Saved`).

#### `KAL-3` — `carlson` produces `NaN` and wipes `P` when `r = r_inn / weight` underflows to `0.0`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/kalman.rs:2915-2925, 2945-2947`; `kalman/forms.rs:98-119`.
- **Counterexample (Python-verified):** `r_inn = 1e-250, weight = 1e100` makes `r = 0.0` while `s_inn = zpz > 0` passes line 2925 (`r_inn > 0 && s_inn > 0`). Entering `carlson` with `alpha = 0.0` yields `b = ±inf / NaN`, `b * 0.0 = NaN`, triggering `lost_definiteness` and wiping `P` (where `Covariance` form succeeds cleanly).
- **Fix:** Require `let r = r_inn / weight; if r > 0.0 && s_inn > 0.0 && s_inn.is_finite() && err.is_finite()` at `kalman.rs:2925`, and skip `fj == 0.0` inside `carlson`.

#### `LASSO-2` — Off-by-one factor of `lam` in `gramfit::young` asymptotic Kish estimate suppresses early `solve_failures`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/gramfit/cd.rs:173-190, 206-210`; `crates/rheon-core/src/ewcov.rs:804`.
- **Derivation & verification (Python):** Row 0 arrives with `lam = 1.0`, so after $t$ rows `EwCov::prior_scale` equals $\lambda^{t-1}$ (not $\lambda^t$). Plugging `prior_scale` into `n_t * (1 + s) / (1 - s)` overestimates $n_\infty$ by up to $2\times$ (and $+\infty$ on row 1): on the author's own test (`k = 50, half_life = 30`, $n_\infty = 86.57 < 115$), `young()` falsely returns `true` on rows $t = 1, 2, 3$. Also `gramfit/cd.rs:189-190` says callers pass `1` for windows where `lasso.rs:1120` correctly passes `0.0`.
- **Fix:** Account for the first-row `lam = 1` offset (`s = prior_scale * lam` for uniform spacing, or derive `n_inf` using the step decay), and fix the doc comment at `gramfit/cd.rs:189-190` to say `0`.

#### `REG-11` — Multi-target non-atomicity at `max_state_bytes` cap in `Sgd` and `Robust`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/sgd.rs:2828-2865`; `crates/rheon-core/src/robust.rs:1758-1797, 2910-2935, 3013-3015`.
- **Mechanism:** Both models check `HeldCap::admit(held + row, limit)` per target inside `for j in 0..m`, mutating target 0's buffer before discovering target 1 exceeds `limit` (triggered even in `sgd/span_tests.rs:134-157`: target 0 admits row 50, target 1 refuses!). In `Robust`, target 0 can also trigger `refit_target(0)` on a row that tripped `self.over`.
- **Fix:** Compute total bytes added across all open targets for the row and call `HeldCap::admit` once before mutating any target.
- **Pass-2 addition:** On each target's first buffered row, `start_due` sets `first = Some(now)` (`+32` B in `RefitBuf::held_bytes`, `huber_refit.rs:670`), which `row` in `sgd.rs:2834` and `robust.rs:1776` omits, allowing up to `32 * m` B overshoot past `max_state_bytes` (see also `SPAN-3`).

#### `REG-15` — `EwDiag::update_keeping` fails to cap `floor` at `self.w_sum`, inflating a light history `1,000,000×` to 50% weight across a 1-HL gap
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/ewdiag.rs:255-278` (called from `kalman.rs:2988`).
- **Counterexample (Python-verified):** Unlike `sgd.rs:1974` (`(floor / history).min(1.0)`), `update_keeping` omits `let kept = floor.min(self.w_sum)`. Row 1 `(w = 1e-6, x = 0)` followed by Row 2 `(w = 1.0, x = 10)`: at gap `0.99 HL`, `a = 5.035e-7, m = 9.999995`; at gap `1.01 HL`, `a = 0.5, m = 5.0` — inflating a `1e-6` weight row **1,000,000×** to equal weight with `1.0`.
- **Fix:** Cap `let kept = floor.min(self.w_sum);` and check `kept > carried`, using `kept` in `total = kept + w` and `a = kept / total`.

#### `REGIME-1` — Post-v2.0.0 `permute_every` missing `since_draw` validation in `CorrChange::restore` and unvalidated on non-window kinds
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/corrchange.rs:560-568, 836, 1891-1893, 2007-2057`.
- **Details:** (1) `CorrChange::restore` never checks `m.since_draw.is_some() == m.cfg.permute_every.is_some()` (unlike `KMeans::restore`), silently disabling clock redraws on malformed states; (2) `CorrChangeCfg::validate` checks `permute_every` only inside `CorrChangeKind::Window`, letting `Monitor`/`Sequential` accept `NaN`/`-1.0` and waste per-row work stepping `since_draw`.
- **Fix:** Refuse `permute_every.is_some()` on non-window kinds (or validate it outside the `Window` arm and only allocate `since_draw` for `Window`), and enforce `since_draw.is_some() == (window && permute_every.is_some())` in `restore()`.

#### `MERGE-2` — `rp.stream.merge` casts `restart_after_step_back` to `f64`, losing exactness past 104.25 days / `2^53` and raising `OverflowError` on huge ints
- **Location:** `python/rheon/polars/stream.py:1214, 1227`; `crates/rheon-polars/src/merge.rs:85, 427-460`.
- **Counterexamples:** `parse_duration("105d1ns")` rounds down to `105d` in `f64` (so a step-back of `105d1ns` silently restarts instead of refusing); `"105d3ns"` rounds up to `105d4ns` (falsely refusing `105d4ns`); `Int64` `(1 << 53) + 1` rounds down; `10**400` raises uncaught `OverflowError`.
- **Fix:** Store exact `enum Restart { Int(i128), Float(f64) }` on `MergeConfig`, passing exact nanoseconds/integers from Python.

#### `MERGE-3` — Resuming a merge holding tie rows under `.head(n)` + `save_state` contradicts docs and drops rows if the caller follows the documented `source`-count slice rule
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/merge/state.rs:101-112`; `python/rheon/polars/stream.py:1056-1060, 1115`.
- **Counterexample:** Run 1 (unsliced, `save_state="s1"`) holds tie rows `a: [2.0], b: [2.0]`. Run 2 (`load_state="s1", save_state="s2"`) on `a2=[3.0], b2=[3.0]` with `.head(1)` emits `[(2.0, "a")]` (consuming **0** rows of `a2`), keeps `b: [2.0]` held in `s2` (contradicting *"nothing is held"*), and a caller slicing `a2.slice(counts["a"])` drops `a2`'s `3.0`.
- **Fix:** Document clearly that slice-resuming by `source` counts applies only when `load_state` held no tie rows (or expose per-input consumed row counts separate from emitted held rows).

#### `STATE-2` — Uncapped `Vec::with_capacity(seq.size_hint())` (4 GiB OOM) in `Bin`/`ByteBuf` and missing `catch_unwind` around `IpcReader::finish()`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/refresh/held.rs:57-63, 182-183`; `crates/rheon-polars/src/checkpoint.rs:84-90`; `crates/rheon-polars/src/windows_frame.rs:2958-2960`.
- **Mechanism:** (1) A MessagePack `array32` header claiming `0xffffffff` elements in `Bin::deserialize` / `ByteBuf::deserialize` (reachable on unsealed legacy `refresh_time` v2 / `windows` v9–10 states or crafted states) allocates 4 GiB immediately instead of capping preallocation at 4096 bytes like standard `serde`; (2) `refresh/held.rs:183` and `windows_frame.rs:2958` omit the `std::panic::catch_unwind` wrapper around `IpcReader::finish()` that `merge/state.rs:312` and `windows_frame.rs:2899` use (review P7).
- **Fix:** Cap `with_capacity(seq.size_hint().unwrap_or(0).min(4096))` in `Bin` and `ByteBuf`, and wrap `IpcReader::finish()` in `catch_unwind` at both remaining sites.

#### `IO-1` — `checksum::Hashing::write` copies entire large stage buffers into `p.buf`, doubling peak memory during `save_checkpoint`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/checksum.rs:121-132`; `crates/rheon-polars/src/checkpoint.rs:230-231, 245-248`.
- **Mechanism:** `StageOut.state` writes the entire stage slice via `hashing.write_all(state)` in one call, and `Hashing::write` unconditionally executes `p.buf.extend_from_slice(buf)`. A 200 MB bank stage reallocates `p.buf` from 64 KiB to 200 MB and holds that extra 200 MB until `save_checkpoint` finishes, contradicting `checkpoint.rs:230-231`.
- **Fix:** When `p.buf.len() + buf.len() >= BLOCK`, flush `p.buf` first if non-empty, then if `buf.len() >= BLOCK`, hash and write `buf` directly without copying into `p.buf`.

#### `IO-2` — `AtomicFile` lacks `O_EXCL`, leaves a world-readable window on `0o600` files, copies `0o7000` special mode bits, and `save_checkpoint` misclassifies OS `EINVAL` as `ValueError`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/atomic.rs:84-96, 114-120, 235-244`; `crates/rheon-py/src/lib.rs:1653-1664`; `python/rheon/polars/_frame.py:167-186`.
- **Details:** (1) `File::create(&tmp)` uses `O_CREAT | O_TRUNC` without `O_EXCL` (symlink/PID-reuse hazard); (2) replacing a `0o600` file writes the entire payload under umask `0o644` before `chmod`ing after `sync_all()`; (3) `& 0o7777` attempts to copy `setuid`/`setgid`/`sticky` bits (`EPERM` risk); (4) `lib.rs:1661` maps all `ErrorKind::InvalidInput` (including kernel `EINVAL` from `sync_dir` on FUSE/vfat) to `PyValueError`; (5) `_save_path("", who)` passes validation, failing only after streaming finishes.
- **Fix:** Use `OpenOptions::new().write(true).create_new(true)` (retrying `NEXT_TEMP` on `AlreadyExists`) with Unix creation `.mode((dest_mode & 0o777) | 0o200)` and `keep_mode` masked by `0o777`; check `e.raw_os_error().is_none()` in `lib.rs:1661`; reject `path == ""` in `_save_path`.

#### `BANK-5` — `reserve_refits` notice names wrong parameter for `Glm`/`Expectile` and all five `*_refit_span` models
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/bank.rs:1646-1655`.
- **Mechanism (expanded in Pass 2):** (1) Wildcard `_ => "huber_refit_rows"` catches `ModelKind::Glm` and `ModelKind::Expectile` (whose count parameter is `refit_rows`); (2) when **any** of the 5 span-capable models (`glm`, `expectile`, `huber`, `quantile`, `sgd`) uses `*_refit_span`, `refit_reservation()` reserves `max_state_bytes` (`256 KiB` default) while `*_refit_rows` is unset and mutually exclusive with `*_refit_span`!
- **Fix:** When a span refit is active (`refit_span`/`huber_refit_span`/`quantile_refit_span` is `Some`), advise lowering `max_state_bytes` (or raising `refit_budget`); otherwise name `bin_refit_rows`, `quantile_refit_rows`, `refit_rows` (`Glm | Expectile`), or `huber_refit_rows` (`Huber | Sgd`).

#### `BANK-7` — Multi-instance `half_life` grids multiply `max_state_bytes` by grid size and emit duplicate `state_cap_notice` warnings; refusal misnames rows as `"events"`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/stream.rs:4191-4193`; `stream/state_cap.rs:129-137`; `bank/refusals.rs:125-131`.
- **Mechanism:** A 5-half-life grid on `glm`, `expectile`, `huber`, `quantile`, `sgd`, `kmeans`, or `hmm` builds 5 independent models each holding up to `max_state_bytes` (up to 320 MiB at the 64 MiB ceiling), emits 5 identical warnings without `@h...` suffix, and `over_state_cap` hardcodes `"learned none of the events"` for all non-Hawkes models.
- **Fix:** Budget `max_state_bytes` per group across instances (or enforce total per-group bytes in `Stream`), deduplicate/suffix notices, and pass `"rows"` vs `"events"` into `over_state_cap`.

#### `SPEC-7` — `Sgd` omits `huber_refit_span` and `max_state_bytes` from foreign-loss refusal tables; stale `"huber_refit_rows"` error wording
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/spec/models.rs:194-228`; `python/rheon/_spec_gradient.py:816-825`; `rheon-core/src/robust.rs:659, 666`; `rheon-core/src/sgd.rs:807` (cross-confirmed in spec and Python reviews).
- **Details:** Direct Rust `Spec::validate()` accepts `sgd(loss="squared", huber_refit_span=..., max_state_bytes=...)`, and multi-problem reporting in `Spec::check()` hides both behind earlier foreign-loss errors; `robust.rs:659, 666` and `sgd.rs:807` hardcode `"huber_refit_rows"` when `huber_refit_span` was given.
- **Fix:** Add `huber_refit_span` and `max_state_bytes` to both foreign-loss tables and parameterize the error string on `self.refit_clock.is_some()`.

#### `SPEC-8` — `Garch` missing from `decays_by_kind` gives contradictory advice; `Expectile` missing from `fit_memory()` / `level_absorber()` breaks `emit_feature_health` under `window_size` and `window_rows`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/spec.rs:3614-3639, 4192-4220`; `crates/rheon-polars/src/spec/diagnostics.rs:307-313, 354-358`.
- **Details (expanded in Pass 2):** (1) `garch(..., min_settled_frac=0.5)` advises `"Set half_life to a finite value"`, which `garch` unconditionally refuses; (2) `expectile` with **either** `window_size=100` **or** `window_rows=100` (`half_life=inf, emit_feature_health=True`) is falsely refused with `"half_life is inf, with no window_size"`, and diagnostics inherit `Decay::Halflife(inf)` instead of the window's Kish half-life.
- **Fix:** Add `ModelKind::Garch { .. }` to `decays_by_kind`, and handle both `Expectile { window_size: Some(w), .. }` and (when unclocked) `Expectile { window_rows: Some(n), .. }` in `fit_memory()` and `level_absorber()` (plus a clear clock-vs-row message in `spec.rs:4205` when `clock.is_some()` + `window_rows.is_some()`).

#### `PYO3-2` — `_cpu_check.py` omits `OSXSAVE`/`XGETBV` check and uses Windows `RWX`; `build.rs` misses `-feature` and `-C target-cpu=x86-64-v3`
- **Location:** `python/rheon/_cpu_check.py:136-178, 192-219`; `crates/rheon-py/build.rs:30-57`; `.github/workflows/vm-checks.yml` (`VM-CHECKS.md:54`).
- **Details (expanded in Pass 2):** (1) Executing AVX/AVX2/FMA requires OS `OSXSAVE` (leaf 1 ECX bit 27) and `XGETBV(0)` XCR0 bits 1–2 (`0x6`); (2) Windows allocates `PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE` (`0x40`, violating W^X / ACG), and POSIX leaks `self._map` if `mprotect` fails; (3) `build.rs` parses only `+feature`, missing `-feature` overrides **and** `-C target-cpu=x86-64-v3` (actually used by `vm-checks.yml` `variants=v3`, leaving `REQUIRED_X86_FEATURES = ""`!).
- **Verified fix (Pass-2 x86 `#UD` trap warning):** **Do NOT execute `XGETBV` (`0x0f 0x01 0xd0`) unconditionally!** On x86-64, `XGETBV` itself raises `#UD` (`SIGILL`) whenever `CR4.OSXSAVE == 0` (`CPUID.1:ECX[27] == 0`) — branch past `XGETBV` unless `OSXSAVE == 1`! Use `PAGE_READWRITE` → `VirtualProtect(..., PAGE_EXECUTE_READ)` on Windows, close `mm` on POSIX `mprotect` failure, and seed psABI `x86-64-v2`/`v3`/`v4` baselines + sequential `+feat`/`-feat` in `build.rs`.

#### `SPAN-3` — `Sgd`, `Robust`, `Garch`, `Hmm`, and `Bocpd` mutate online model state before `HeldCap::admit` rejects an over-cap span row and continue mutating past `state_over_cap()`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/sgd.rs:2360-2870`; `robust.rs:1758-1797, 2820-3016`; `garch.rs:683-712`; `hmm.rs:1468-1506, 2134`; `bocpd.rs:1309-1325, 1993`.
- **Mechanism:** Extending `SPAN-2` (`Glm`/`Expectile`) and `REG-11` across all five remaining span-capped models: `Sgd` and `Robust` update all online accumulators (`beta`, `cov`, `scaler`, `w_sum`, `mad`) *before* `HeldCap::admit` rejects at the end of `step_into` and keep learning online on subsequent rows when `self.over.is_some()`; `Garch` increments `self.w_sum` past `over` and mutates `series[0..j]` before target `j` fails `admit`; `Hmm` and `Bocpd` decay `a`/`buffer`/`n_eff` and advance `place` before `admit` rejects.
- **Fix:** Check `if self.over.is_some() { return ...; }` immediately after OOS prediction and run atomic multi-target `HeldCap::admit` *before* mutating any accumulator or `place` across all five models.

#### `GLM-7` — `Glm::step_into` skips `self.next.take()` on `weight == 0.0` and `refused_step` rows, leaking stale `Stamp`s under `refit_clock`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/glm.rs:603-607, 876-888, 981-983`.
- **Mechanism:** Unlike `Expectile`, `Hawkes`, `Garch`, and `Since`, `Glm::step_into` calls `self.stamp_row` (`self.next.take()`) only inside `if weight > 0.0`, leaving `self.next = Some(stamp)` dangling across `w = 0` or `refused_step` rows to corrupt the next unstamped learned row's `refit_clock` span check.
- **Fix:** Consume `let handed = self.next.take();` unconditionally on entry to `Glm::step_into`.

#### `KAL-6` — `Kalman::step_as` gates feature-moment `update_keeping` on target-observation `self.elapsed` instead of feature-moment gap time
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/kalman.rs:1285-1287, 2712-2718, 2987-2991` (introduced in `1b122fb5`).
- **Mechanism:** (1) On a regular observed stream with `half_life < d_clock` (`hl = 0.5, d = 1.0`, `lam = 0.25`), `unobserved = 1.0 > 0.5` and `carried = 1/3 < 1.0` hold on **every row forever**, overriding `b` from `0.75` to `0.50`; (2) across `y = None` (`embargo`) rows, `self.stats` updates every row while `self.elapsed` never resets, falsely clamping subsequent regular steps.
- **Fix:** Track a separate feature-moment `stats_elapsed` reset on every `weight > 0` row, and review the `unobserved > half_life` threshold when `d_clock > half_life` on regular grids.

#### `STATE-3` — Missing `restore` / `has_shape` cap, finiteness, and structural invariants across core models
- **Location:** `glm.rs:996-1028`; `expectile.rs:1600-1638`; `sgd.rs:1126`; `robust.rs:3125`; `bocpd.rs:2050`; `hmm.rs:2201`; `ewdiag.rs:60`; `ftrl.rs:574`; `rls.rs:830`; `audit.rs:1046, 1096`; `cluster/micro.rs:643, 877`; `rcov.rs:908, 964, 1346`; `corrchange.rs:2007`.
- **Verified failure modes (several live-reproduced via `ModelBank.load_bytes`):**
  1. **Post-v2.0.0 span-cap bypass on load:** `Glm`, `Expectile`, `Sgd`, `Robust`, `Bocpd`, and `Hmm` never check `held_bytes <= cap()` when `over.is_none()`.
  2. **Panics & unbounded rings on damaged/legacy states:** `ColumnAudit::has_shape` (`audit.rs:1046`) omits `min <= max`, panicking `f64::clamp` in `report()` (`1096`); `Micro::restore` omits `n_clusters == non_outlier_count`, underflowing `n_clusters -= 1` in `drop_at` (`643`); `Rcov::emit`/`push` (`908, 964`) test exact `==`, growing `VecDeque` without bound if restored `fin.len() > bandwidth` or `pre_ring.len() >= kn - 1`.
  3. **Silent `NaN` / negative-variance corruption (live-reproduced):** `EwDiag::try_from` accepts `w_sum = -2.5` (negative variances); `Ftrl::restore` accepts `n = -4.0` (`sqrt -> NaN`, zeroing coords); `Rls::restore` accepts `0.0` diagonal pivots; `StepGain` accepts `{ resting: true, n: 0.0 }` (`0/0 = NaN`, never wakes).
- **Fix:** Enforce `held_bytes <= cap()` when `over.is_none()`, `min <= max` in `ColumnAudit`, `n_clusters` count in `Micro`, `>=` ring pops + cap checks in `Rcov`/`CorrChange`, and non-negative finite weights/variances/pivots across `EwDiag`, `StepGain`, `Glm`, `Expectile`, `Ftrl`, `Rls`, `Bocpd`, `Hmm`, and `Lasso`.

#### `PY-13` — Post-v2.0.0 Python surface gaps in `ModelBank.check()`, `_spec.py` integer floors & `drift_options` types, and `UnstableWarning`
- **Location:** `python/rheon/polars/_check.py:179-203, 470-520`; `python/rheon/_spec.py:553-595, 680-690, 1122`; `python/rheon/_warnings.py:111-119`.
- **Live-reproduced details:**
  1. `ModelBank.check()` omits `"glm"` from `_UNCENTRED_LIMITS` and `_RIDGED` (`l2`), reporting zero findings on `glm` with `x ~ 50,000 ± 0.3` even though `glm` uses uncentred `[1, x]` and penalizes the intercept (`R/2 |beta|^2`); `_UNCENTRED_LIMITS` also ignores `fit_intercept=False` on linear models.
  2. `_AT_LEAST_ONE` / `_FLOOR` in `_spec.py` omit `horizon`, `truncation`, `start_rows`, `window_events`, `window_rows`, and `max_state_bytes`, telling callers with `-1` to `"Set <key> to 0 or more"` when `0` is immediately refused (`start_rows >= 50`, others `>= 1`).
  3. `drift_options` union typing lets `{"delta": True}` or `{"stat_size": 1.5}` bypass Python checks and raise raw Rust `serde` errors.
  4. `rheon.UnstableWarning.__doc__` contradicts task 542 (`with_windows` state & formula stability) and publicly names private `:mod:rheon.polars._gbdt` (task 470).
- **Fix:** Add `"glm"` (and `fit_intercept=False`) to `_check.py`; add the 6 post-v2.0.0 integer keys to `_AT_LEAST_ONE`/`_FLOOR`; validate per-key `drift_options` types in `_spec.py`; and update `UnstableWarning.__doc__`.

#### `SPEC-10` — Missing duplicate checks on `resid_quantiles`, `mahal_quantiles`, and `hawkes.kernel_half_lives` + dead `refit_budget` accepted when refit is disabled
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/spec.rs:3263-3291, 3842-3867`; `spec/models.rs:134, 384-427, 955-1320`.
- **Live-reproduced details:** (1) `resid_quantiles = [0.5, 0.5]` and `mahal_quantiles = [0.99, 0.99]` pass `Spec::validate()` and hit `duplicate_field` with unworkable advice (`"rename a target, feature or grid label"`); (2) `hawkes(kernel_half_lives=["5m", "300s"])` or `[1e-308]` fails inside `HawkesCfg::validate` blaming `kernel_decays` (which the user never set) with raw `0.0023104906018664843` or `inf`; (3) `refit_budget` is silently accepted and ignored when refit is off (`*_refit_rows = 0`, `huber_delta = inf`, `ridge_scale = "sum"`, windowed `expectile`), inconsistent with `marginal(bin_huber_delta=inf)`.
- **Fix:** Call `first_duplicate` on all three lists in `Spec::validate()` (checking `(LN_2 / h.value()).is_finite()` on `kernel_half_lives`), and refuse `refit_budget.is_some()` whenever initial refit is disabled.

---

### Low (Post-v2.0.0)

- **`KAL-1`** (`kalman.rs:2715-2718, 2987-2991`, `ewdiag.rs:255-278`, downgraded High → Low in Pass 2): Unreachable via `rheon-polars` (task 539 routes `w = 0` to `predict()`) and explicitly documented as an accepted `> 1075`-HL underflow boundary in `kalman/tests/gap_moments.rs:232-234`. *(Pass-2 fix warning: Pass 1's `has_history: bool` on `EwDiag` would mutate `EwDiagWire` under Hard Rule 5 **and** is defeated by `REG-15`'s `floor.min(self.w_sum) == 0.0`; only needed if `rheon-core` ever tracks `max_w_sum` under a schema bump.)*)

- **`HMM-4`** (`hmm.rs:1620, 1636-1638, 1862-1895`, downgraded Medium → Low in Pass 2 — `rheon-core` only since `ModelBank` routes `w = 0` to `predict()`): In `age_stabilized(..., transient = true)`, (1) `if !transient` is missing around `self.solve_failures += 1` (`1890`), and (2) `let all = self.factor_states.iter().any(|f| f.ages_out(lam, p0))` (`1862`) sets `saved = None` whenever *any* state ages out, permanently mutating surviving non-aged-out states on a direct-core `w = 0` row. *Fix:* Save/restore per non-restarted state when `transient == true` and guard `solve_failures += 1` with `if !transient`.)

- **`PY-4`** (`python/rheon/_spec_point.py:291-304`, downgraded Medium → Low & corrected in Pass 2): `hawkes` emits its half-cap `RheonWarning` (using `{name!r}` single quotes instead of `quoted(name)`) *before* `_common(...)` validates the spec, so invalid specs warn before raising `ValueError`. *Verified fix:* Call `spec = _common(...)` first, then warn with `quoted(name)` when `window_events is not None` *(Pass-2 corrections: `@_checked` already converts tuples via `_lists()`, so tuples DO warn; and do **not** warn `whenever window_size is None` with the `window_events` template, which prints `"its window keeps None rows"` on windowless specs!)*.)

- **`GARCH-10`** (`garch/linalg.rs:194-209`, `garch/series.rs:392-397`): Dykstra stopping test `moved < 1e-14` is unscaled relative to `||y||` (fails when `omega ~ 1e8`), single-pass cleanup is non-idempotent, and at `cond(H) = 2.3e8` on FIGARCH Dykstra exhausts 2,000 sweeps and silently falls back to Euclidean projection. *Fix:* Scale tolerance to `1e-14 * (1 + ||y||)` or replace with an exact primal/dual active-set QP for $n \le 4, r \le 7$.

- **`GARCH-11`** (`garch/fit.rs:378-382`, `docs/MODELS.md:2989`, `garch/series.rs:175`): (1) FIGARCH's `d = 0, phi = beta` is 2 equality restrictions leaving a 1D gauge parameter (`4 - 1 = 3` excess parameters, not "3 restrictions"); (2) `MODELS.md:2989` says the start buffer *adds* 16 B/row where `series_bytes` takes `max(16 * rows, 8 * M)`; (3) all-zero series keep `withheld_reason = "below_warm_rows"` instead of `"not_identified"`.

- **`GLM-EXP-LOW`** (`_spec_expectile.py:145`, `_spec_glm.py:87`, `glm.rs:147, 303, 366, 930, 963`, `expectile.rs:448, 788-805, 822`): (1) `_spec_expectile.py:145` claims `pred_<t>` waits for a row per coefficient, contradicting default `ridge=1e-6` fitting from row 1; (2) `glm.rs:147` hardcodes `k = n_features + 1` even when `fit_intercept = false` (contradicting `_spec_glm.py:87`); (3) direct-core `weight < 0.0` skips learning in `glm.rs:885, 950, 956` yet adds negative `weight` to `w_sum`/`w_target`; (4) `Fit::refit` runs `wls(None)` twice when `tau == 0.5 && warm.is_none()`, and `seen` clones an unread `Vec<f64>` every pass; (5) `restore` uses unchecked `n * width`/`n * k`/`n * m` (32-bit wrap risk) and omits `first.is_some()` / `over.is_none()` checks; (6) `glm.rs:303` omits `refit_span` from the inverse/power link refusal message.

- **`HAWKES-4`** (`hawkes.rs:201-208, 256-257, 725-731`): Subnormal `half_life = 1e-310` produces `gamma = +inf` and `g0_step(inf, 0.0) = NaN` on row 0 (and huge `kernel_decays = [1e308, 0.9e308]` overflow `bu + bv` to `inf`). *Fix:* Refuse `!gamma.is_finite()` and non-finite `bu + bv` / `bu * bv`, and return `0.0` on `d == 0.0` in `g0_step`.

- **`HAWKES-5`** (`hawkes.rs:1006-1012`): `stamp_ahead` uses `back_by(last, -d_clock)`, causing float-clock `window_size` edges to compute `(t1 - t0) + (t2 - t1)` instead of `t2 - t0` (159 edge mismatches found over tenths in `[0.1, 2.9]`, e.g., `t = [0.1, 0.2, 0.9], span = 0.8`). *Fix:* Pass the exact `Stamp` into `predict_into` for scoring/zero-weight rows.

- **`HAWKES-6`** (`docs/MODELS.md:4083-4085, 4206-4212`): Stale byte examples (`72` and `208` should be `64` and `200`, matching `Hawkes::state_bytes` and `HAWKES.md`) and row-count example (`4,090` at 40 B/row in 256 KB should be `6,551`, and `3,200` should be `3,273`).

- **`DRIFT-8`** (`drift/kswin.rs:108-140`): `ks_critical_h` rebuilds `ratio` (`Vec` of size `r + 1`) linearly for `h = 1..=h*` ($O(r^{3/2})$ time, $h^*$ allocations — ~219 MB temporary allocation at `stat_size = 50,000`). *Fix:* Compute `ratio` once in $O(r)$ and binary-search `h`.

- **`DRIFT-9`** (`drift/adwin.rs:331-335`): At `max_buckets == 1` when `levels[0]` is empty, the final bucket sets `n1 = 0.0` and divides `u1 / 0.0` before checking `n1 >= l` (can trigger spurious cuts if `min_window_length == 0` via direct Rust / damaged state). *Fix:* Break on `n1 == 0.0` before dividing and assert `min_window_length >= 1`.

- **`HMM-5`** (`hmm.rs:312-371, 596-608, 1520-1526, 2232-2249`): (1) `warm_clock` is unvalidated when `means`/`covs` are provided; (2) `Hmm::restore` omits `m.place.is_some() == m.cfg.warm_clock.is_some()`; (3) `step_in` calls `self.seed()` (clearing `buffer`) before `held_cap().note(...)`, missing half-cap warnings on the seeding row; (4) `skip_serializing_if` on non-trailing fields breaks positional compact msgpack (`rmp_serde::to_vec`; `Bank` uses `to_vec_named` and is unaffected).

- **`BOCPD-4`** (`bocpd.rs:346-352, 1170-1194`): `skip_serializing_if` on non-trailing `hazard_on_clock`, `solve_failures`, `place`, `over` breaks positional compact msgpack (`rmp_serde::to_vec`) round-trips (named encoding used by `Bank` is unaffected).

- **`KAL-4`** (`kalman/forms.rs:166-187`): `Kalman::with_covariance_form` takes `.sqrt()` converting `Covariance -> SquareRoot`, but fails to square diagonal entries back when converting `SquareRoot -> Covariance`.

- **`KAL-5`** (`kalman.rs:1947, 1958-1966`): `covariance_now` omits the `!(open && i < off)` diffuse-intercept guard used by `add_process_noise` and `factor_noise` when `standardize = false` *(Pass-2 note: reachable only via crafted state or if `zpz` overflows twice while `zb` is finite, since normal row 1 closes `open` before `readiness_noise` becomes finite on row 2)*.

- **`REG-16`** (`robust.rs:3125-3141`): `Robust::restore` omits `m.place.is_some() == m.cfg.refit_clock.is_some()` (unlike `Sgd::try_from`), letting a damaged state never close its span until hitting `max_state_bytes`.

- **`AUDIT-2`** (`audit.rs:86-91, 1299-1308, 1401-1430`): (1) `Audit::merge` ignores `has_clock` and `gap_cap`; (2) module doc at lines 86–91 contradicts task 539's `weight == 0.0` clock exclusion; (3) direct-core negative `weight < 0.0` updates columns/clock while skipping `n_eff`.

- **`UTIL-1`** (`hasher.rs:44-56`): Packing `rest.len()` into `buf[7]` creates deterministic 100% collisions between `(8k+r)`-byte strings and NUL-padded `8(k+1)`-byte strings ending in byte `r` (e.g., `hash(b"group-1") == hash(b"group-1\x07")`). Safe inside `HashMap`/`IndexMap` (which check equality), but latent hazard if reused for fingerprints.

- **`MERGE-4`** (`merge.rs:177, 184, 352-363, 618`, `merge/state.rs:309-338`, `arrow.rs:1087-1090`): (1) `by` dtype (`List`/`Array`/`Binary`) unchecked in `Merge::new` (fails mid-stream with raw Polars error); (2) `restarts` addition unchecked (`u64::MAX` wraps/panics on crafted state); (3) corrupted held rows in `restore()` report input-order errors instead of `"state is damaged"`; (4) `dtype_name` omits parameters for `Struct`/`Decimal`/`Enum`/`Categorical`.

- **`BANK-10`** (`bank.rs:3228-3258, 4056-4072`, `garch.rs:492-497`): (1) `fit_predict_arrow_with` and `predict_arrow` run dtype/pool checks before `refuse_if_broken()`, inconsistent with `fit_predict_rows`/`predict_rows`; (2) `Garch::next` and `Garch::warning` omit `#[serde(skip)]` (unlike all other state-cap models).

- **`SPEC-9`** (`spec/label_clock.rs:174-198`, `spec_diff.rs:39-99`, Pass-2 refined): (1) `check_label_clock` omits `Hawkes`, clock-hazard `Bocpd`, and featureless `Garch` under out-of-order label-time learning; (2) `spec_diff.rs` sorts keys alphabetically, reporting `model.coef_prior` instead of `model.type` on `lasso` vs `ewridge` (live-reproduced) — short-circuit on `f.model.kind_name() != b.model.kind_name()` at `spec_diff.rs:43`.

- **`PY-11`** (`python/rheon/polars/_check.py:183, 501-519`): `ModelBank.check()` includes `"expectile"` in `_RIDGED` and recommends `standardize=True`, which `expectile` does not accept (`TypeError`).

---

## 4. Confirmed Findings — Pre-Existing Code & Theory (`<= v2.0.0`, Still Present at `c4e7c7d2`)

### Critical (Pre-Existing)

#### `AUDIT-1` — Integer underflow panic (debug) / silent `u64` wraparound corruption (release) in `Counts::merge`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/audit.rs:592-608`.
- **Mechanism & live reproduction:** `cut = c[cap]` is the `(cap + 1)`-th largest count. Whenever `self.counts.len() >= cap + 2` and any entry at rank `> cap + 1` has `*e < cut`, line 603's unchecked `*e -= cut` panics in debug builds and wraps to `u64::MAX` in release builds.
  - **Reproduced live** (`bank.audit("a", pooled=True)`, `distinct_cap=2`, Group A = `[1.0]*10 + [2.0]*8`, Group B = `[3.0]*6 + [4.0]*2`, `cut = 6`): key `4.0` wraps `2 - 6 -> 18446744073709551612`, emitting `top_value = 4.0`, **`top_count = 9223372036854775807` (`i64::MAX`)**, **`equal_by_chance = 5.0338e35`**, and leaving `counts.len() == 3 > 2` (so `has_shape()` returns `false`, breaking state reload!).
- **Fix:** Replace `*e -= cut` with `*e = e.saturating_sub(cut)`.

#### `WIN-1` — Catastrophic cancellation in `mass_between*` silently corrupts or nulls `ewm_mean`, `ewm_var`, and `ewm_std` at `>= 54` half-lives
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/windows.rs:1906-1952` (`mass_between`, `mass_between_in`, `mass_between_fill`, `mass_between_close`).
- **Derivation & live reproductions:** Computing $M(\text{far}) - M(\text{near})$ instead of factored $2^{-\text{near}/h} M(\text{far} - \text{near})$ loses $\text{near}/h$ bits of mantissa precision; at $\text{near} \ge 54h$, both `exp_m1` calls round to `-1.0`, yielding exact `0.0` (100% cancellation).
  - **Reproduced live (backward null):** `t = [0.0, 54.0], x = [42.0, None], half_life = 1.0, window_size = 200.0`: unbounded `ewm_mean = 42.0`, windowed `ewm_mean = None`, windowed `ewm_var(bias=True) = None` (true `0.0`).
  - **Reproduced live (backward wrong mean):** `t = [0.0, 1.0, 54.0], x = [100.0, 200.0, None], half_life = 1.0, window_size = 200.0`: unbounded `ewm_mean` at `t=54` is `150.0` (equal weights on `[-146, 0]` and `(0, 1]`), while windowed `ewm_mean` cancels `M(200) - M(54) = 0.0`, erasing `100.0` and outputting **`199.99999999999997`**.
  - **Reproduced live (forward null & wrong mean):** `t = [0, 54, 201], x = [1, 42, 99], half_life = 1, window_size = 200, reverse = True` gives **`None`** at `t = 0`; `t = [0, 54, 55, 201], x = [1, 100, 200, 999]` gives **`99.99999999999999`** instead of `150.0`.
- **Fix:** Replace `M(far) - M(near)` with `k.discount(near) * k.mass(b - a)` across all four `mass_between*` functions (returning `b - a` when `half_life.is_infinite()`).
- **Pass-2 fix note:** `mass_between_fill` (`1935-1940`) is only called with `a == t` (`3063`) so never cancels, **and** the reference test helper `mass_bf` (`windows.rs:4081-4100`) has the identical `m(t - a) - m(t - b)` bug and must be updated alongside `mass_between*` so `every_operator_matches_its_definition` passes.

#### `WIN-2` — Backward and forward `ewm_quantile` underflow surviving weights to `0.0` at `> 1074` half-lives (and `close()` nulls forward `ewm_mean`)
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/window_quantile.rs:340-347, 418-458`; `crates/rheon-polars/src/windows.rs:3315-3366`.
- **Live reproductions (`rheon 2.0.0`, expanded in Pass 2):**
  1. **Backward `ewm_quantile` null-anchor wipeout (Pass-2 catch):** `Direction::Backward` in `QSide::turn_over` (`423, 434`) sets `anchor = *tau.last()`, which includes **null rows**! On `t = [0, 10, 1100, 2001], x = [99, 42, None, None], half_life = 1, window_size = 2000`, `turn_over` anchors at `1100` (`None`), underflowing `(10, 42)` to `0.0` and returning **`None`** (while unbounded returns `42.249`).
  2. **Forward `ewm_quantile(reverse=True)` (`turn_over` & `add_from_anchor`):** Anchors `front` (and `back` on 100% non-null data!) at the oldest/null row, turning true median `100.0` into **`1.00775`** or `None` once $\tau_i - \tau_0 > 1074 h$.
  3. **Forward `ewm_mean(reverse=True)` (`windows.rs:3316, 3344-3346`):** Both `mass_between_close` (`3316`) and `close()` (`3344`) discount relative to `t.tau`; on `t = [1198, 2300, 2401], x = [None, 42, None], half_life = 1, window_size = 1202`, `discount(1102) = 0.0` turns `[42.0]` into `None`.
- **Verified fix (Pass-2 corrected — avoids `front` `+inf` overflow):** (1) Anchor backward `turn_over` at the **last non-null** row in `tau[..n]`; (2) anchor forward `turn_over` and `back` at the **first non-null** row, and when `QSide::pop` evicts older rows such that the next surviving non-null row `tau[k]` has `tau[k] - front.anchor > 500 * h` (or `front.total == 0.0`), rebuild `front` from the surviving slice anchored at `tau[k]` *(never rescale weight-1 forward `front` buckets up by $2^{+500}$, which overflows to `+inf`!)*; (3) in `close()` (`Stat::Mean`), anchor **both** `mass_between_close` (`3316`) and `seg` (`3344`) at `r_ref = at if base.0.n > 0.0 else otau`.

---

### High (Pre-Existing)

#### `BOCPD-1` — `read_into` Pass 2 (`rel != 1.0`) skips `z.is_finite()` (permanently poisoning `logjoint`), and `Robust` BOCPD computes `0.0 * -inf = NaN` (or `(-inf) - (-inf) = NaN`) on extreme outliers
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/bocpd.rs:990-995, 1526, 1570-1571, 1604-1605, 1756-1760, 1818-1849, 1870-1874`.
- **Counterexamples (Python-verified):** (1) When `rel != 1.0` (e.g., `w = 1e100` then `w = 1e-300` so `rel` underflows to `0.0`), Pass 1 (`rel = 1.0`) passes line 1820, then Pass 2 computes `0.0 * (-inf) = NaN` without checking `log_sum_exp(new).is_finite()` and swaps `NaN` permanently into `self.logjoint` (symmetrically `rel > 1.0` can write all `-inf`). (2) Under `Robust`, a run with `lp = -inf` gives `weights[i] = 0.0` and `0.0 * (-inf) = NaN` in `grow_into` — **and (Pass-2 catch)** if *all* runs yield `lp == -inf` (`mode == -inf`), `lp - mode = NaN` and Rust's `(f64::NAN).clamp(0.0, 1.0)` returns `NaN != 0.0`, bypassing a `weights[i] == 0.0` guard!
- **Verified fix:** In `Robust` (`bocpd.rs:1756-1760`), return `false` from `log_predictive` when `!mode.is_finite()`; in `grow_into`, treat `weights[i] == 0.0` as `0.0`; and check `log_sum_exp(new).is_finite()` after Pass 2 before committing `self.logjoint`.

#### `REG-6` — `Sgd::refit_target` on warmup-switch row 22 with `standardize = true` and `constraint` projects using uninitialized `mbuf = [0]` and `sbuf = [1]`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/sgd.rs:1675-1696, 1716-1738, 2380-2420, 2815-2865`.
- **Counterexample (Python-verified):** On row 22, `held = self.warmup.switched()` is still `false` at line 2380, so `mbuf = [0]` and `sbuf = [1]` are never populated; at line 2817 warmup switches `true`, and line 2863 calls `refit_target(j)` → `project_mapped(j)` using `[0]` and `[1]`. On `fit = [5.0, 0.6, 0.6], m = [100, 100], s = [0.01, 100]` with simplex constraint: true projection gives `b0 = 25.0, b = [0.4, 0.6]` (`pred(m) = 125.0`); row-22 bug gives `b0 = 5.0, b = [0.5, 0.5]` (`pred(m) = 105.0`, `-20.0` bias).
- **Fix:** In `refit_target`, project using `sc`'s actual `scales_of(sc, off)` and `sc.mean(i)` directly rather than `mbuf`/`sbuf`.

#### `REG-7` — Catastrophic cancellation on `a == 0.0, b == 1.0` after total-forgetting gaps wipes out the post-gap mean (`m -> 0.0`) across 7 accumulators
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/ewdiag.rs:238-244, 349-359, 470-480, 503-509`; `ewcov.rs:1103-1159, 1268-1288, 1516-1521` (`TargetMoments::learn`); `gaps.rs:184-211, 227-234`; `robust.rs:2890-2900`; `stats.rs:919-934` (`EwAutoCorr::update_weighted`).
- **Live reproduction (`rheon 2.0.0`):** Feeding `(t=0, x=1e18, y=1e18)` then `(t=10000, x=1.0, y=1.0)` at `half_life = 1.0` (`a = 0.0, b = 1.0`) makes `ewridge` emit `coef = [0.0, 0.0]` instead of `[1.0, 0.0]` because `1.0 - 1e18` rounds to `-1e18` and `comp::add(1e18, 0.0, -1e18)` yields `0.0` *(Pass 2 identified two additional affected sites: `TargetMoments::learn` and `EwAutoCorr::update_weighted`)*.
- **Fix:** Fast-path `a == 0.0` (`carried == 0.0`) across all seven `comp::add` Welford accumulators to set `mean = x; mean_lo = 0.0; var = 0.0` directly.

#### `REG-8` — `Constraint::project_in_units` overflows `1.0 / (sc * sc)` on usable small-variance features (`var <= 1.8e-308`), producing `NaN` or violating `coef_sum`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/constraint.rs:274-281, 321-365`.
- **Live reproduction (`rheon 2.0.0`):** `sgd(standardize=True, coef_sum=1.0)` on `x ~ ±1e-155` turns `coef` and `pred` permanently `None` (`NaN`) at row 22 (`1.0 / sc^2 = +inf -> mu = -0.0 -> -0.0 * inf = NaN`); at `var = 1e-308` on 2 features, `den = +inf -> mu = -0.0` returns unprojected `[0.3, 0.2]` (`sum = 0.5 != 1.0`).
- **Verified fix (Pass-2 correction — see `REG-19`):** *(Do **not** clamp `ti = (r_i * r_i).max(f64::MIN_POSITIVE)` as Pass 1 proposed: on `c = [0.2, 10.0], sc = [1e-155, 1.0], lo = [0, 0], hi = [2, 2], coef_sum = 1.0`, `t_hi(1)` overflows to `+inf`, is dropped by `if t.is_finite()`, and returns `[0.0, 0.0]` with `sum = 0.0 != 1.0`!)* Instead, use the unified caller-units + signed-infinite-breakpoint + pivot-residual KKT solver detailed in `REG-19`.

#### `REG-9` — `Ftrl::teach` (and `Sgd` AdaGrad) permanent `inf`/`NaN` state poisoning via `g_max` vs `g` multiply-order mismatch and unchecked `n + g^2` / `/ alpha` overflow
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/ftrl.rs:413-436`; `crates/rheon-core/src/sgd.rs:1539-1545, 2721-2775`.
- **Counterexamples (Python-verified):** (1) `err = 1e220, z = 1e100, w = 1e-200`: `g_max = (err * w) * z = 1e120` passes `g_max^2` guard, while `g = (err * z) * w = +inf` poisons `n, prox, zz` to `inf`/`NaN`; (2) two rows with `g = 1.2e154` pass `g * g = 1.44e308` yet overflow `n + g^2 = +inf` (freezing `Sgd(AdaGrad)` slot forever and turning `NaN` on next gap); (3) tiny `alpha = 1e-300` overflows `s = (sqrt(n_new) - sqrt(n)) / alpha`.
- **Fix:** Match `(err * weight) * z[i]` order and pre-check finiteness of `n_new`, `s`, `zz_new`, and `prox_new` across all `i` before mutating state; check `(sums[i] + g * g).is_finite()` in `Sgd` AdaGrad.
- **Pass-2 fix note:** When `Sgd::apply_step` rejects an overflowed `sums[i] + g * g` or `bi + di`, `Sgd::step_into` must take `self.refused_step(j, found)` (`sgd.rs:1617, 2773`) rather than `took_step` so a resting `StepGain` gate's probe counter (`rested`) and `Level` state do not advance.

#### `REG-10` — `EwRidge` unchecked `sig2` update overflows `weight * resid^2` to `+inf` and permanently poisons residual variance to `NaN`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/ewridge.rs:3017-3028`.
- **Counterexample (Python-verified):** Unlike `robust.rs`, `sgd.rs`, and `kalman.rs`, `ewridge.rs` writes `sig2[j]` unconditionally and multiplies `weight * resid * resid` before dividing by `ws_new`: `w = 1e100, resid = 1e105` overflows to `+inf` (even though `resid^2 = 1e210` is finite!), and the next `lam = 0` gap computes `0.0 * inf = NaN`, permanently destroying `sigma2`, `se_coef`, and `pred_se`.
- **Fix:** Compute in share form `a * sig2 + b * (resid * resid)` and commit only `if next.is_finite()`.
- **Pass-2 fix note:** If `next` is non-finite (e.g. `|pred| > 1.34e154` so `resid * resid = +inf`), still age `self.wsig[j] = aged` (matching line 3026's `_ => self.wsig[j] = aged` arm).

#### `MARG-1` — Windowed `LagOrigin` re-centering omits unmatched-row bias, causing unbounded `lag_corr` explosion
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/marginal.rs:1462-1504`; `crates/rheon-core/src/marglag.rs:92-125`.
- **Derivation & live reproduction:** Re-centering via `at(C_ab) - (m_a - r_a) * side_b` omits the term $-(m_b - r_b) \cdot \frac{1}{W_R}\sum_{i \in R \setminus P} \tilde w_i (a_i - m_a)$, which is non-zero whenever any row in the window lacks a lagged partner (any missing target `y[i-lag] = None` or any `clear_lags()` gap/session break) and grows linearly with drift from row 0's origin.
  - **Reproduced live** (`N = 100`, row 0 at `0.0`, rows 1..99 i.i.d. `N(1000, 1)`, `window_size = 50, window_lags = True, lags = [1]`, one missing `y[75] = None`): **`lag_corr_yy = 30.4386`** (true `~0.01`), `lag_corr_xy = -24.1620`, collapsing `n_serial` 4.24× (`49.0 -> 11.55`).
- **Fix:** Track matched current-side first moments and matched weight $W_P$ in `MarginalLags` and its snapshots, and re-center with $C_{ab} - e_a \text{side}_b^{\text{lag}} - e_b \text{side}_a^{\text{now}} + e_a e_b (W_P / W_R)$.
- **Pass-2 fix correction:** A single matched weight $W_P$ per `(li, t)` fails when `y` has `None`s because `marglag.rs:170-175, 1060-1068` accumulates `(cxx, cyx, bx)` over $P_x = \{i \in R : \ell \le \text{depth}_i\}$ while accumulating `(cyy, cxy, by)` over $P_y = \{i \in P_x : y_{i-\ell, t}\text{ present}\}$ (live test: single `y = None` leaves `lag_corr_xx = -0.919` valid while `lag_corr_yy = -251.85`!). Either restrict all 4 lag stats for `(t, j)` to $P_y$ or track separate current-side moments for $P_x$ and $P_y$.

#### `COV-1` — `EwCovModel::refresh_pca` with default `bias = false` poisons and freezes `pca` as `NaN` + canonical basis for an entire `pca_every` interval
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/ewcov.rs:2401-2409, 2510-2535, 2976-2983`.
- **Live reproductions:** When `rows_read() == 1` and `n_eff() >= min_weight` (either a volume-weighted row 0 with `w >= min_weight`, OR a windowed `ew_cov` right after a gap `> window_size`!), `Pca::of` succeeds on the zero matrix, then `.scaled(second_moment_scale)` multiplies by `f64::NAN` and resets `rows_since_pca = 0`.
  - **Reproduced live (both triggers):** emits `pc0_var = null, pc0_share = null, pc0_loading = [0.0, 1.0]` frozen for the entire `pca_every` / `max_rows_between_pcas` interval (100 rows in Trigger A; destroys previously valid PCA after gap in Trigger B).
- **Fix:** Return early from `refresh_pca()` when `!scale.is_finite()` (without resetting `rows_since_pca` / `since_pca` so it retries on row 2).

#### `DECO-1` — `Deco::advance` violates Hard Rule 9 on zero-weight / refused rows when `x == diag.mean`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/deco.rs:691-696, 780-832`.
- **Live reproduction:** `advance` checks `if !um.is_finite() { continue; }` *before* checking `w <= 0.0`. When `x == diag.mean` (including `refused_step` on a zero-mean stream), $u_m = 0/0 = \text{NaN}$, skipping `self.rho_w[m] *= lam`. Feeding identical streams with a `w = 0` row at `x = [0, 0]` vs `x = [1, 2]` diverged by **`Delta rho = +0.175014`** (`0.421140` vs `0.246126`).
- **Fix:** Decay `self.rho_w` and return early on `if w <= 0.0` before inspecting `u`.

#### `CWIN-1` — `Product::take` prematurely wipes to `0.0` for normal decay factors `lam in (2^-1024, 2^-512)`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/window.rs:547-565`.
- **Counterexample (Python-verified):** When `self.0` is near `DOWN = 2^-512` (after 512 halvings) and a normal gap of `513` half-lives arrives (`lam = 2^-513 = 3.729e-155`, still inside a `600`-HL window), `(self.0 * UP) * lam < DOWN` triggers the wipe `self.0 = 1.0; self.1 += 4`, making `now.since(snap) == 0.0` instead of `3.729e-155` and leaking pre-window mean `50.0` (exact `0.0`) unsubtracted into the window.
- **Fix:** Add a second exact rescale `m = (self.0 * UP) * (lam * UP); if m >= DOWN { self.0 = m; self.1 += 2; }` before wiping.
- **Pass-2 test-contract warning:** `window.rs:2025-2033` (`a_running_product_scales_exactly_and_forgets_at_zero`) explicitly asserts `Product(0.75, 3).take(2^-1030)` forgets to `0.0` (`lam < f64::MIN_POSITIVE`). Guard the second rescale arm with `if lam >= f64::MIN_POSITIVE` so normal `lam in [2^-1022, 2^-512)` never wipe while preserving `window.rs:2025-2033` (or use 3 `UP` rescales and update that test intentionally).

#### `DIAG-1` — Non-PSD hybrid Bartlett variance (returning `None` on shocks) and un-demeaned H1 power ceiling in `Breaks` & `Coverage::wald` under `horizon_rows > 0`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/breaks.rs:361-367, 384-394`; `crates/rheon-core/src/calibration.rs:285-298`.
- **Derivation & counterexamples (Python-verified):** (1) Replacing empirical lag-0 $\Gamma_0$ with null variance (`S2`, `q(1-q) W2`) breaks Bochner PSD: alternating shocks `[+5, -5]` (`L = 4`) yield `f_long_run = -0.8182 <= 0` (`cusum()` and `break_wald` return `None`); `Coverage` `[hit, miss, hit]` (`q = 0.9, L = 10`) yields `var = -0.0409 < 0` (`calibration_wald` returns `None`). (2) Un-demeaned lag products `u_t u_{t-l}` inflate the denominator by $O(L_{\text{eff}} \mu^2)$ under sustained shifts, capping `Coverage::wald` at **`3.451`** (`< 3.841` 5% critical value!) under **100% miscalibration** and `Breaks::cusum` at **`1.916`** (`< 1.96`) under a **1000σ break** (`HL = 20, L = 20`).
- **Fix:** Compute Bartlett long-run variance from demeaned scores with empirical lag-0 (or `max(null_var, empirical_demeaned_bartlett)`).
- **Pass-2 caller addition (`checks.rs:509-517`):** Under `nw_lags > 0` (`embargo` / `label_clock`), `Checks::read` also passes `b.long_run_residuals()` into `TwinFit::wald`, so `DIAG-1` equally nulls/deflates **`break_wald`**!

#### `DIAG-2` — `Calibration::wald` under HAC (`horizon_rows > 0`) and `Sandwich::wald` with intercept collapse at large prediction levels
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/calibration.rs:158-161`; `crates/rheon-core/src/sandwich.rs:350-370, 404-429`.
- **Counterexample (Python-verified):** `Sandwich::covariance_in` transforms origin-shifted `V_c` back to raw coordinates `V = T V_c T'`, inflating `cond(V)` by $O((\text{level}/\text{spread})^2)$: at `level = 1e6`, `cond(V) = 5.77e23`; at `level = 1e8`, `cond(V) = inf` and `calibration_wald` fails Cholesky and returns **`None`** (exact `37.484`).
- **Fix:** Solve `Sandwich::wald` in the origin-shifted frame `V_c` (`d_c = T^{-1} d`), passing `[(m_y - m_p) - (b - 1)(m_p - origin), b - 1]` from `Calibration::wald`.

#### `DIAG-3` — Ramsey RESET & Breusch–Pagan (`Specification`): catastrophic ill-conditioning of unscaled `(q, q^2, q^3)` and unguarded `q^6` / `e^4` overflow
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/specification.rs:268-300, 377-413, 439-456`.
- **Counterexamples (Python-verified):** (1) Unscaled `[q, q^2, q^3]` fed to unjittered Cholesky hits `cond(C) = 1.42e25` after an outlier first prediction `origin = 1e5` (`ramsey_reset` returns `None` permanently) or `8.41e25` at scale `1e6` (correlation-form `cond = 28.6`); (2) `|q| > 1.34e51` overflows `q^6` in `reset`, `|e| > 1.16e77` overflows `e^4` in `het`, and `|e| > 1.34e154` overflows `e2`, permanently breaking `Specification::has_shape()`.
- **Fix:** Solve `Specification::slopes`/`r2` in correlation form and guard `(q^3)^2`, `(e^2)^2`, and `e^2` for finiteness before updating.

#### `DIAG-4` — `Tails::jarque_bera` divides by `w2 == 0.0` after `>= 538` HL, `d^4` overflow bricks reload, and `m2^2` underflows
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/tails.rs:71, 96, 125, 130, 134-138`.
- **Counterexamples (Python-verified):** (1) After a `540`-HL gap, `w2 = 0.0` while `w, m > 0`, making `jarque_bera` compute `w * w / 0.0 = inf` (or `NaN`); (2) `|d| > 1.16e77` overflows `d^4` into `m[2]`, permanently failing `Tails::has_shape()` on reload; (3) `|v| ~ 1e-90` underflows `m2 * m2 = 0.0`, producing `NaN`/`inf` kurtosis.
- **Fix:** Guard `self.w2 > 0.0` and `jb.is_finite()` in `jarque_bera()`; compute `(m3 / m2) / m2.sqrt()` and `(m4 / m2) / m2 - 3.0`; check `next_m` finiteness before mutating in `update()`.

#### `DIAG-5` — `EwQuantile::kept` bypasses the `band` memory cap on multi-level sketches when a lower level is `None` (zero)
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/stats.rs:525-550`.
- **Counterexample (Python-verified):** With `levels = [0.25, 0.75]`, `at = [None, Some(0)]`, `band = 8` (cap 1,025 buckets), a single tiny value `1e-300` (`k = -63,787`) sets `low = lo = -63,787, high = 0`, allocating **63,788 buckets (498 KB)** — and up to **130,944 buckets (> 1 MB)** with `f64::MIN_POSITIVE` and `f64::MAX`.
- **Fix:** Compute `(low, high)` over `self.at.iter().flatten()`, falling back to `(lo, lo)` only when *all* levels are `None`.

#### `REFRESH-1` — `RefreshTime::feed_limited` skips `clock_kind` validation when draining held points, silently corrupting timestamps
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/refresh.rs:651-677, 844-852`; `refresh/held.rs:246-269`.
- **Live reproductions:** Because `self.kind` is checked only inside `self.scan`'s row loop and Polars' `strict_cast` succeeds between `Datetime` and `Float64`/`Int64`, resuming a `pairs=True` state with held points on a mismatched clock kind silently corrupts `time_refresh`:
  1. `Datetime("us")` resumed on `Float64` (`.head(1)` or empty frame) outputs `time_refresh = [1704099602000000.0]` (raw epoch micros as float).
  2. `Int64` (`[1, 2, 3]`) resumed on `Datetime("us")` (`.head(1)`) outputs `time_refresh = [1970-01-01 00:00:00.000003]`.
- **Fix:** Validate `self.grouped`, `time_dtype`, `self.kind`, and `value_dtype` at the top of `feed_limited` before draining `self.held`.

#### `BANK-1` — `ModelBank.skip_learned` / `--skip-learned` crashes on `group_close = "monotone"` banks and re-emits trailing `w = 0` rows
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/bank/learned.rs:40-52, 119-122`; `bank.rs:3980-3985`; `python/rheon/polars/_bank.py:675, 4159-4214`.
- **Counterexamples:** (1) Under `group_close = "monotone"`, once group `"B"` closes group `"A"`, `"A"` is removed from `self.states` and omitted from `last_clocks()`, so `skip_learned` treats `"A"` as unseen and keeps all `"A"` rows, crashing `fit_predict` in `check_monotone` (`"A" < high_water "B"`); (2) under task 539, trailing `w = 0` rows after `last_clock` are re-emitted on overlapping resume; (3) multi-spec banks with heterogeneous `group`/`weight` columns can silently drop unlearned rows of one spec under logical-AND filtering.
- **Fix:** Include `high_water` (and `integer_keys`) in `Learned` and Python `skip_learned` to drop keys `< high_water` for monotone specs, and document or refuse heterogeneous `group`/`weight` specs.

#### `STRM-4` — `EwClass` counts null-label rows in `n_eff` and omits `target_n_eff_into`, defeating `min_weight`; `learned_clock` advances on all-null-target rows
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/ewclass.rs:1010-1011, 1047, 763-782`; `crates/rheon-polars/src/stream.rs:3092-3100, 4252-4263`.
- **Counterexamples:** (1) `ew_class` (`min_weight = 3`) fed 4 rows with valid features and `label = null` increments `n_eff` to `3.0 >= 3.0`, setting `withheld_reason = null` while `score()` emits `NaN` (or predicting 100% from 1 labeled row despite `min_weight = 3`); (2) on all 12 supervised models, all-null-target rows with `w > 0` advance `self.persisted.last_learned`, causing `emit_clocks = True` to report false `learned_clock` timestamps.
- **Fix:** Implement `EwClass::target_n_eff_into` returning `total` (or only increment `n_eff` when `label.is_some()`), and update `last_learned` for supervised models only when at least one target is usable on that row.
- **Pass-2 addition:** Under `embargo` / `label_clock`, `stream.rs:4252` also sets `last_learned = row_clock` when a row is merely queued into `pending` rather than when a usable target is actually learned.

#### `SPEC-2` — `HmmCfg::validate()` never checks `tvtp_coef` for `NaN` or `±inf`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/hmm.rs:403-413`; `crates/rheon-polars/src/stream/model_cfgs.rs:516-542` (cross-confirmed in HMM and spec reviews).
- **Mechanism:** TOML (`nan`, `inf`) and Rust `Spec` accept non-finite floats in `tvtp_coef`, bypassing validation and poisoning every softmax transition row with `NaN`.
- **Fix:** Refuse `!v.is_finite()` across `a.iter().chain(b.iter())` in `HmmCfg::validate()`.

#### `MATH-1` — Higham `corr.nearest` reverses `rel(y, xk)` and returns `0.0` on `norm(q) == 0`, stopping prematurely at `iters = 2` on negative-definite inputs
- **Location:** `python/rheon/corr.py:442-458`.
- **Live reproductions:** Higham (2002) Eq. (4.1) scales by `||Y_k||_inf >= 1`, not `||X_k||_inf`, and `rel(p, q)` returns `0.0` whenever `norm(q) == 0.0`. On $A = \begin{pmatrix}-10 & 1 \\ 1 & -10\end{pmatrix}$, `corr.nearest(A)` stops at `iters = 2` returning $I$ (`dist = 15.6205`) instead of $\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^\top$ (`dist = 15.5563`); on $3 \times 3$ negative-definite $A$, returns $I$ (`dist = 11.1580`) instead of `10.6066` (48 iters). Same for weighted `corr.nearest`.
- **Fix:** Return `float("inf")` when `d == 0.0` and `norm(p - q) > 0.0`, and call `rel(xk, y)` instead of `rel(y, xk)`.

#### `MATH-2` — `corr.epps_invert` emits bogus finite off-diagonal correlations when both Bartlett variances are negative
- **Location:** `python/rheon/corr.py:1719-1723`.
- **Live reproduction:** When `auto[i] < 0` and `auto[j] < 0`, `auto[i] * auto[j] > 0`, so `sqrt(outer(auto, auto))` is real and positive. With $L = 3, \text{auto} = [-1.6, -1.6]$, `epps_invert` returns `[[nan, 1.5625], [1.5625, nan]]`.
- **Fix:** Compute `s = np.sqrt(np.clip(auto, 0.0, None))` and mask rows/columns where `~(auto > 0.0)` to `np.nan`, matching `gram.correlation`.

#### `PY-1` — Silent signed wraparound in `eval.window_metrics` on `Int64` clocks and `pl.Duration("ms" | "us" | "ns")` clocks
- **Location:** `python/rheon/polars/eval.py:1267-1280` (expanded in Pass 2 to include `pl.Duration` at lines `1278-1280`).
- **Live reproductions (`polars 2.1.0`):**
  1. **`Int64` (`1267-1274`):** `-(2**63) < low < 0` excludes `Int64` (`low == -(2**63)`); `[-9223372036854775805, -9223372036854775804]` with `every = 10` wraps `window_start` to **`+9223372036854775806`**.
  2. **`pl.Duration` (`1278-1280`, Pass-2 catch):** `(pl.col(clock).dt.total_nanoseconds() // ns * ns)` overflows signed `i64` inside `.dt.total_nanoseconds()` past ±292.47 years on `Duration("ms")` and `Duration("us")`: `+10**13 ms` (`+316.9` years) with `every="1s"` silently wraps `window_start` to **`-97,764 days` (`-267.7` years)**! And `Duration("ns")` near `i64::MIN` wraps `// ns * ns` to `+106,751 days`.
- **Verified fix:** Bucket both `Int64` and `pl.Duration` in native physical units cast to `pl.Int128` (`pl.col(clock).to_physical().cast(pl.Int128)`), refusing out-of-range underflow below `-(2**63)` before casting back.

#### `REG-14` — `gramfit/merge.rs` (`solve_subsets`) overflows `w * wb / (total * total)` to `NaN` and fabricates `target_n_kish = 102,010` on `n_kish = NaN`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/gramfit/merge.rs:106-118, 168, 199-271` (upgraded Medium → High in Pass 2; see also `MATH-7` in the same file).
- **Live reproductions (`rheon 2.0.0` `solve_subsets`):**
  1. Unfixed `w * wb / (total * total)` (missed by task 269) returns `coef = [nan, nan], r2 = nan, se = [nan, nan]` for `weight_sum = 1e160` (`total > 1.34e154`) or `< 1.5e-154`.
  2. `q_of` maps `target_n_kish = NaN` on a weighted part (`w = 1000`) to `Q = 0.0`, fabricating **`n = 102,010.0`** (`1010^2 / 10`) and **understating `se` by 100×** when merged with a 10-row part!
- **Fix:** Use `ewcov::between_share` and share-form averages, and propagate `NaN` from `q_of` whenever `w > 0.0 && !(nk > 0.0 && nk.is_finite())`.

#### `MATH-7` — Rust `gramfit/merge.rs` (`solve_subsets`) poisons merged target & feature moments with `0.0 * NaN = NaN` on zero-weight targets/parts
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/gramfit/merge.rs:165-174, 191-215, 241-265, 316-337` (called by `rheon.gram.solve_subsets`).
- **Live reproduction (`rheon 2.0.0`):** While Pass 1 (`MATH-3`) checked Python `gram.py`, Rust `gramfit::merge`, `merge_within`, and `within_cross` perform unguarded `a * ... + b * ...` when `w == 0` or `wb == 0`. Whenever a closed-group row has `null` for an unobserved target (`gram.from_row` writes `NaN` with `target_weights[t] = 0.0`), `solve_subsets([gA, gB_nan], subsets=[[0, 1]], ridge=1e-6)` (both `within=False` and `within=True`) returns **`coef = [nan, nan], resid_var = nan, r2 = nan, se = [nan, nan]`** and collapses LARS paths to `[[nan, 0.0]]`!
- **Fix:** Branch on `(w > 0.0, wb > 0.0)` in `merge`, `merge_within`, and `within_cross`, skipping `wb <= 0.0` parts and copying `p` directly when `w <= 0.0 && wb > 0.0`.

#### `MATH-8` — `gramfit::lars_lasso_weighted` underflows/overflows `w[i] * w[j]`, silently dropping the least-penalized and heavily-penalized features as collinear (`coef = 0.0`)
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/gramfit/lars.rs:374-410, 631-639`.
- **Derivation & live reproductions (`rheon 2.0.0`):** `lars_lasso_weighted` forms $\tilde R_{ij} = R_{ij} / (w_i w_j)$. (1) For priority/unpenalized features with $w_i < 1.5 \times 10^{-154}$, `w[i]^2 = 0 -> R_ii = +inf`, and `Factor::push` (`634-638`) tests `+inf > +inf` (`false`!), marking the highest-priority feature collinear (`coef = 0.0` across the entire path: `lars_path(g, penalty_weights=[1e-160, 1.0])` returns `[[6.02, 0.0, 0.0], [6.02, 0.0, 0.0]]`!). (2) For $w_i > 1.34 \times 10^{154}$, `R_ii = 0.0` (`0 > 0 == false`), dropping features even at $\lambda = 0$ and zeroing all slopes on equal weights `[1e200, 1e200]`.
- **Fix:** Solve active KKT steps on the unit-diagonal correlation matrix $R_{AA}$ ($R_{AA} \delta_A = \tilde w_A \odot s_A$ with normalized $\tilde w_i = w_i / w_{\text{scale}}$) without forming $1 / (w_i w_j)$.

#### `LASSO-3` — `Lasso::solve_path_into` warm-start `p * s[i]` overflows to `±inf` on valid bounded streams (`<= 1e100`), triggering `LASSO-1` live in `ModelBank`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/lasso.rs:1075-1081` (affects both `cd_sweep` and post-`v2.0.0` `cd_sweep_split` at `k >= 40`).
- **Live reproduction (`rheon 2.0.0` `ModelBank`, `k = 2` and `k = 45`):** When feature `i` starts at tiny spread $s_{\text{old}} = 10^{-130}$ against $y \sim 10^{100}$ (`p = b_old / s_old ~ 1e230`, finite) and a later valid row arrives with $x_i = 10^{100}$ ($s_{\text{new}} \sim 3 \times 10^{99}$), warm-start `b[i] = p * s[i]` overflows to `+inf`, triggering `LASSO-1` (`solve_failures = [0]`, `coef = ±inf/NaN`, `pred = None` with `withheld_reason = None`).
- **Fix:** Only warm-start when `(p * s[i]).is_finite()` (and reset warm-start `b` to `0.0` if any coordinate overflows or exceeds a safe multiple of target spread).

#### `REG-18` — `quantile(standardize=True)` zeroes out active binary/sparse/regime coefficients (`0.0` vs `+20.0`), and `refit_quantile` treats ascent-direction line-search stall as convergence
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/robust.rs:2022-2034, 2125-2163`; `crates/rheon-core/src/robust/quantile_refit.rs:116-155, 198-236, 341-370`.
- **Mechanism & live reproduction (`rheon 2.0.0`):** `self.cov[j]` accumulates curvature **only** for rows inside the narrow smoothing band $|r_i| < h$. Whenever a binary, sparse, or regime-indicator feature $x_k \in \{0, 1\}$ is constant inside $B$ (e.g., Pass 1 OLS pulled by 2 outliers so all $x_k = 1$ rows sit outside $B$), `cov[j].var(k+1) == 0`, so `band_scales_into` drops feature $k$ and `solve_centred` forces `next[k+1] = 0.0` — discarding all outside-band subgradient nudges! In `refit_quantile`, this makes `Pass::Newton(h)` a strict ascent step; `damped` exhausts 40 halvings (`stalled = true`), line 234 **breaks as converged (`solve_failures = 0`) without switching to `Pass::Majorize(h)`**, and `rebuild_band_stream` overwrites $\hat\beta_k$ to `0.0`:
  - Live 60-row run (`y = 2*x1 + 20*x2` + 2 outliers): `standardize=False` gives `[0.0001, 2.0002, 19.9996]`; **`standardize=True` gives `[0.0008, 2.0019, 0.0]`**!
- **Fix:** Compute feature standardization scales from all positive-weight target rows (not just in-band $|r_i| < h$ rows), and when `Pass::Newton(h)` stalls (`damped == None`), transition to globally positive-weight `Pass::Majorize(h)` instead of breaking as converged.

#### `REG-19` — Three KKT and bound failures in `Constraint` at normal float scales inside `INPUT_BOUND` (independent of `REG-8`)
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/constraint.rs:62-72, 261-366`.
- **Live reproductions (`rheon 2.0.0`, all with finite `1 / sc^2`):**
  1. **Breakpoint `(bi - bound) / ti = ±inf` dropped (`304-319, 343`):** At `sc = [1, 1e85]`, `t_hi` overflows and is dropped by `if t.is_finite()`; when `below == false`, `at_hi` is `false`, `mu = +inf`, returning **`coef = [0.0, 0.0]` (`sum = 0.0 != 1.0`)**.
  2. **16-digit cancellation in `bi - mu * ti` (`354-364`):** At `sc = [1, 1e-85]` (`t = [1, 1e170]`), `pa(mode="pa1", c=1e300, standardize=True, fit_intercept=False, coef_max=[2, 2], coef_sum=1.0)` outputs **`coef = [-1.0, -3.6313e121]`** (`sum = -3.63e121 != 1.0`, next-row `pred = -7.26e36`).
  3. **`Scratch::fill` bound underflow (`68-71`):** `c.lo[i] * sc` underflows `coef_min = [1e-220]` at `sc = 1e-110` to **`coef_x = 0.0 < 1e-220`**.
- **Unified fix (solves `REG-8` and all 3 modes):** Project in caller units (`b_i / sc_i` during warmup so `lo`/`hi` are exact), weight metric by $w_i = (s_{\min}/\text{sc}_i)^2 \in [0, 1]$ without `MIN_POSITIVE` clamping, classify `t_hi == +inf` as `at_hi = true` and `t_lo == -inf` as `at_lo = true` for all finite $\mu$, and compute the largest-$w_i$ free coordinate from the exact sum residual `(s - sum_{i != p} b[i]).clamp(lo[p], hi[p])`.

#### `COV-3` — Hard-window `precision_scale` leak across `EwCov` & `EwClass` + `precision_scale -> 0.0` permanent `NaN` death on `ew_class("diag")`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/ewcov.rs:1383-1386`; `ewcov/diag.rs:414-417`; `ewclass.rs:706-725, 842, 887-906`.
- **Live reproductions (`rheon 2.0.0`):**
  1. **Hard-window leak:** `set_window_of` copies unwindowed whole-history `precision_scale`, and `EwClass` `Shared`/`Diagonal` call `self.ridge(c)` on unwindowed `self.classes`/`self.diag_classes`. After 990 expired rows (`window_size = 5, half_life = inf`), `ew_cov` `mahal` is **7.3× off** (`0.231` vs `0.032`) and `ew_class` `"tied"`/`"diag"` **flip predicted classes** (`'0'` → `'1'`).
  2. **Permanent `ew_class("diag")` `NaN` death:** Past ~1,075 HL on any continuous stream, `precision_scale` underflows to `0.0`; because `Covariance::Diagonal` (`889-906`) has no jitter floor, any constant/inactive feature produces `0.0 / 0.0 = NaN`, permanently outputting **`class = None, p_* = None`**!
- **Fix:** Compute windowed `precision_scale` strictly from window quantities ($q_R / w_R^2 = 1 / n_{\text{kish}, R}$), read `view` in `ewclass.rs:842, 887`, and apply a jitter floor (or $q / w^2$ scale) in `Covariance::Diagonal` so `v > 0` always.

#### `CLUST-3` — Catastrophic cancellation in `w_mean` across `>= 53`-HL weight shifts collapses `Micro::unit()` to `0.0` and corrupts `Conformal`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/cluster/micro.rs:383-389, 824, 832-833`; `crates/rheon-core/src/conformal.rs:338-339`.
- **Live reproduction (`rheon 2.0.0`):** Whenever $\lambda R_{\text{old}} \le 2^{-53}$ (`>= 53` HL, including `lam == 0`!), `self.w_rows = lam * self.w_rows + 1.0` rounds to `1.0`. After `(0, 0)` at `w = 1e16` and a 60-HL gap to `w = 1.0`, `1e16 + (1.0 - 1e16) / 1.0 == 0.0`, collapsing `Micro::unit()` to **`0.0`** — admitting `(1e6, 1e6)` (**distance `1.414e6` against `eps = 1.0`!**) into cluster `0` with `outlier = False`! `Conformal` likewise fails total-forgetting parity across `lam == 0`.
- **Fix:** Update `w_mean` in share form `let prev = lam * self.w_rows; self.w_rows = prev + 1.0; self.w_mean = (prev / self.w_rows) * self.w_mean + (1.0 / self.w_rows) * w;`, guard `self.w_mean > 0.0` in `Micro::unit()`, and reset `Conformal` when `prev == 0.0 && held == 0`.

#### `MARG-3` — `Marginal::pair` divides `wt^2 / qt` when `qt == 0.0`, emitting `n_kish = +inf` and `t_stat = ±inf` on unit-weight noise
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/marginal.rs:1304, 1443-1449, 1510-1515, 1551-1559, 2151-2152`.
- **Live reproduction (`rheon 2.0.0`):** Unlike `TargetMoments::n_kish` (`q > 0.0`), unwindowed `Marginal::pair` computes `self.wt[t] * self.wt[t] / self.qt[t]` whenever `n_eff > 0.0`. On 70 unit-weight `N(0, 1)` rows followed by one `y = None` row `538.1` HLs later, `qt` underflows to `0.0` while `wt^2 > 0`, emitting **`n_kish = inf, corr = -0.0027, t_stat = -inf`** (cascading to `n_serial`, `t_stat_serial`, and `split_gain_t`).
- **Fix:** Guard `self.qt[t] > 0.0` (returning `f64::NAN` otherwise, matching `marginal.rs:1388`) and require `n_kish.is_finite()` before forming `t_stat`, `n_serial`, and `split_gain_t`.

#### `CLI-4` — `rheon-cli` `model_key(v)` fails on 7 models when any required field is omitted, falsely blaming valid model keys as `"unknown key for spec"`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-cli/src/main.rs:254-268, 305-327`; `crates/rheon-polars/src/spec.rs:146-320`.
- **Mechanism:** When `toml::from_str::<Config>` fails, `model_key(v)` calls `v.clone().try_into::<Spec>().ok()`, which returns **`None`** whenever any of 7 models with required fields (`lasso`, `quantile`, `kmeans`, `micro`, `ew_class`, `hmm`, and post-`v2.0.0` `expectile`) omits that required field (`penalties`, `quantile`, `expectile`, etc.). `check_spec` then checks only common `SPEC_KEYS`, falsely blaming valid model-specific keys (`window_size`, `l1_ratio`, `warm_rows`) as `unknown key "..." for spec` or falling back to raw untagged-enum serde errors.
- **Fix:** Read `"type"` directly from the TOML table (`v.get("model").and_then(|m| m.get("type")).or_else(|| v.get("type"))`) instead of round-tripping through `try_into::<Spec>()`, and report missing required model keys explicitly.

#### `WIN-8` — Subnormal quantization drift (convex-hull violation `33.0` on `[42, 50]`) and premature `null` wipeout in `ewm_mean`/`ewm_var`/`ewm_std` across null rows and `closed="left"`/`"none"` gaps
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/windows.rs:661-670, 1183-1190, 3212-3222`; `windows/plain.rs:208-216, 268-277`.
- **Derivation & live reproductions (`rheon 2.0.0`):** Discounting unnormalized `(s, w, q, e)` on null rows (and before `closed="left"`/`"none"` reads) multiplies numerator and denominator by a common factor $f$ that cancels mathematically, yet underflows in IEEE-754:
  1. On `t = 0..1080, x = [42.0, 50.0, None, ..., None], half_life = 1.0`: `ewm_var(bias=False)` collapses to **`None` at row 538** (`2*a.e == 0 >= 0`); subnormal quantization drives **`ewm_mean = 33.0` at row 1076** (**13 units below $\min(x) = 42$!**); and all three collapse to **`None` at row 1079** (violating `WINDOWS.md:61`).
