  2. Even on **100% non-null** `t = [0, 1, 540, 1080], x = [42, 50, 60, 70], closed = "left"`, `ewm_var` collapses to `None` at `t = 540` (and `ewm_mean` at `t = 1080` on `[0, 1, 1080]`).
- **Fix:** Anchor `Acc` at `last_valued[o]` without discounting across null rows or before `closed="left"`/`"none"` reads (`Stat::Mean`/`Var`/`Std`), and store mean $\mu$ directly + normalized $r = 2e/w^2$ with `RENORM` epoch tracking.

#### `WIN-9` — `Float64` clock `elapsed(now, origin)` overflows to `+inf` across valid uncapped steps, poisoning all window operators to `null` and saving an unloadable `"damaged"` state file
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/windows.rs:1707-1715, 2634`; `windows/plain.rs:326-331, 618`; `window_quantile.rs:377`.
- **Live reproduction (`rheon 2.0.0`):** On `t = [-1e308, 0.0, 1e308, 1e308 + 1.0], x = [10, 20, 30, 40], half_life = 10.0, gap_cap = 1e308`, each step is `<= 1e308`, yet `elapsed(1e308, -1e308) = +inf` sets `g.tau = +inf`, turning every operator (`ewm_mean`, `ewm_sum`, `ewm_quantile`, formula targets) permanently `None` (`+inf - +inf = NaN`) and writing `Sketch.anchor = +inf` into `save_state`, which `load_state` immediately rejects with `ValueError: with_windows: the state is damaged`!
- **Fix:** Rebase `g.origin` (and group `tau`/`Queue`/`Sketch` offsets) whenever `!(now - origin).is_finite()` or `tau > 1e300`.

#### `WIN-10` — `Acc::held` overflows `s = m * x` to `±inf` on usable inputs (`|x| <= 1e100`), emitting literal `±inf` into output Polars DataFrames
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/src/windows.rs:651-657, 3207-3224`; `windows_frame.rs:1931`.
- **Live reproduction (`rheon 2.0.0`):** For any `half_life` in `(1.246e208, 1.246e308)` (100 orders of magnitude below `WIN-4`), `m_0 = h / LN_2` is finite while `s = m_0 * x` overflows to `±inf` for valid `|x| <= 1e100` (e.g., `x = [1e100, 1e100], half_life = 1e210` or `x = [10, 20], half_life = 1e308`), and `windows_frame.rs:1931` (`!v.is_nan()`) writes **literal `+inf` / `-inf`** into output DataFrame columns!
- **Fix:** Store mean $\mu$ directly in `Acc::s` for `Stat::Mean` (`Acc::held(x, m)` sets `s = x`, matching `Acc::merge`'s `var == true` representation) and filter kernel outputs with `v.is_finite().then_some(*v)`.

---

### Medium (Pre-Existing)

- **`SOLVE-1`** (`solve.rs:115-119, 165-172, 213-220, 253-261`): On valid SPD `diag(1e308, 1e308)`, `trace` overflows to `+inf`, making Rung 0 jitter `(+inf) * 0.0 = NaN` and causing `SpdFactor::of`/`solve_spd` to fail. *Fix:* Return `0.0` explicitly when `eps == 0.0`, and compute mean diagonal as `sum(diag / k)` when `!trace.is_finite()`.

- **`BOCPD-2`** (`bocpd.rs:908-919, 1603`): `ln_sum_prod` checks only the final 8-element block product; on mixed-scale `psins = [1e-160, 7.4e-164, 1e160, 1e164]` (inside `INPUT_BOUND`), the intermediate subnormal loses 52 bits (`0.404` nats error, breaking feature-order invariance). *Fix:* Require each block element in `[1e-35, 1e35]` before using `block.product().ln()`.

- **`BOCPD-3`** (`bocpd.rs:1027-1033, 1649-1660`): `Branches::over` rejects `d / tau == +inf` (e.g., `d = 1e100, tau = 1e-250`) as `None` (`solve_failures += 1`) instead of returning the exact limit `Branches { ln_break: 0.0, ln_grow: f64::NEG_INFINITY }`.

- **`REG-12`** (`warmup.rs:118-124`, `sgd.rs:625-637, 2589`, `rls.rs:660, 769`, `ewcov.rs:639, 802`): Raw `w * w` underflows to `0.0` for normal `w < 1.5e-154` (e.g. `1e-200`), trapping `Sgd(Quantile/EpsilonInsensitive)` permanently at the sample mean (`0.994` vs `0.9`-quantile `2.303`), disabling `Warmup` switching, and making `n_kish()` return `None`. *Fix:* Maintain a power-of-two weight scale in `Warmup`, `Level`, `Rls::s2`, and `EwCov::q_sum`.

- **`REG-13`** (`ewridge.rs:1798-1820, 1975-2003, 2812-2901`, live-reproduced): Under `TargetGaps::Pairwise`, `coef_variance`, `error_inflation_into`, and `row_error_inflation_into` use all-row Kish size `n_all` and `cov.mean` instead of target `j`'s own `n_j` and `cross.mj[j]` (up to `10×` slope SE and `1000×` intercept SE underestimate). *Verified zero-schema-change fix (Pass-2 correction):* `self.acc.tm: TargetMoments` (`ewcov.rs:1454-1505`, `AccSnap.tm`) **already** tracks per-target `q` both live and windowed — read `view.tm.n_kish(view.wj)` and `cross.mj[j]` with zero state-layout change (and note the homogeneous-$C_j$ assumption in docs).

- **`CLUST-1`** (`cluster/micro.rs:665-688`): Integer `.ceil()` on continuous-time $T_p$ breaks clock-unit invariance and collapses $\xi(\infty)$ from `72.0` to `2.0` (`36×`) at `h = 1s, beta_mu = 72` (and to `1.0` at `h = 0.01s`). *Fix:* Drop `.ceil()`, simplifying $\xi(a) = \beta\mu - (\beta\mu - 1) 2^{-a/h}$.

- **`CLUST-2`** (`cluster/kmeans.rs:882-887, 940-954`): `split(jd, jd)` halves already-dead `clusters[jd].n` and discards `far[jd].n`, rebirthing the cluster dead and unstable (`n = 0.5` instead of `25.0`). *Fix:* Use `self.far[source].n` when `target == source`.

- **`MARG-2`** (`marginal.rs:1048-1057, 2970-3086`, Pass-2 corrected): `Marginal::restore` omits `x_runs`/`y_runs` validation, `s.rows` length check on `MarginalMoments`, and turning `Runs` off when `window.is_none()`, silently regressing C4 held-constant window variance zeroing *(Pass-2 note: `Runs` safely checks slice lengths and returns `None` without panicking)*.

- **`CWIN-2`** (`window_moments.rs:250-259` **and** `corrchange.rs:975, 1181, 1293, 1338, 1646`, Pass-2 expanded & live-reproduced): All six sites compute `(aa * bb).sqrt()`, collapsing `stat` and `crit` across all 3 `corrchange` kinds (`window`, `monitor`, `sequential`) to `0.0` at `|x| ~ 1e80` and `None` at `|x| ~ 1e-85`. *(Danger: fixing `window_moments.rs` without `corrchange.rs:1646` (`permutation_crit`) yields `crit = 0.0` against finite `stat > 0`, causing 100% false alarms!)* *Fix:* Use `(cab / aa.sqrt() / bb.sqrt()).clamp(-1.0, 1.0)` at all 6 sites (plus `audit.rs:418`).

- **`DIAG-6`** (`conformal.rs:180-181, 241-255, 330, 437-438`): `1.0 - 0.5 * alpha` rounds to `1.0` at valid `coverage = 1.0f64.next_down()`, setting `z = NaN, gain = NaN` (permanently bricking the tracker); `update_against` accepts `w = NaN`/`+inf` (collapsing radius to `0.0`). *Fix:* Use `-norm_ppf(0.5 * alpha)` and guard `w > 0.0 && w.is_finite()`.

- **`DIAG-7`** (`specification.rs:253-262, 321-340`): `ljung_box` uses raw uncentred `e2 - w * mean^2`, producing negative denominator (`-768`) and `NaN` at residual bias `1e8`. *Fix:* Use compensated Welford variance and centred lag cross-covariances.

- **`DIAG-8`** (`breaks.rs:268-318, 1202-1242`): `Breaks::update` computes `z` pre-age across `lam == 0` full wipes, and neither `Breaks::update` nor `TwinFit::update` guards `z^4` / `w^2 z_i z_j` overflow (bricking `has_shape()` on reload).

- **`DIAG-9`** (`stats.rs:918-935`): `EwAutoCorr::update_weighted` uses single-variable Welford `a` factor on the lagged leg, imposing a systematic multiplicative bias `lam` (`-0.5` instead of `-1.0` on `(-1)^t` at `lam = 0.5`; `0.444` vs `0.555` on AR(1) `0.8` at `lam = 0.8`). *Fix:* Track lagged-leg EW mean or document the $O(1 - \lambda)$ shrinkage bias.

- **`REFRESH-2`** (`refresh.rs:564-584, 863`; `stream.py:727-839`, cross-confirmed in IO and Python reviews): `RefreshTime::load_bytes` omits `last_time` finiteness / clock-kind validation and `held.row` checks (allowing `NaN` to disable backwards checks on legacy v1/v2 states); `rp.stream.refresh_time` omits `is_pure=save_path is None`, `_explain_named`, `_warn_if_order_unspecified`, and `ConsumedSourceWarning`.

- **`BANK-2`** (`bank/learned.rs:144-166`; `python/rheon/polars/_bank.py:4214`, cross-confirmed in bank and Python reviews, reproduced live): Both Rust and Python `skip_learned` silently drop corrupt `-inf` clock rows instead of keeping them for `fit_predict` to refuse. *Fix:* Keep `!now.is_finite()`.

- **`BANK-4`** (`bank.rs:3300-3318`, `arrow.rs:569-578, 953-968, 1287-1301`, `runner.rs:696-723`): `holds_no_value` returns `true` whenever `height == 0`, causing `clock_forms` to skip clock-dtype conflict checks on typed empty chunks and defeating `rheon --dry-run`. *Verified fix (Pass-2 corrected):* `chunk_from_frame_rows` currently maps both `DataType::Null` and `DataType::Float64` to `ArrowCol::F64` (`clock_dtype = None`); record `DataType::Null` explicitly on `ArrowChunk` so `holds_no_value` exempts 0-row columns *only* when their source dtype was `DataType::Null`.

- **`BANK-6`** (`bank.rs:2511-2548`): `drop_groups` does not release `self.refit_reserved[si]`, permanently leaking `refit_budget` when streaming callers evict keys. *Fix:* Subtract `stream.refit_reservation()` for dropped streams where `!stream.refit_over_budget()`.

- **`BANK-8`** (`bank/refusals.rs:30-34, 190-194`, `bank.rs:1734-1738`): `over_state_cap`, `over_budget_message`, and `say_notices` print `""` for `GroupKey(None)` instead of `" group null"` (inconsistent with `reserve_refits`).

- **`BANK-9`** (`bank/audit.rs:248-255`, `bank/refusals.rs:142, 253-270, 333-343`): Casting `(v * 1e9).round() as i64` silently saturates temporal differences past 292.47 years (e.g., 400-year `Datetime("ns")` gaps/step-backs print as 292.47 years). *Fix:* Widen `span::format_duration` to `i128` and guard overflow in `audit.rs:250`.

- **`CLI-2`** (`runner.rs:1997-2046`): `temporal_text` recurses into `Struct` but omits `List` and `Array` (unlike `without_categorical`), breaking CLI NDJSON export on pass-through `List(Datetime(_, Some(tz)))` columns.

- **`CLI-3`** (`runner.rs:1333-1379`, Pass-2 fix corrected): `LastReasons::note` reads the group's final row even if skipped (`withheld_reason = null`), erasing withheld status from the CLI closing summary. *Verified fix:* Once `STRM-3` restricts `remember_last` to `w > 0` rows, delete `LastReasons` and read `bank.last_row()` once at end of run *(do not filter on `!weight_sum.is_null()`, which still breaks on trailing `w = 0` `Weightless::Fresh` rows where `weight_sum == 0.0`)*.

- **`WIN-4`** (`windows.rs:483-497`, `windows/held_mass.rs:41-47`, live-reproduced, Pass-2 fix corrected): Finite `half_life > f64::MAX * LN_2` (`~1.246e308`) overflows `self.h / LN_2` to `+inf` — both in `mass_finite` (`495`) / `held_mass` (`44`) **and on Row 0 in `Kernel::mass(+inf)` (`windows.rs:485`: `return self.h / LN_2;`)**, collapsing `ewm_mean`/`var`/`std` to `null` and `ewm_rate` to `0.0`. *Verified fix:* Use `t * (-(-x).exp_m1() / x)` for finite spans, and normalize `Mean`/`Var`/`Std` masses by `h / LN_2` (so `mass(+inf) = 1.0`, `mass_finite(t) = -(-x).exp_m1() in [0, 1]`, valid by scale invariance).

- **`WIN-5`** (`windows.rs:763-765, 3216-3222`, live-reproduced, Pass-2 expanded): Unbiased `ewm_var`/`ewm_std` (`bias=False`) overflows `a.w * a.w / (2.0 * a.e)` whenever `a.w > 1.34e154` — including **unbounded** runs for any `half_life in (9.29e153, 1.246e308)` on ordinary `t=[0, 1, 2], x=[1, 2, 3]` — emitting **literal `+inf`** into Polars DataFrames when `a.e` is finite (and `null` when `a.e` also overflows). *Fix:* Maintain normalized $r = 2e / w^2 \in [0, 1]$ (invariant under `Acc::scaled`).

- **`WIN-7`** (`window_quantile.rs:230-234, 372-381, 468-473`, Pass-2 fix corrected): `QSide::has_shape` never validates `prev` triples (`NaN`/negative/`inf`), `Sketch::has_shape` permits `total > 0.0 && anchor.is_nan()`, and `Arm::clear` retains peak bucket capacity (~480 KB/arm). *Fix note:* Enforce `!(self.total > 0.0 && self.anchor.is_nan())` — **not** `(total == 0.0) == anchor.is_nan()`, because `QSide::turn_over` on null windows and `QSide::pop` validly leave `total == 0.0` with a finite `anchor`.

- **`PYO3-1`** (`crates/rheon-py/src/lib.rs:680-683`, reproduced live): `PyModelBank::threads` takes `&self` and raises raw PyO3 `RuntimeError: Already mutably borrowed` on concurrent access instead of `busy("threads")`.

- **`MATH-4`** (`python/rheon/gram.py:3159-3199`, live-reproduced): `gram.coef_stats` omits intercept squared bias `(\beta_0 + \mu_{x,t}^\top b - \bar y_t)^2` in `resid_var`, reporting `r2 = 1.0, sigma2 = 0.0` when `coef = [100, 2]` on `y = 2x` *(Pass-2 fix note: read `means_by_target[t]` when present, falling back to `means`)*.

- **`MATH-5`** (`python/rheon/corr.py:1577-1610`, live-reproduced, Pass-2 expanded): `corr.loss(..., kind="z_mse")` accepts `bool` and `n <= 3` (`-0.302` at `n = 2`); `kind="qlike"` checks only `slogdet(m)[0] > 0` (`rc.loss(-I2, I2, kind="qlike") == -4.0`, beating true `I2`!); and `kind="minvar"` (`1600-1610`, Pass-2 catch) also skips PD checks on `f`/PSD on `r` and leaves `mu` shape unvalidated.

- **`PY-5`** (`python/rheon/_errors.py:121, 149` vs docstrings across `corr.py`, `gram.py`, `ops.py`, `_bank.py`, `_frame.py`, `stream.py`, `eval.py`, `lib.rs`): `WrongTypeError(TypeError)` and `RefusedValueError(ValueError)` are single-inheritance, contradicting docstrings across the package claiming `"It is both a TypeError and a ValueError"`.

- **`PY-6`** (`python/rheon/polars/eval.py:1230, 2651-2691, 4277-4296, 4649, 4849`, reproduced live): `diebold_mariano` and `clark_west` omit `_check_min_samples` (`min_samples=-5`/`True`/`None`/`"30"` misbehave/crash); `_check_half_life` and `window_metrics(..., every=...)` accept `bool` (`True == 1`) and `every=None` crashes with raw `TypeError`; `seqtest(df, targets="ret")` iterates characters `'r','e','t'`.

- **`PY-7`** (`python/rheon/polars/stream.py:448-474`, reproduced live): `stream.embargo` crashes with `ColumnNotFoundError` if input already has `"__embargo_key"` or `"__embargo_order"`, and computes `weight * 0.0 = NaN` on predict copies when `weight` has `inf`.

- **`BOCPD-5`** (`bocpd.rs:1467-1470, 1562-1571, 1592-1605`, live-reproduced): `psin * (kn + 1.0) / (nun * kn)` overflows before `/ kn` and `var == +inf` passes `var <= 0.0`, fabricating recurring changepoints on constant `x = 0` (`run_mode = 4` vs `11` at `covariance_prior = [2e307]`) and breaking `Robust` (`solve_failures = [5]`); `k0 * run.n / kn * dm^2` also overflows to `NaN` at `x == mu0`. *Fix:* Compute `(psin / nun) * (1.0 + 1.0 / kn)` and `(k0 / kn) * (run.n * dm) * dm`, and require `var.is_finite()`.

- **`KAL-7`** (`kalman.rs:2150-2158, 2244, 2502`): Unlike `coef_variance_of` (`2296`), `current_diagonal` and `prior_quad` omit `solve::clamp_rounding`, allowing cancellation when `remap` is refused (`|c_1| > 1024`) to yield `out[0] = -16384.0` and `NaN` in `error_inflation`. *Fix:* Wrap `out[0]` (`2244`) and `quad` (`2159`) in `crate::solve::clamp_rounding`.

- **`REG-20`** (`sgd.rs:665-682, 2639-2680`, live-reproduced): `StepGain::learn` returns early on `if q == 0.0`, failing to decay `(q, q2)` on zero-curvature teaching rows (`Logistic` saturation $p(1-p) = 0$ or outside-band `Huber` $c = 0$ with `l2 = 0`) and locking `sgd(loss="logistic", schedule="inv_scaling", learning_rate=80)` at `withheld_reason = "unstable_rate"` across 1,000 separable rows even after `coef` is 100% accurate. *Fix:* Decay `(q, q2)` on any teaching row with `||z||^2 > 0` even when `q == 0.0` (while keeping `resting` probes asleep on `q == 0.0`).

- **`AUDIT-3`** (`audit.rs:245-248, 340-354, 416-442`, live-reproduced): `CoMoments::unit_root_t` divides `ss / ((n - 2) * saa)` before `.sqrt()`, overflowing `se = +inf` and emitting `unit_root_t = -0.0` instead of `-0.57735` on `[0.0, 1e-155, 0.0, 1.0]`; `Moments::scale_for` scales only `e > 64` (never `e < -64`), corrupting `kurtosis` (`-2.4` at `1e-81`, `None` at `1e-85`), `skew`, and `autocorr`. *Fix:* Compute `(ss / (n - 2.0)).sqrt() / self.saa.sqrt()` and scale symmetrically for `e.abs() > 64`.

- **`DIAG-12`** (`stats.rs:1081`, `influence.rs:54-69`, live-reproduced): Unchecked `w * e * e` in `SlotMetrics::update_with` and `w * v * v` in `Influence::update` overflow to `+inf` on valid `<= 1e100` inputs, killing `r2` (`None`) and permanently failing `ModelBank.load_bytes` via `has_shape()`; `Influence::of` also overflows `infl * infl` above `1.34e154`. *Fix:* Guard finiteness before updating (aging-only on overflow) and rewrite `Influence::of` via `r = 1.0 / infl`.

- **`COV-4`** (`ewcov.rs:668-674, 2407`): When `q_sum` underflows (`w < 1.5e-154` or after a `538`-HL `w = 0` decay step in core), `unbiased_factor()` returns `NaN`, turning all scale-dependent `ew_cov(bias=False)` outputs (`var`, `std`, `cov`, `mahal`, `pc*_var`) to `NaN` while `bias=True` works. *Fix:* Track dimensionless $\rho_Q = Q / W^2 = a^2 \rho_Q + b^2 \in (0, 1]$ (which solves both `COV-4` and `REG-12`).

- **`BANK-11`** (`bank.rs:1676-1680, 3651-3655, 3768-3785`, live-reproduced): When `self.states[si]` is empty (`needs_snapshot == false`) and a chunk is refused after `resolve_targets` (e.g., `window_budget = {"refuse": ...}`), `Bank::forget` removes `fresh` groups from `self.states` without removing them from `self.resolvers[si].runs`/`saved`, bloating `save_bytes()` (`3,316` vs `1,376` B live). *Fix:* Remove `(si, key)` from `self.resolvers` inside `forget`.

- **`BANK-12`** (`arrow.rs:111-121, 584-602`, `resolvers.rs:148-156`): `ArrowChunk::from_record_batch` wraps `Float32`/integer arrays in `ArrowCol::Num`, which `ArrowChunk::series` maps to `None` and `ArrowCol::is(Form::Number)` maps to `false` — causing `Bank::fit_predict_arrow` with formula targets reading `Float32`/int columns to fail with self-contradictory `formula target column "p" is not in the DataFrame (columns: [..., "p", ...])`. *Fix:* Handle `ArrowCol::Num` in `series`, `is(Form::Number)`, and `f64`.

- **`CLI-5`** (`runner.rs:58-64, 944-952`, `atomic.rs:84-96, 179-185` — *replaces Pass-1 false positive `CLI-1`*): Non-existent paths with a trailing `/` (`-o /tmp/results/`) have `path.is_dir() == false` and `Path::file_name()` strips `/`, so `--dry-run`, `check_writable`, and `AtomicFile::new` succeed and process the entire dataset before `fs::rename` fails with `ENOTDIR` at commit; `run_config_on_reported` (`944, 950`) also calls stat-only `check_parent` rather than `check_writable` on `output` and `closed_groups`. *Fix:* Reject trailing directory separators in `check_parent`/`temp_beside` and call `check_writable` on `output` and `closed_groups`.

- **`WIN-11`** (`windows_frame.rs:384-410, 3034-3042`, `windows.rs:1388-1396, 2169-2275`, live-reproduced): `WindowsRun::load_bytes` performs zero validation on `file.increments` (`IncrState`), causing an unchecked OOB Rust `PanicException` in `step_increments` on supported unsealed v9/v10 states; `Windows::check()` also omits finiteness/monotonicity/`ClockKind` checks on `g.tau`, `Acc`, `Side.tau`, and `stream_clock`. *Fix:* Validate `IncrState` slice lengths/finiteness/unique keys in `load_bytes` and `g.tau`/`Acc`/`ClockKind` in `Windows::check()`.

- **`PY-14`** (`python/rheon/polars/_check.py:813-824`, live-reproduced): Under `target_gaps="pairwise"`, `_gram_findings` divides per-target `cross_centered[t][j]` by all-row `sqrt(comoments[j][j] * target_vars[t])` (even ignoring `comoments_by_target` when present!) and clamps `min(abs(corr), 1.0)` — producing both false-positive `leakage = 1.000000` warnings when true correlation is `0.6` and false negatives missing exact `y == x` leakage. *Fix:* Use `comoments_by_target[t][j][j]` when present and per-target feature variance (never clamping an invalid `> 1.0` ratio to `1.0`).

- **`PY-15`** (`eval.py:884, 1908-1912, 2637-2730`, `corr.py:551-575`, `gram.py:187, 1732`, `sim.py:345-435`, live-reproduced): (1) `eval.grid_search` (`_slot_sums`) includes `"selected"` and `"averaged"` slots in `common`, altering candidate `n` and rankings whenever `emit_selected`/`emit_averaged` is on; (2) `eval.seqtest` leaves `min_weight` unvalidated (`nan` nulls all outputs, `-5`/`True` accepted); (3) `eval.{metrics,window_metrics,compare_specs,sums,from_sums}` treat `binary="false"` as `True`; (4) `corr.shrink(R, x=x_nan)` blames `alpha`; (5) `gram.{lasso_path,lars_path,solve_subsets}` omit `Mapping`/`_check_shapes`; (6) `sim.regimes` accepts `noise < 0`, `NaN`, and float `durations`.

---

### Low (Pre-Existing)

- **`WIN-3`** (`docs/WINDOWS.md:155-159`, `python/rheon/polars/ops.py:115-120`, downgraded High → Low in Pass 2): Because forward `ewm_mean(..., reverse=True)` holds row $j$ over $[t_j, t_{j+1})$ and task 430 sets `open_on_edge` mass to `0.0` at $t_j = t + w$, on a regular grid with 1-bar `window_size == step`, default `closed="right"` returns 100% `null` (and at `3 * step` averages only `[t+step, t+2*step)`). Document this alongside backward `"left"`/`"both"` and point to `ewm_sum("x", ...) / ewm_sum(pl.lit(1.0), ...)` for point-weighted bar means. *(Pass-2 correction: Pass 1's second claim about `Windows::finish()` at EOF was a **false positive** — leaving far-edge `"right"`/`"both"` windows without a stamp past `t + w` unresolved at EOF is intentional and pinned by `task 160 PC5` at `windows.rs:4323, 5832` and `stream.py:1508`.)*)

- **`MATH-3`** (`python/rheon/gram.py:715-758, 870-879`, downgraded High → Low & corrected in Pass 2): *Pass-2 correction:* `TargetMoments::new` (`ewcov.rs:1475`) initializes `mean` and `var` to `0.0` (not `NaN`), so `ModelBank.gram()` and `gram.from_row()` are **unaffected** (contrast Rust `solve_subsets` in `MATH-7`, where `from_row` on `closed_groups` null columns *does* produce `NaN`). For external `Gram` dicts holding `NaN` on zero-weight targets/parts, guard `tmean`, `tvar`, **and `cc`/`ybar` at `gram.py:743, 749-752` under `fit_intercept=False`** (missed by Pass 1, live-reproduced poisoning `cross_centered` to `NaN`) with `both = (tw > 0) & (twb > 0)`.)

- **`WIN-6`** (`stream.py:1792-1804`, `docs/WINDOWS.md:326-332`, downgraded Medium → Low in Pass 2): Backward `with_windows(closed="right"/"both", save_state=...)` skips `w.finish()`, withholding the final timestamp's rows while task 158 (E13) refuses resuming at that timestamp, and `WINDOWS.md:326-332` retains pre-E13 text *(note: calling `with_windows(df.clear(), ..., load_state=path)` with `save_state=None` drains `w.finish()` today and should be documented)*.

- **`PY-10`** (`python/rheon/polars/sim.py:107, 148, 463-466`, downgraded Medium → Low in Pass 2 since `sim.py` is `@unstable`, live-reproduced): `sim.regimes` accepts boundary-PSD matrices down to `min_eig >= -1e-10` then crashes in `cholesky(r + 1e-12 * I)` when `min_eig in [-1e-10, -1e-12)`, and `states=[]` crashes with `IndexError`.

- **`REG-17`** (`robust.rs:1910-1917, 1950-1960`): `replay_rows` centers quantile nudges via `(p - total * cov.mean(i2)) / wj`, losing digits on offset features (`x = 1e14 + N(0,1)`: abs err `0.547`). *Fix:* Shift by first nudged row's features.

- **`COV-2`** (`ewcov.rs:528-555`, `ewcov/diag.rs:94-120`, Pass-2 expanded): Both `EwCov::from_moments` and `EwCovDiag::from_moments` leave `q_sum = Some(0.0)` alongside `w_sum > 0.0`, inflating `n_kish()` to `10,201` after one unit update. *Fix:* Set `ew.q_sum = (w_sum == 0.0).then_some(0.0)` in both.

- **`CWIN-3`** (`window.rs:1831-1864`): `truncated_mean`/`truncated_scalar` use uncentred `(w_now * m - f * w_old * m_old) / w` (loses ~10 digits when `w / w_now ~ 1e-10` and overflows at `m = 1e305`). *Fix:* Use centered `m - (f * w_old / w) * (m_old - m)`.

- **`DIAG-10`** (`breaks.rs:1270`, `clock.rs:1020-1027`, `held_median.rs:235-241`, `stream.rs:2216-2221`): (1) `TwinFit::wald` checks `idx.len() >= KEPT_ORDER` instead of `idx.len() + usize::from(intercept)`; (2) `ClockState::advance_scoring` mutates `prev_clock` backward on late rows for direct Rust callers (Polars clones per row); (3) `HeldMedian::Tree::rescale` can leave phantom subnormal `subtree.sum` when leaves underflow to `0.0`; (4) `Conformal` has no `has_shape()` load check in `stream.rs:2216`.

- **`MATH-6`** (`corr.py:886, 1032, 1267, 1337, 1388`, `gram.py:1215, 2543`, reproduced live): (1) `mp_edge`/`mp_density` accept `NaN`/`inf`; (2) `absorption`/`spectral` pass `m` unsymmetrized to `eigh`; (3) `from_blocks` `nanargmax` reports nonsensical diagonal mismatch on `NaN`; (4) `gram.subset` leaves stale `cross_moments` width when `targets == []`; (5) `gram.solve_subsets` accepts `bool`/`float` indices.

- **`PYO3-3`** (`crates/rheon-py/src/lib.rs:628-950, 1051-1345, 1669`, `gramfit.rs:178, 243`, Pass-2 refined): Pure-Rust state serialization/deserialization and file I/O hold the GIL instead of running inside `py.detach` (before constructing `PyDataFrame`/`PyByteArray` under the GIL); direct `_rheon.gram_*` calls have unchecked `k * k` in `gramfit.rs:243` and raw `BufferError` on non-native-endian (`>f8`) buffers at `gramfit.rs:178`.

- **`PY-12`** (`eval.py:2282-2284`, `_spec_robust.py:132`, `_spec_gradient.py:116`, Pass-2 refined): (1) `grid_search` blames an innocent candidate in `late` when `emit_selected`/`emit_averaged` caused 0 common rows (root cause in `eval.py:1908-1912` — see `PY-15`); (2) `huber` and `sgd` docstrings for `huber_refit_span` carry copy-paste text `"(three a coefficient under the quantile loss)"`.

- **`COV-5`** (`deco.rs:459-462`, `corrchange.rs:1405-1411`, `humanfloat.rs:118, 170, 223, 280`, `rcov.rs:943-945`): (1) `Deco::block_sums_into` and `CorrChange::scalarise` overflow `s1^2` and `s2` to `+inf` (`NaN`) on variance jumps (`1e-100 -> 1e80`, live-reproduced); (2) 4 serde visitors in `humanfloat.rs` use uncapped `Vec::with_capacity(size_hint)` (32 GiB OOM on crafted/legacy states); (3) `Rcov(kind="kernel", jitter=m >= 2)` emits `lead()` at return `m` rather than holding until `2m`, breaking time-reversal symmetry across short stretches.

- **`SPEC-11`** (`crates/rheon-polars/src/targets.rs:319-321`): Formula-target operator refusal lists `ewm_mean, ewm_std, ewm_var, ewm_cov, ewm_corr or ewm_sum`, omitting `ewm_quantile` (supported via `rewm_quantile` in `formula.rs` since task 266; note `ewm_cov`/`ewm_corr` are not in `ops.py`).

## 5. Test, Oracle, CI & Documentation Findings

*(Note: the missing `ModelKind::Glm` handling in `stream.rs::emit_metrics` and `rp.eval` — also uncovered by the test/CI audit because no test runs `emit_metrics = True` on GLM — is consolidated under `GLM-METRICS` in Section 3 to avoid double counting.)*

### Critical (Tests, Oracles, CI & Docs)

#### `CI-1` — Heavy post-v2.0.0 oracle grids use `@pytest.mark.extended` instead of `@pytest.mark.oracle_grid`, breaking Intel SDE CPU-paths budget and bypassing `oracles.toml`
- **Location:** `tests/test_second_opinion_garch.py:269-271, 287-289, 450-452, 535-537, 615-617`; `tests/test_second_opinion_glm.py:244-246`; `tests/test_second_opinion_expectile.py:209-211, 226-228`; `.github/workflows/cpu-paths.yml:192, 293-303`; `tests/oracle_marks.py:27-37`; `tests/test_oracle_ledger.py:227-236`; `oracles.toml`.
- **Mechanism & impact:**
  1. Because all three modules import `scipy`/`statsmodels`, `tests/oracle_marks.py::is_oracle_module()` automatically applies `@pytest.mark.oracle` to every test in them. `.github/workflows/cpu-paths.yml:293-303` runs `pytest -m "oracle and not soak and not oracle_grid"` under **Intel SDE `-hsw` emulation** (10–100× slower, 120-minute timeout) and does **not** exclude `extended`. Without `@pytest.mark.oracle_grid`, those 8 tests execute ~9,900 `arch` fits (including 700 on 100k rows) and 60 `glum` fits (up to 100k rows) inside Intel SDE.
  2. `tests/test_oracle_ledger.py:227-236` scans only `@pytest.mark.oracle_grid`, so all 8 grids bypass `oracles.toml` / `oracle_hashes.toml`, leaving wide hardcoded tolerances unratcheted (`within >= 46` vs 50, `ratio <= 1.5`, `max <= 4.0`) and `arch`/`glum` oracle versions unpinned by `scripts/oracles.py`.
- **Fix:** Replace `@pytest.mark.extended` with `@pytest.mark.oracle_grid` on all 8 grid tests (or also exclude `not extended` in `cpu-paths.yml` as an immediate guard), register their groups in `oracles.toml`, and pin `arch`/`glum` in `oracle_hashes.toml`.
- **Pass-2 CI impact expansion:** Beyond `cpu-paths.yml` Intel SDE `-hsw` and `oracles.toml` bypass, because `pyproject.toml` excludes `oracle_grid` by default (`-m "not soak and not oracle_grid"`) everywhere except `oracles.yml`, marking these 8 heavy grids `extended` instead of `oracle_grid` **also** executes all ~9,900 `arch` fits and 60 `glum` fits across all 9 matrix legs of `ci.yml` (including 4 slow Windows legs), 3 compatibility legs of `release.yml`, all 3 legs of `polars-canary.yml`, and every local `scripts/gate.sh --extended` run.

---

### High (Tests, Oracles, CI & Docs)

#### `CI-2` — `scripts/release_probe.py` feeds `garch` degenerate linear data (100% null output), omits `garch`/`hawkes` from `CLOCKED`, and `PATHS` omits post-v2.0.0 feature paths
- **Location:** `scripts/release_probe.py:403-411, 618-622`; `tests/test_release_compare.py:257-292` (compare `tests/test_budgets_memory.py:90-100, 363-377`).
- **Details:** (1) `release_probe.py` feeds `garch` the shared homoskedastic linear target `y = 0.5 + x @ [1, -0.5, 0] + 0.3 * eps` (~133 rows/group), where task 548's LR identification gate (`LR_CUT_2 = 13.8155`) never triggers, leaving `variance_y`, `std_resid_y`, `forecast_y`, and `coef` **100% null** (note the misplaced `hawkes` comment at lines 403–404 above `garch`); (2) `CLOCKED` (lines 618–622) omits `garch` and `hawkes` even though both take `**CLOCK` in `WORKLOAD`, skipping `step_back_*` and `session_*` release-diff coverage; (3) `WORKLOAD` and `PATHS` omit `drift_kind` (5 new detectors), `kalman(covariance_form="square_root")`, `hmm` factor form (`d >= 13`) / `prior_rows`, `lasso` split sweep (`k >= 40`), `glm` canonical links / `binomial`, `expectile` windows, `hawkes` windows / `kernel_half_lives`, and `*_span` cadences.
- **Fix:** Add simulated GARCH return columns (as in `test_budgets_memory.py:90-100`), assert non-null `variance_y`/`forecast_y`, add `"garch"` and `"hawkes"` to `CLOCKED`, fix the comment at line 403, and extend `WORKLOAD` / `PATHS` for post-v2.0.0 code paths.

#### `TEST-2` — `state_portability.rs` and `zero_weight.rs` run `garch` on degenerate linear data where it never starts (100% nulls)
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-polars/tests/state_portability.rs:92-95, 240-241, 299-303`; `crates/rheon-polars/tests/zero_weight.rs:384-395`.
- **Details:** Both suites feed linear `y = 2a - b + 0.1*r` (400 / 420 rows) to `garch` (`vol="gjr", dist="t"` in `state_portability.rs`, `start_rows=50` and `start_span=@20` in `zero_weight.rs`). Under task 548's LR gate (`LR_CUT_3 = 16.266` / `LR_CUT_2 = 13.8155`), `garch` never starts (`started = false` at `SPLIT = 200` and through row 400), emitting 100% nulls. Consequently, cross-OS state portability (`release.yml`) never serializes a started GARCH state (`theta`, Hessian `H`, lags, sensitivities, `nu`, `forecast`), and the bank-level zero-weight sweep never exercises an active GARCH across all four clock types or `start_span`.
- **Fix:** Add a simulated GJR-GARCH return column (`yg`) to both suites, point the `garch` specs at `yg`, and assert finite non-null `variance_yg` before and after `SPLIT` / across the sweep.

#### `TEST-3` — `glm` oracle suite misses `binomial` statistical/refit checks and has zero numerical oracle tests for canonical links (`gamma(inverse)`, `tweedie(power)`)
- **Location:** `tests/test_second_opinion_glm.py:130-260`; `tests/test_glm.py:152-165`.
- **Details:** `test_glm.py:152-165` only asserts `pred[100:] > 0` for `gamma(link="inverse")` and `tweedie(link="power")`, and neither link appears in `test_second_opinion_glm.py`. `binomial` is omitted from the 5-coef 10k, 20-coef 100k, decay, and refit-vs-`glum` tests. Non-unit `weight`, `var_power != 1.5`, and binding `step_cap` are also absent from the oracle suite.
- **Fix:** Add `binomial`, `gamma(link="inverse")`, `tweedie(link="power")`, a second `var_power`, and non-unit `weight` to `test_second_opinion_glm.py` against `glum`/`statsmodels`, and cover all four families in the refit parity test.

#### `TEST-4` — `expectile` oracle suite never tests default `ridge = 1e-6` or post-refit continuation under decay
- **Location:** `tests/test_second_opinion_expectile.py:88`; `tests/test_expectile.py:43, 72`.
- **Details:** The shared `spec(...)` helper in `test_second_opinion_expectile.py:88` (and longhand IRLS checks in `test_expectile.py:43, 72`) hardcodes `ridge = 0.0` on every oracle test. Default `ridge = 1e-6` (`G + ridge * (W / S) * D` sparing the intercept, plus the centered fallback) and decayed continuation after `refit_rows`/`refit_span` have zero oracle coverage.
- **Fix:** Add penalized oracle tests (`ridge = 1e-6` and `ridge = 1.0`, confirming unpenalized intercept and `W/S` scaling) and a decayed post-refit continuation oracle test.

#### `TEST-5` — `lasso` post-v2.0.0 split sweep (`SPLIT_MIN_K = 40`) has zero Python, Polars, or `sklearn` oracle tests
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/src/lasso/split.rs:46`; `tests/test_lasso.py` (max `k = 3`); `tests/test_second_opinion_gram.py` (max `k = 8`); `crates/rheon-core/tests/golden.rs:339` (`k = 2`); `scripts/release_probe.py` (`k = 3`).
- **Details:** Across the entire Python suite, core goldens, and release probe, the maximum feature count ever passed to `lasso` is `k = 8`, so `cd_sweep_split` (`k >= 40`) is exercised only by Rust unit tests inside `lasso/split.rs` — never against `sklearn.linear_model.enet_path`/KKT residuals or through Polars chunk/save/window invariance.
- **Fix:** Add a `k = 50` sparse + correlated test against `sklearn` in `test_second_opinion_gram.py` and a `k >= 40` chunk/save/window test in `test_lasso.py`.

#### `DOC-1` — Sixteen docstrings and guide passages directly contradict Zero-Weight Hard Rule 9
- **Location:** `python/rheon/spec.py:89-90, 183, 225-227, 530-531`; `python/rheon/_spec.py:1805-1808, 1875` (`ewridge`), `2758, 2765-2766` (`kalman`), `3205-3208` (`ftrl`), `3596-3609` (`ew_cov`), `5059-5060` (`deco`); `_spec_cluster.py:609` (`micro`); `_spec_gradient.py:530-531` (`sgd`); `_spec_robust.py:443` (`huber`); `polars/_bank.py:1965-1967`; `polars/stream.py:312-313`; `docs/DIAGNOSTICS.md:1229-1230`; `docs/MARGINAL-LAGS-AND-BINS.md:441` *(expanded from 4 to 16 locations in Pass 2)*.
- **Details:** All 16 passages state pre-task-539 behavior — claiming a `weight = 0` row *"advances the clock"*, enters `embargo`/`max_held_rows`, counts toward `max_rows_between_coefs`, adds Kalman process noise `Q`, or ages `sigma` and moment matrices. Post-task-539 `weightless.rs` scores `w = 0` rows without advancing any clock, entering held buffers, or aging any model or diagnostic.
- **Fix:** Sweep and update all 16 locations (plus `ops.py:530`, where `ewm_sum` claims `min_samples < 1` raises `ValueError` despite defaulting to `0`) to state Hard Rule 9 accurately.

#### `DOC-2` — `CHANGELOG.md` has duplicate `### Changed` headings and two truncated mid-sentence copy-paste fragments in `[Unreleased]`
- **Location:** `CHANGELOG.md:32, 375` (two `### Changed` subsections under `## [Unreleased]`); `CHANGELOG.md:1049-1061` (truncated duplicate of lines 1033–1048); `CHANGELOG.md:1072-1076` (truncated duplicate of lines 1062–1071).
- **Fix:** Delete both truncated duplicate fragments and merge the two `### Changed` sections under `## [Unreleased]`.

#### `DOC-3` — Stale model counts, summary tables, `window_size` / Poisson exclusivity, and `weight-rows.md` omissions across guides and docstrings
- **Location:** `docs/DIAGNOSTICS.md:322-336, 357, 875, 981, 1239`; `docs/MODELS.md:99, 219-220, 460-486, 1447-1448`; `docs/FEATURES.md:203`; `docs/TESTING.md:55, 92, 112`; `docs/how-to/weight-rows.md:85-99`; `docs/how-to/{detect-a-break, check-feature-health, check-the-scale, find-what-the-model-misses, measure-heavy-tails, put-an-interval-on-a-prediction}.md`; `python/rheon/_spec_gradient.py:50` *(expanded in Pass 2)*.
- **Details:**
  1. `DIAGNOSTICS.md` and 6 `docs/how-to/*.md` pages say *"ten linear models"* / *"nine with features"* (now **12** and **11**); `MODELS.md:460-486` says *"Eleven models ... Ten regress it on features"* and omits `glm`/`expectile` from the `ridge`, `window_size`, `standardize`, and `half_life` summary bullets.
  2. `MODELS.md:99, 219-220` and `FEATURES.md:203` claim only 5 models take `window_size` (omitting `expectile` and `hawkes`).
  3. `MODELS.md:1447-1448` and `_spec_gradient.py:50` claim `sgd` is the only model for count targets (`loss="poisson"`), contradicting `glm(family="poisson")` and `hawkes`.
  4. `docs/how-to/weight-rows.md:85-99` (*"Ten models read a weight differently"*) omits `hawkes` (any `w > 0` learns unscaled marks `m_j`), `glm` (`G` on sum scale against fixed `ridge`), and `hmm` `prior_rows`.
- **Fix:** Update all counts, summary bullets, exclusivity claims, and `weight-rows.md` for all 26 models.

#### `CI-3` — Un-tolerated `Timeout` mutants on `changed` pass exit `0` (green CI!), silently violating `PLAN.md` task 546(1)
- **Location:** `scripts/mutants_report.py:155-186, 244-246`; `.github/workflows/mutants.yml:298, 324-326`.
- **Mechanism:** `mutants.yml:298` maps `cargo mutants` exit code `3` (timeout) to `exit 0` (`0|2|3) exit 0 ;;`) so `mutants_report.py --fail-on-missed` decides pass/fail. Yet `mutants_report.py:178` computes `missed = sum(len(v) for v in survivors.values())` — **ignoring `len(hung)`** (untolerated `Timeout`s not in `mutants_tolerated.toml` `kind = "loop"`) — and exits `0`! Any infinite-loop or 10× blowup mutant on changed lines passes CI green.
- **Fix:** Add `len(hung)` into `missed`, include `kept["loop"]` in `n_kept` / report untolerated `len(hung)` separately from total `counts["Timeout"]`, and add a regression test in `tests/test_mutants_report.py`.

#### `CI-4` — `scripts/vm_checks.py` builds x86-64 `default` and `aligned` wheels without `X86_FEATURES`, benchmarking SSE2 (`rheon-compat`) instead of shipped `rheon`
- **Location:** `scripts/vm_checks.py:35-38, 62-67`; `tests/test_vm_checks.py:244-273` (vs `.github/workflows/release.yml:36-42, 314`).
- **Mechanism:** Task 476 moved shipped x86-64 `rheon` wheels to `X86_FEATURES` (`+avx2,+fma,...`), keeping baseline SSE2 only in `rheon-compat`. `vm_checks.py` still sets `LAYOUTS = {"default": "", ...}`, so x86-64 `vm-checks.yml` runs (and upcoming task 545 benchmarks) time unvectorized SSE2 code instead of the AVX2+FMA wheel users install.
- **Fix:** Prepend `release.yml`'s `X86_FEATURES` on `x86_64` in `vm_checks.py` and assert equality with `release.yml` in `test_vm_checks.py`.

#### `TEST-10` — Inverted meta-test in `model_contract.rs` leaves 6 models and 4 post-v2.0.0 modes without `*_recovers_from_bounded_extremes` tests, and `proptest!` omits `garch` & `expectile(window_clock)`
- **Location:** `crates/rheon-core/tests/model_contract.rs:5067-5087, 6140-6378`.
- **Details:** (1) `every_model_with_a_recovery_test_has_a_predict_parity_test` checks `recovery => predict_parity` instead of `ALL_MODELS == recovery_tests`, leaving `garch`, `glm`, `expectile`, `deco`, `seqtest`, `audit`, `kalman(SquareRoot)`, `hmm(d >= 13)`, and windowed `hawkes` with **zero bounded-extremes (`<= 1e100`) recovery tests** (explaining how `GARCH-4`, `GLM-5`, `EXP-5`, `KAL-2/3`, and `HMM-1` escaped); (2) `proptest!` omits `garch`, `expectile` `window_clock` (where `EXP-1`/`EXP-4` live), `kalman(SquareRoot)`, `hmm(d >= 13)`, and `glm` non-Poisson/canonical links/`refit_clock`.
- **Fix:** Enforce `ALL_MODELS == recovery_tests == predict_parity_tests` and add all missing models and post-v2.0.0 modes to both `*_recovers_from_bounded_extremes` and `proptest!`.

---

### Medium (Tests, Oracles, CI & Docs)

- **`TEST-6`** (`tests/test_second_opinion_hmm.py`): Every third-party oracle test uses `d = 2`; `hmm` Cholesky factor form (`d >= 13`) and custom `prior_rows != d` have zero `scipy`/`hmmlearn` oracle tests end-to-end through Python/Polars (`test_hmm.py:155-204` only checks chunk/save invariance).

- **`TEST-7`** (`crates/rheon-polars/tests/spec_inf.rs:27-180`): Only `hmm.prior_rows` was added after v2.0.0; TOML `inf`/`-inf`/`nan` validation tests are missing for all `garch`, `glm`, `expectile`, `hawkes`, `bocpd` clock-hazard, `*_span`, `*_every`, and `drift_options` parameters (which would have caught `SPEC-4`).

- **`TEST-9`** (`tests/test_garch.py:67-90, 133-188, 316-319`, `test_second_opinion_garch.py:555-625`, `test_second_opinion_drift_kinds.py:118-153`, `test_hawkes.py:594-595`): (1) `test_garch.py` chunk/save/weight-scale tests omit non-null assertions (`equal_nan=True` passes on all-null outputs); (2) recursion test omits `egarch`, `variance_targeting`, `garch(dist="t")` (leaving dead code at line 84), and `skewt`, and line 316 is tautological at `t == 1999`; (3) `test_second_opinion_garch.py` omits FIGARCH from `lr_cases` and has no weighted-likelihood oracle; (4) `test_second_opinion_drift_kinds.py` omits ADWIN `restart=False`/`max_width` and default EDDM parameters; (5) `test_hawkes.py:594` silently skips `coef` checks for `"2999500us"`.

- **`DOC-4`** (`docs/MODELS.md:309`, `docs/GLOSSARY.md:115, 180`, `docs/HIGHLIGHTS.md:307`, `docs/explanation/state-and-resuming.md:23`, 17 `docs/how-to/*.md` files, `scripts/outputs_doc.py` → `docs/OUTPUTS.md`): 20+ active user docs still instruct callers to use the removed v1.x `.online` namespace (`online.fit_predict`, `online.unnest`) instead of `.rheon`, and `outputs_doc.py` / `OUTPUTS.md` still use `from rheon import polars as po` / `po.spec.*` instead of `rp`.

- **`DOC-5`** (`scripts/outputs_doc.py:116, 204, 267, 393-438, 493`, `docs/OUTPUTS.md:104` vs `136`): `OUTPUTS.md:104` says `` `withheld_reason`'s six `` right above line 136 listing all **seven** reasons (including `not_identified`), and omits `garch`'s `forecast_<t>` (`horizon > 1`) completely because `OPTIONAL` never registers it.

- **`DOC-6`** (`docs/EXTENDING.md:153, 186, 360, 493-518, 849-862`, `python/rheon/spec.py:26-27, 698`, `docs/MODELS.md:18-19, 160`, `docs/TESTING.md:141-200, 278-285`, `docs/DIAGNOSTICS.md:412, 659-667, 1337-1361`, `docs/how-to/choose-a-model.md:130`, `CHANGELOG.md:889`, `tests/test_glm.py:206`, `glm.rs:3`, `expectile.rs:2`, `tests/test_second_opinion_{glm,expectile}.py:2` — Pass-2 deduplicated against task 537): Stale 22-model checklists in `EXTENDING.md` *(excluding `max_state_bytes`, which is already tracked in task 537)*, `spec.py:26-27` omitting `garch`/`hawkes` from featureless models, `spec.py:698` (`EMIT_DRIFT` naming only Page-Hinkley), `"Four models"` above a 5-row table in `choose-a-model.md:130`, missing `drift_options` reference and ADWIN/DDM/EDDM/KSWIN citations in `DIAGNOSTICS.md`, stale oracle tables in `TESTING.md`, stale `"19 kinds"` in `CHANGELOG.md:889`, stale `"refit_rows... has no clock form"` docstring in `test_glm.py:206`, and broken references to nonexistent `docs/records/STUDY-500-GLM.md` and `docs/records/STUDY-501-509.md`.

- **`TEST-11`** (`tests/test_semantics_all_models.py`, `tests/test_properties.py:682-738, 977-1071, 1115-1118`, `tests/test_properties_temporal.py:546-552`): `MINIMAL["glm"]` and `MINIMAL["expectile"]` default to `refit_rows = 210` while both universal suites cap streams at `n <= 120`, so neither suite ever triggers `glm`/`expectile` refits or post-refit continuation; `REFIT_MODELS` omits `quantile`, `glm`, `expectile`, and all five `*_refit_span` modes; `SWITCHES` omits all post-`v2.0.0` switches; and `garch` is 100% null in `TestEveryOtherKind` and `test_properties_temporal.py`.

- **`TEST-12`** (`tests/test_old_names.py:66, 133-144, 217-229, 249`): Root cause of `DOC-4` escaping CI: (1) `.online`'s lookbehind (`L221`) requires a preceding identifier/bracket, missing bare `` `online.fit_predict` `` / `` `.online` `` across 20+ docs; (2) `po.` rule `trees` (`L249`) omits `scripts/*` (`outputs_doc.py`, `leakcheck.sh`) and `GUIDES` (`OUTPUTS.md`, `ARROW-SOURCES.md`, `CLUSTERING.md`, `HUBER.md`, `MARGINAL-LAGS-AND-BINS.md`); (3) 9 files remain exempted in `NOT_YET` (`L133-144`) despite task 395 being ticked `[x]`.

- **`CI-5`** (`crates/rheon-polars/tests/state_fixtures.rs`, `crates/rheon-cli/tests/{run,surface}.rs`, `scripts/leakcheck.sh`, `crates/rheon-bench/{src/scaling.rs, tests/allocations.rs, tests/product_memory.rs}`): (1) Bank-level `rheon-polars/tests/state_fixtures.rs` has zero frozen fixtures for `garch`, `hawkes`, or `kalman(square_root)` (task 548 only added `garch` to `rheon-core`); (2) `rheon-cli` has zero tests for post-2.0 models or envelope wrong-kind state refusals; (3) `leakcheck.sh` tests only `ewridge` (using `po.*`) and misses `merge`/`checkpoint`/`with_windows`; (4) `BENCH_CELLS`, `allocations.rs`, and `product_memory.rs` omit `glm`, `expectile`, `hawkes`, and `garch`.

## 6. Verified Sound (Re-Derived & Checked Across Both Passes)

Beyond the findings above, both audit passes independently re-derived and verified the following core subsystems, mathematical identities, SIMD kernels, and streaming invariants at `c4e7c7d2`:

- **GARCH recursions, scores, forecasts, and online RPEM (`rheon-core/src/garch/`):** All four recursions (GARCH, GJR with `alpha + gamma >= 0`, EGARCH, FIGARCH) and exact mathematical admissibility sets (`figarch_set` guaranteeing `beta < 1` and `lambda_j >= 0` for all `j >= 1`, modulo Dykstra projection tolerances in `GARCH-12`); FIGARCH batch-vs-streaming parity (`<= 3.5e-15`) and multi-step forecasts; `ln_gamma`/`digamma` and Hansen (1994) skewed-t / Student-t constants and analytical scores (`<= 1.5e-7` vs central FD across `nu in [2.05, 300]`, `lam in [-0.99, 0.99]`, continuous across the kink `z = -a/b`); variance-targeting Jacobian `J`, Huber-clipped score calibration (`-E[d psi / d ln s] = m`), Schur-complement metric projection (return-scale invariant to `7.3e-16`), Kish weight scaling (`sum(w)/sum(w^2)`), LR cutoffs, `predict_with` vs `step_into` bit parity, and documented struct byte sizes.
- **GLM families & Expectile core algebra (`family.rs`, `glm.rs`, `expectile.rs`, `clock_span.rs`):** All six `(family, link)` pairs in `family.rs` (`V(mu)`, `g`, `g^-1`, `g'`, `W`, `z`, unit deviance `d(y, mu)`) match finite differences to `<= 3e-10`; no-decay GLM recursion matches penalized batch IRLS with consistent `0.5` deviance/ridge scaling, unshrunk Levenberg–Marquardt steady state under decay, and direction-preserving `step_cap`; `Expectile`'s one-pass recursion, intercept-sparing ridge `r(W/S)D`, exact Schur-complement algebra in `centred_solve`, convex C¹ WLS descent direction, and `cmp_int_f64` across full `i128` range.
- **Hawkes continuous-time Gram & drift mechanics (`hawkes.rs`, `drift/`):** Exact integration-by-parts continuous-time Gram recursion across all 7 `gamma` vs `beta` regimes (`rel err <= 1.6e-15` vs independent piecewise-analytic integrator, including simultaneous multi-node marks and `d = 0` ties); two-block window forward/backward subtraction; diagonal-congruence-invariant Cholesky pivot floor (`solve.rs:1349`); ADWIN Chan parallel variance merge and `u128`-safe level bound; DDM/EDDM core recursions matching Gama/Baena-García/River; KSWIN SplitMix64 constants, unbiased Lemire `below_by` rejection sampling, Floyd sampling without replacement, and exact Gnedenko–Korolyuk DP p-values.
- **HMM factor form, BOCPD NIW/clock hazards, Carlson square-root Kalman, Lasso, & SIMD Cholesky (`hmm/`, `bocpd/`, `kalman/`, `lasso/`, `solve.rs`, `gramfit/`):** `solve.rs` 13–64 register-blocked Cholesky `pulp` AVX2 lanes (`mul_f64s` + `sub_f64s` without FMA) match scalar `llt_loop` and `faer` **to the bit**; `solve/condition.rs` Hager–Higham 1-norm estimator; `gramfit/lars.rs` Murty least-index anti-cycling LCP and `trisolve.rs`/`colstats.rs` thread-count bit-determinism; HMM Cholesky factor form `M = sigma L L'` (`d >= 13`), Givens rank-1 updates, exponent-verified `lift_sigma`, `shift_prior`/`shift_prior_shaped`, and schema `< 66` and `68/69 -> 70` state migrations; BOCPD Murphy (2007) NIW Student-t predictive DoF/scale, task 547 `hazard` vs `hazard_rows` vs `hazard_col`, schema `< 72` migration, and bit-exact `HeldPrior`/`HeldZ` caches; Carlson (1973) square-root measurement update and Durbin–Koopman (§5.2) exact diffuse-intercept step (`max |C C' - P_joseph| = 1.776e-15`); Lasso `cd_sweep_split` (`SPLIT_MIN_K = 40`) bit-identical association order to `cd_sweep`.
- **Core regressors, SIMD lanes, & solvers (`ewridge/`, `rls.rs`, `robust/`, `sgd.rs`, `pa.rs`, `ftrl.rs`, `holt.rs`):** `ewridge/unread.rs` Higham `gamma_n` solve-skipping bound (`MARGIN = 2^-16`), `FirstPeriod` scheduling, and `session_shrink` cache invalidation; `ewridge` blocked Gram `gram_block_rows` flush parity and `OwnRows` window subtraction; QR square-root `rls.rs` overflow-safe `hypot` Givens rotations; `HeldMedian` Split `(m, k)` treap rotations (`RESCALE_BITS = 512`); all `pulp` AVX2 `x86_v3()` kernels in `sgd.rs`, `ewdiag.rs`, `huber_refit.rs`, and `ewcov/held.rs` verified bit-identical to scalar paths.
- **Covariance, realized kernels, regimes, clustering, & audit (`ewcov.rs`, `rcov.rs`, `cluster/`, `corrchange.rs`, `boundary.rs`, `seqtest.rs`, `audit.rs`, `margbins.rs`):** `ewcov` thread-local `MAHAL_WORK` reentrancy safety and parallel block merge; `rcov` Barndorff-Nielsen–Hansen–Lunde–Shephard (2008, 2011) Parzen realized kernel (`H*`) and Christensen–Kinnebrock–Podolskij (2010) pre-averaging; `margbins.rs` dynamic per-target `scale` preventing `m2` overflow up to `|y| = 1e100, w = 1e100` (live-verified); `corrchange` van Herk / Gil-Werman block-merged window, Wied–Galeano Bartlett LRV, and Scharfetter–Gummel Crank–Nicolson Fokker–Planck PDE boundary CDF (M-matrix positivity verified down to `gamma = 1e-12`); `seqtest` Krichevsky–Trofimov / normal-mixture e-processes; `audit.rs` $q\arcsin$ merging t-digest.
- **Clocks, diagnostics, & core windows (`clock.rs`, `gaps.rs`, `since.rs`, `window.rs`, `breaks.rs`, `influence.rs`):** `Decay::factor` (`exp2` determinism), `Stamp` `i128` nanosecond arithmetic; `window.rs` & `window/flat.rs` word-level packing and `keep_alternate` thinning; `breaks.rs` `cusum_null*` and `cusum_sq_null_at` (verified against 64-point 1D/2D Gauss–Legendre quadrature to `<= 1e-15`) and `TwinFit::lower_variance` 4×4 register-blocked `part_mul`.
- **Merge, state envelope, checksum, bank, windows, PyO3, Python, & user-doc equations:** N-way heap merge `(restarts, clock, input)` ordering, `float_key`/`float_of` bit-flip bijection, and exotic Polars dtype unification; MessagePack `Cursor` overflow-safe header parsing and `xxh3_64` `Trailer::Streamed`/`Patched` byte parity; Arrow sliced-array validity/offset handling, `Inline` TLS unwind safety, deterministic `(row, spec_idx)` refusal reduction, and `SIGINT`/`SIGTERM` temp-file cleanup; `windows/plain.rs` (`plain_pass`) bit-for-bit parity with `Windows::push`, categorical-to-`String` group/session hashing (`windows/keys.rs`), and v9–v12 window state loaders; Polars 2.1.0 `_formula.py` expression serializer and `register_io_source` pushdowns; `eval.py` HAC/HLN Diebold–Mariano, Clark–West, and Murphy estimators; and mathematical update equations across `MODELS.md`, `DIAGNOSTICS.md`, `WINDOWS.md`, `REGIMES.md`, `STATE-WORKFLOW.md`, and `README.md`.
